==== BEGIN Adnan Halilović | Adnan Halilović_18894_assignsubmission_file | projektivna_transformacija.m ====
% 2. Projektivna transformacija
clear; close all; clc;

% Preberi sivinsko sliko (že brez radialne distorzije)
I = imread('Image_rect1.tif');  % tukaj vstavi svojo undistorted sliko
if size(I,3) == 3
    I = rgb2gray(I);
end

% Kliknem 4 vogale šahovnice (v smeri urinega kazalca)
figure; imshow(I); title('Klikni 4 vogale šahovnice (v smeri urinega kazalca)');
[x, y] = ginput(4);
hold on; plot(x, y, 'ro-', 'LineWidth', 2);
%Scatter za risanje pikic
% Določi idealne (nepopačene) točke
X = [0 640 640 0];
Y = [0 0 480 480];


% Izračun projektivne transformacije (fitgeotrans)
tform = fitgeotrans([x y], [X' Y'], 'projective');

% Korekcija slike (imwarp)
J = imwarp(I, tform, 'OutputView', imref2d(size(I)));

% Rezultati
figure;
subplot(1,2,1); imshow(I); title('Izvirna slika');
subplot(1,2,2); imshow(J); title('Korigirana slika');


% Velikost mreže
numCols = 12; % število stolpcev
numRows = 11; % število vrstic
stepX = 640 / numCols;
stepY = 480 / numRows;

[Xg, Yg] = meshgrid(0:stepX:640, 0:stepY:480);
gridPoints = [Xg(:), Yg(:)];

% Inverzna transformacija – iz idealnega koordinatnega sistema v originalno sliko
tform_inv = invert(tform);
[origX, origY] = transformPointsForward(tform_inv, gridPoints(:,1), gridPoints(:,2));

% Prikaži mrežo na originalni sliki
figure; imshow(I); title('Izvirna slika z izrisano mrežo (inverzna transformacija)');
hold on;
plot(origX, origY, 'g.', 'MarkerSize', 10);

% Po želji izrišeš povezave (linije med točkami)
for r = 1:size(Yg,1)
    idx = (r-1)*(numCols+1)+(1:(numCols+1));
    plot(origX(idx), origY(idx), 'y-');
end
for c = 1:size(Xg,2)
    idx = c:(numCols+1):numel(Xg);
    plot(origX(idx), origY(idx), 'y-');
end

==== BEGIN Adnan Halilović | Adnan Halilović_18894_assignsubmission_file | Radialna_distorzija.m ====


function radialna_distorzija(ime_slike)

    if nargin < 1
        ime_slike = 'Image15.tif'; % privzeta slika iz naloge
    end

    % --- 1. Branje slike ---
    I = imread(ime_slike);
    figure, imshow(I);
    title({'Klikni točke na dveh vertikalnih linijah šahovnice', ...
           '(najprej vse točke prve linije, nato vse točke druge)'});

    % --- 2. Označevanje točk ---
    disp('Klikni točke na PRVI liniji, nato pritisni Enter');
    [x1, y1] = ginput; % prva linija
    disp('Klikni točke na DRUGI liniji, nato pritisni Enter');
    [x2, y2] = ginput; % druga linija

    lin1 = [x1'; y1'];
    lin2 = [x2'; y2'];
    % Hardcode pikic
    % --- 3. Definicija funkcije napake za optimizacijo ---
    err_fun = @(f) napaka_distorzije(f, lin1, lin2, size(I));

    % --- 4. Optimizacija parametra f (z uporabo nelinearne metode) ---
    f0 = 500; % začetna ocena
    f_opt = fminsearch(err_fun, f0);
    fprintf('Optimalni parameter f = %.2f\n', f_opt);

    % --- 5. Korekcija slike z najboljšim f ---
    [Xp1, Yp1] = popravi_tocke(lin1, f_opt, size(I));
    [Xp2, Yp2] = popravi_tocke(lin2, f_opt, size(I));

    % --- 6. Prikaz originalnih in korigiranih točk ---
    figure, imshow(I); hold on;
    plot(lin1(1,:), lin1(2,:), 'ro', 'MarkerFaceColor', 'r');
    plot(lin2(1,:), lin2(2,:), 'bo', 'MarkerFaceColor', 'b');
    plot(Xp1, Yp1, 'go', 'MarkerFaceColor', 'g');
    plot(Xp2, Yp2, 'co', 'MarkerFaceColor', 'c');

    % --- 7. Izračun in prikaz premic (pred in po korekciji) ---
    C1 = fitline(lin1);
    C2 = fitline(lin2);
    C1_corr = fitline([Xp1; Yp1]);
    C2_corr = fitline([Xp2; Yp2]);

    x = 1:size(I,2);
    y1 = -(C1(1)*x + C1(3)) / C1(2);
    y2 = -(C2(1)*x + C2(3)) / C2(2);
    y1_corr = -(C1_corr(1)*x + C1_corr(3)) / C1_corr(2);
    y2_corr = -(C2_corr(1)*x + C2_corr(3)) / C2_corr(2);

    plot(x, y1, 'r-', 'LineWidth', 1.2);
    plot(x, y2, 'b-', 'LineWidth', 1.2);
    plot(x, y1_corr, 'g--', 'LineWidth', 1.5);
    plot(x, y2_corr, 'c--', 'LineWidth', 1.5);

    legend('Linija 1 - original', 'Linija 2 - original', ...
           'Linija 1 - korigirano', 'Linija 2 - korigirano', ...
           'Location', 'southoutside');
    title(['Korigirane točke (f = ', num2str(f_opt, '%.2f'), ')']);
    hold off;

    % --- 8. Analiza napake glede na f ---
    f_values = 400:20:800;
    errors = zeros(size(f_values));

    for i = 1:length(f_values)
        errors(i) = napaka_distorzije(f_values(i), lin1, lin2, size(I));
    end

    figure;
    plot(f_values, errors, 'b-o', 'LineWidth', 1.5);
    xlabel('Parameter f');
    ylabel('Vsota kvadratov razdalj');
    title('Odvisnost napake od parametra f');
    grid on;
end

% -------------------------------------------------------------------------
function E = napaka_distorzije(f, lin1, lin2, img_size)
    % Izračuna povprečno vsoto kvadratov razdalj do idealne premice
    [Xp1, Yp1] = popravi_tocke(lin1, f, img_size);
    [Xp2, Yp2] = popravi_tocke(lin2, f, img_size);

    [~, d1] = fitline([Xp1; Yp1]);
    [~, d2] = fitline([Xp2; Yp2]);

    % Povprečje napak obeh linij
    E = (sum(d1.^2) + sum(d2.^2)) / (length(d1) + length(d2));
end

% -------------------------------------------------------------------------
function [Xp, Yp] = popravi_tocke(XY, f, img_size)
    % Uporabi model iz enačbe (2) za popravek radialne distorzije
    cx = img_size(2) / 2; % središče slike (x)
    cy = img_size(1) / 2; % središče slike (y)

    X = XY(1,:) - cx;
    Y = XY(2,:) - cy;
    r = sqrt(X.^2 + Y.^2);

    % Inverzni model radialne distorzije
    rp = -(f/2) .* ((exp(-2*r./f) - 1) ./ exp(-r./f));

    % Pretvorba nazaj v slikovne koordinate
    Xp = rp .* X ./ (r + eps) + cx;
    Yp = rp .* Y ./ (r + eps) + cy;
end
==== END Adnan Halilović | Adnan Halilović_18894_assignsubmission_file | Radialna_distorzija.m ====


==== BEGIN Aleksander Rijavec | Aleksander Rijavec_18902_assignsubmission_file | fitline.m ====
%SLIKE 2 3 6 8 13
% NAPAKA od -1 do 1

% FITLINE - Least squares fit of a line to a set of points
%
% Usage:   [C, dist] = fitline(XY)
%
% Where:   XY  - 2xNpts array of xy coordinates to fit line to data of
%                the form 
%                [x1 x2 x3 ... xN
%                 y1 y2 y3 ... yN]
%                
%                XY can also be a 3xNpts array of homogeneous coordinates.
%
% Returns: C    - 3x1 array of line coefficients in the form
%                 c(1)*X + c(2)*Y + c(3) = 0
%          dist - Array of distances from the fitted line to the supplied
%                 data points.  Note that dist is only calculated if the
%                 function is called with two output arguments.
%
% The magnitude of C is scaled so that line equation corresponds to
%   sin(theta)*X + (-cos(theta))*Y + rho = 0
% where theta is the angle between the line and the x axis and rho is the
% perpendicular distance from the origin to the line.  Rescaling the
% coefficients in this manner allows the perpendicular distance from any
% point (x,y) to the line to be simply calculated as
%   r = abs(c(1)*X + c(2)*Y + c(3))
%
% If you want to convert this line representation to the classical form 
%     Y = a*X + b
% use
%  a = -c(1)/c(2)
%  b = -c(3)/c(2)
%
% Note, however, that this assumes c(2) is not zero

% Copyright (c) 2003-2008 Peter Kovesi
% School of Computer Science & Software Engineering
% The University of Western Australia
% http://www.csse.uwa.edu.au/
% 
% Permission is hereby granted, free of charge, to any person obtaining a copy
% of this software and associated documentation files (the "Software"), to deal
% in the Software without restriction, subject to the following conditions:
% 
% The above copyright notice and this permission notice shall be included in 
% all copies or substantial portions of the Software.
%
% The Software is provided "as is", without warranty of any kind.

% June      2003 - Original version
% September 2004 - Rescaling to allow simple distance calculation.
% November  2008 - Normalising of coordinates added to condition the
%                  solution.
% December  2016 - Simplified rescaling of coefficients to allow line
%                  distances to be calculated


function [C, dist] = fitline(XY)
  
  [rows,npts] = size(XY);    

  if npts < 2
      error('Too few points to fit line');
  end  
  
  if rows ==2    % Add homogeneous scale coordinate of 1 
      XY = [XY; ones(1,npts)];
  end

  if npts == 2    % Pad XY with a third column of zeros
    XY = [XY zeros(3,1)]; 
  end
  
  % Normalise points so that centroid is at origin and mean distance from
  % origin is sqrt(2).  This conditions the equations. 
  [XYn, T] = normalise2dpts(XY);
  
  % Set up constraint equations of the form  XYn'*C = 0,
  % where C is a column vector of the line coefficients
  % in the form   c(1)*X + c(2)*Y + c(3) = 0.

  [u d v] = svd(XYn',0);   % Singular value decomposition.
  C = v(:,3);              % Solution is last column of v.

  % Denormalise the solution
  C = T'*C;
  
  % Rescale coefficients so that line equation corresponds to
  %   sin(theta)*X + (-cos(theta))*Y + rho = 0
  % so that the perpendicular distance from any point (x,y) to the line
  % to be simply calculated as 
  %   r = abs(c(1)*X + c(2)*Y + c(3))
  C = C / sqrt(C(1)^2 + C(2)^2);
  
  % If requested, calculate the distances from the fitted line to
  % the supplied data points 
  if nargout==2   
      dist = abs(C(1)*XY(1,:) + C(2)*XY(2,:) + C(3));
  end
==== END Aleksander Rijavec | Aleksander Rijavec_18902_assignsubmission_file | fitline.m ====

imshow(rgb_slika);

    optimizirani_center_r = (-f / 2) .* ((exp(-2 .* r_center ./ f) - 1) ./ exp(-r_center ./ f));

    % polarni v krtezicni
    [x_rub_opt, y_rub_opt] = pol2cart(theta_rub, optimizirani_rob_r);
    [x_srediste_opt, y_srediste_opt] = pol2cart(theta_center, optimizirani_center_r);

    rub_opt_tocke = [x_rub_opt; y_rub_opt];
    srediste_opt_tocke = [x_srediste_opt; y_srediste_opt];

    % Izračun grešaka za optimizirane točke
    [~, greska_rub] = fitline(rub_opt_tocke);
    [~, greska_srediste] = fitline(srediste_opt_tocke);
    
    suma_odstupanja_rub = [suma_odstupanja_rub, sqrt(sum(greska_rub.^2))];
    suma_odstupanja_srediste = [suma_odstupanja_srediste, sqrt(sum(greska_srediste.^2))];
end

title('Skupna napaka v odvisnosti od f');
xlabel('f');
ylabel('Skupna vsota kvadratov napak');

==== BEGIN Aleksander Rijavec | Aleksander Rijavec_18902_assignsubmission_file | Naloga4.m ====
slika = imread('Image15.tif');
rgb_slika = repmat(slika, 1, 1, 3);
%figure;
%imshow(rgb_slika);
%title('Kliknite 12 točk: prvih 6 na robu, naslednjih 6 v središču');

% Izberi 12 točk na sliki
%[x_koordinate, y_koordinate] = ginput(12);
x_koordinate = [43.8898, 42.0124, 40.7608, 38.8833, 43.2640, 43.8898,274.8155, 271.6864, 266.6799, 259.1701, 254.1636, 251.6604]';
y_koordinate = [67.1493, 144.7503, 221.0997, 257.3970, 326.2366, 392.5730,82.1688, 120.3435, 194.1897, 266.1584, 333.7464, 396.3279]';
[visina, sirina, ~] = size(slika);


% Razdeli točke na robne in sredinske
tocke_rob = [(sirina / 2 - x_koordinate(1:6))'; (visina / 2 - y_koordinate(1:6))'];
tocke_center = [(sirina / 2 - x_koordinate(7:12))'; (visina / 2 - y_koordinate(7:12))'];

% Pretvori točke iz kartezičnih v polarne koordinate
[theta_rub, r_rub] = cart2pol(tocke_rob(1,:), tocke_rob(2,:));
[theta_center, r_center] = cart2pol(tocke_center(1,:), tocke_center(2,:));
% Začetni izračun napak brez optimizacije
[koef_rub, pocetna_greska_rub] = fitline(tocke_rob);
[koef_srediste, pocetna_greska_srediste] = fitline(tocke_center);
ukupna_greska_rub = sqrt(sum(pocetna_greska_rub.^2));
ukupna_greska_srediste = sqrt(sum(pocetna_greska_srediste.^2));
suma_odstupanja_rub = ukupna_greska_rub;
suma_odstupanja_srediste = ukupna_greska_srediste;

% Funkcija za izračun skupne napake glede na f
function skupna_napaka = izracunNapake(f, theta_rub, r_rub, theta_center, r_center)
    % Optimiziraj robne točke
    optimizirani_rob_r = (-f / 2) .* ((exp(-2 .* r_rub ./ f) - 1) ./ exp(-r_rub ./ f));
    optimizirani_center_r = (-f / 2) .* ((exp(-2 .* r_center ./ f) - 1) ./ exp(-r_center ./ f));
    
    % Pretvori nazaj v kartezične koordinate
    [x_rub_opt, y_rub_opt] = pol2cart(theta_rub, optimizirani_rob_r);
    [x_srediste_opt, y_srediste_opt] = pol2cart(theta_center, optimizirani_center_r);
    
    rub_opt_tocke = [x_rub_opt; y_rub_opt];
    srediste_opt_tocke = [x_srediste_opt; y_srediste_opt];

    % Izračunaj napake
    [~, greska_rub] = fitline(rub_opt_tocke);
    [~, greska_srediste] = fitline(srediste_opt_tocke);
    
    % Skupna napaka
    skupna_napaka = sqrt(sum(greska_rub.^2)) + sqrt(sum(greska_srediste.^2));
end

% Optimizacija z uporabo fminsearch
f_optimalno = fminsearch(@(f) izracunNapake(f, theta_rub, r_rub, theta_center, r_center), 400);

% Izpis optimalne vrednosti f
disp(['Optimalna vrednost f: ', num2str(f_optimalno)]);

==== BEGIN Aleksander Rijavec | Aleksander Rijavec_18902_assignsubmission_file | normalise2dpts.m ====
% NORMALISE2DPTS - normalises 2D homogeneous points
%
% Function translates and normalises a set of 2D homogeneous points 
% so that their centroid is at the origin and their mean distance from 
% the origin is sqrt(2).  This process typically improves the
% conditioning of any equations used to solve homographies, fundamental
% matrices etc.
%
% Usage:   [newpts, T] = normalise2dpts(pts)
%
% Argument:
%   pts -  3xN array of 2D homogeneous coordinates
%
% Returns:
%   newpts -  3xN array of transformed 2D homogeneous coordinates.  The
%             scaling parameter is normalised to 1 unless the point is at
%             infinity. 
%   T      -  The 3x3 transformation matrix, newpts = T*pts
%           
% If there are some points at infinity the normalisation transform
% is calculated using just the finite points.  Being a scaling and
% translating transform this will not affect the points at infinity.
% Peter Kovesi
% School of Computer Science & Software Engineering
% The University of Western Australia
% pk at csse uwa edu au
% http://www.csse.uwa.edu.au/~pk
%
% May 2003      - Original version
% February 2004 - Modified to deal with points at infinity.
% December 2008 - meandist calculation modified to work with Octave 3.0.1
%                 (thanks to Ron Parr)
function [newpts, T] = normalise2dpts(pts)
    if size(pts,1) ~= 3
        error('pts must be 3xN');
    end
    
    % Find the indices of the points that are not at infinity
    finiteind = find(abs(pts(3,:)) > eps);
    
    if length(finiteind) ~= size(pts,2)
        warning('Some points are at infinity');
    end
    
    % For the finite points ensure homogeneous coords have scale of 1
    pts(1,finiteind) = pts(1,finiteind)./pts(3,finiteind);
    pts(2,finiteind) = pts(2,finiteind)./pts(3,finiteind);
    pts(3,finiteind) = 1;
    
    c = mean(pts(1:2,finiteind)')';            % Centroid of finite points
    newp(1,finiteind) = pts(1,finiteind)-c(1); % Shift origin to centroid.
    newp(2,finiteind) = pts(2,finiteind)-c(2);
    
    dist = sqrt(newp(1,finiteind).^2 + newp(2,finiteind).^2);
    meandist = mean(dist(:));  % Ensure dist is a column vector for Octave 3.0.1
    
    scale = sqrt(2)/meandist;
    
    T = [scale   0   -scale*c(1)
         0     scale -scale*c(2)
         0       0      1      ];
    
    newpts = T*pts;
==== END Aleksander Rijavec | Aleksander Rijavec_18902_assignsubmission_file | normalise2dpts.m ====

==== BEGIN Alja Belinger | Alja Belinger_18897_assignsubmission_file | fitline.m ====
% FITLINE - Least squares fit of a line to a set of points
%
% Usage:   [C, dist] = fitline(XY)
%
% Where:   XY  - 2xNpts array of xy coordinates to fit line to data of
%                the form 
%                [x1 x2 x3 ... xN
%                 y1 y2 y3 ... yN]
%                
%                XY can also be a 3xNpts array of homogeneous coordinates.
%
% Returns: C    - 3x1 array of line coefficients in the form
%                 c(1)*X + c(2)*Y + c(3) = 0
%          dist - Array of distances from the fitted line to the supplied
%                 data points.  Note that dist is only calculated if the
%                 function is called with two output arguments.
%
% The magnitude of C is scaled so that line equation corresponds to
%   sin(theta)*X + (-cos(theta))*Y + rho = 0
% where theta is the angle between the line and the x axis and rho is the
% perpendicular distance from the origin to the line.  Rescaling the
% coefficients in this manner allows the perpendicular distance from any
% point (x,y) to the line to be simply calculated as
%   r = abs(c(1)*X + c(2)*Y + c(3))
%
% If you want to convert this line representation to the classical form 
%     Y = a*X + b
% use
%  a = -c(1)/c(2)
%  b = -c(3)/c(2)
%
% Note, however, that this assumes c(2) is not zero

% Copyright (c) 2003-2008 Peter Kovesi
% School of Computer Science & Software Engineering
% The University of Western Australia
% http://www.csse.uwa.edu.au/
% 
% Permission is hereby granted, free of charge, to any person obtaining a copy
% of this software and associated documentation files (the "Software"), to deal
% in the Software without restriction, subject to the following conditions:
% 
% The above copyright notice and this permission notice shall be included in 
% all copies or substantial portions of the Software.
%
% The Software is provided "as is", without warranty of any kind.

% June      2003 - Original version
% September 2004 - Rescaling to allow simple distance calculation.
% November  2008 - Normalising of coordinates added to condition the
%                  solution.
% December  2016 - Simplified rescaling of coefficients to allow line
%                  distances to be calculated


function [C, dist] = fitline(XY)
  
  [rows,npts] = size(XY);    

  if npts < 2
      error('Too few points to fit line');
  end  
  
  if rows ==2    % Add homogeneous scale coordinate of 1 
      XY = [XY; ones(1,npts)];
  end

  if npts == 2    % Pad XY with a third column of zeros
    XY = [XY zeros(3,1)]; 
  end
  
  % Normalise points so that centroid is at origin and mean distance from
  % origin is sqrt(2).  This conditions the equations. 
  [XYn, T] = normalise2dpts(XY);
  
  % Set up constraint equations of the form  XYn'*C = 0,
  % where C is a column vector of the line coefficients
  % in the form   c(1)*X + c(2)*Y + c(3) = 0.

  [u d v] = svd(XYn',0);   % Singular value decomposition.
  C = v(:,3);              % Solution is last column of v.

  % Denormalise the solution
  C = T'*C;
  
  % Rescale coefficients so that line equation corresponds to
  %   sin(theta)*X + (-cos(theta))*Y + rho = 0
  % so that the perpendicular distance from any point (x,y) to the line
  % to be simply calculated as 
  %   r = abs(c(1)*X + c(2)*Y + c(3))
  C = C / sqrt(C(1)^2 + C(2)^2);
  
  % If requested, calculate the distances from the fitted line to
  % the supplied data points 
  if nargout==2   
      dist = abs(C(1)*XY(1,:) + C(2)*XY(2,:) + C(3));
  end
==== END Alja Belinger | Alja Belinger_18897_assignsubmission_file | fitline.m ====

==== BEGIN Alja Belinger | Alja Belinger_18897_assignsubmission_file | projektivna_transformacija.m ====
% 2. Projektivna transformacija
clear; close all; clc;

% Preberi sivinsko sliko (že brez radialne distorzije)
I = imread('Image_rect1.tif');  % tukaj vstavi svojo undistorted sliko
if size(I,3) == 3
    I = rgb2gray(I);
end

% Kliknem 4 vogale šahovnice (v smeri urinega kazalca)
figure; imshow(I); title('Klikni 4 vogale šahovnice (v smeri urinega kazalca)');
[x, y] = ginput(4);
hold on; plot(x, y, 'ro-', 'LineWidth', 2);

% Določi idealne (nepopačene) točke
X = [0 640 640 0];
Y = [0 0 480 480];

% Izračun projektivne transformacije (fitgeotrans)
tform = fitgeotrans([x y], [X' Y'], 'projective');

% Korekcija slike (imwarp)
J = imwarp(I, tform, 'OutputView', imref2d(size(I)));

% Rezultati
figure;
subplot(1,2,1); imshow(I); title('Izvirna slika');
subplot(1,2,2); imshow(J); title('Korigirana slika');

% Velikost mreže
numCols = 14; % število stolpcev
numRows = 13; % število vrstic
xv = linspace(0, 640, numCols+1);
yv = linspace(0, 480, numRows+1);

% Generiraj mrežne točke
[Xlist, Ylist] = deal([]);
for i = 1:length(yv)
    for j = 1:length(xv)
        Xlist = [Xlist; xv(j)];
        Ylist = [Ylist; yv(i)];
    end
end
gridPoints = [Xlist, Ylist];

% Inverzna transformacija – iz idealnega koordinatnega sistema v originalno sliko
tform_inv = invert(tform);
[origX, origY] = transformPointsForward(tform_inv, gridPoints(:,1), gridPoints(:,2));

figure; imshow(I); title('Izvirna slika z mrežo (scatter prikaz)');
hold on;
scatter(origX, origY, 20, 'g', 'filled'); % 20 = velikost markerja

% Če želiš obarvati vogalne točke posebej:
cornerIdx = [1, numCols+1, (numRows)*(numCols+1)+1, (numRows+1)*(numCols+1)];
scatter(origX(cornerIdx), origY(cornerIdx), 60, 'r', 'filled'); % rdeče vogale
legend('Mrežne točke', 'Vogali');
==== END Alja Belinger | Alja Belinger_18897_assignsubmission_file | projektivna_transformacija.m ====

==== BEGIN Alja Belinger | Alja Belinger_18897_assignsubmission_file | Radialna_distorzija.m ====
% RADIALNA_DISTORZIJA - Odprava radialne distorzije slike z optimizacijo

function radialna_distorzija(ime_slike)

    if nargin < 1
        ime_slike = 'Image15.tif'; % privzeta slika iz naloge
    end

    % --- 1. Branje slike ---
    I = imread(ime_slike);
    figure, imshow(I);
    title({'Klikni točke na dveh vertikalnih linijah šahovnice', ...
           '(najprej vse točke prve linije, nato vse točke druge)'});

    % --- 2. Označevanje točk ---
    disp('Klikni točke na PRVI liniji, nato pritisni Enter');
    [x1, y1] = ginput; % prva linija
    disp('Klikni točke na DRUGI liniji, nato pritisni Enter');
    [x2, y2] = ginput; % druga linija

    lin1 = [x1'; y1'];
    lin2 = [x2'; y2'];

    % --- 3. Definicija funkcije napake za optimizacijo ---
    err_fun = @(f) napaka_distorzije(f, lin1, lin2, size(I));

    % --- 4. Optimizacija parametra f (z uporabo nelinearne metode) ---
    f0 = 500; % začetna ocena
    f_opt = fminsearch(err_fun, f0);
    fprintf('Optimalni parameter f = %.2f\n', f_opt);

    % --- 5. Korekcija slike z najboljšim f ---
    [Xp1, Yp1] = popravi_tocke(lin1, f_opt, size(I));
    [Xp2, Yp2] = popravi_tocke(lin2, f_opt, size(I));

    % --- 6. Prikaz originalnih in korigiranih točk ---
    figure, imshow(I); hold on;
    plot(lin1(1,:), lin1(2,:), 'ro', 'MarkerFaceColor', 'r');
    plot(lin2(1,:), lin2(2,:), 'bo', 'MarkerFaceColor', 'b');
    plot(Xp1, Yp1, 'go', 'MarkerFaceColor', 'g');
    plot(Xp2, Yp2, 'co', 'MarkerFaceColor', 'c');

    % --- 7. Izračun in prikaz premic (pred in po korekciji) ---
    C1 = fitline(lin1);
    C2 = fitline(lin2);
    C1_corr = fitline([Xp1; Yp1]);
    C2_corr = fitline([Xp2; Yp2]);

    x = 1:size(I,2);
    y1 = -(C1(1)*x + C1(3)) / C1(2);
    y2 = -(C2(1)*x + C2(3)) / C2(2);
    y1_corr = -(C1_corr(1)*x + C1_corr(3)) / C1_corr(2);
    y2_corr = -(C2_corr(1)*x + C2_corr(3)) / C2_corr(2);

    plot(x, y1, 'r-', 'LineWidth', 1.2);
    plot(x, y2, 'b-', 'LineWidth', 1.2);
    plot(x, y1_corr, 'g--', 'LineWidth', 1.5);
    plot(x, y2_corr, 'c--', 'LineWidth', 1.5);

    legend('Linija 1 - original', 'Linija 2 - original', ...
           'Linija 1 - korigirano', 'Linija 2 - korigirano', ...
           'Location', 'southoutside');
    title(['Korigirane točke (f = ', num2str(f_opt, '%.2f'), ')']);
    hold off;

    % --- 8. Analiza napake glede na f ---
    f_values = 400:20:800;
    errors = zeros(size(f_values));

    for i = 1:length(f_values)
        errors(i) = napaka_distorzije(f_values(i), lin1, lin2, size(I));
    end

    figure;
    plot(f_values, errors, 'b-o', 'LineWidth', 1.5);
    xlabel('Parameter f');
    ylabel('Vsota kvadratov razdalj');
    title('Odvisnost napake od parametra f');
    grid on;
end

% -------------------------------------------------------------------------
function E = napaka_distorzije(f, lin1, lin2, img_size)
    % Izračuna povprečno vsoto kvadratov razdalj do idealne premice
    [Xp1, Yp1] = popravi_tocke(lin1, f, img_size);
    [Xp2, Yp2] = popravi_tocke(lin2, f, img_size);

    [~, d1] = fitline([Xp1; Yp1]);
    [~, d2] = fitline([Xp2; Yp2]);

    % Povprečje napak obeh linij
    E = (sum(d1.^2) + sum(d2.^2)) / (length(d1) + length(d2));
end

% -------------------------------------------------------------------------
function [Xp, Yp] = popravi_tocke(XY, f, img_size)
    % Uporabi model iz enačbe (2) za popravek radialne distorzije
    cx = img_size(2) / 2; % središče slike (x)
    cy = img_size(1) / 2; % središče slike (y)

    X = XY(1,:) - cx;
    Y = XY(2,:) - cy;
    r = sqrt(X.^2 + Y.^2);

    % Inverzni model radialne distorzije
    rp = -(f/2) .* ((exp(-2*r./f) - 1) ./ exp(-r./f));

    % Pretvorba nazaj v slikovne koordinate
    Xp = rp .* X ./ (r + eps) + cx;
    Yp = rp .* Y ./ (r + eps) + cy;
end
==== END Alja Belinger | Alja Belinger_18897_assignsubmission_file | Radialna_distorzija.m ====

%SLIKE 2 3 6 8 13
% NAPAKA od -1 do 1

% FITLINE - Least squares fit of a line to a set of points
%
% Usage:   [C, dist] = fitline(XY)
%
% Where:   XY  - 2xNpts array of xy coordinates to fit line to data of
%                the form 
%                [x1 x2 x3 ... xN
%                 y1 y2 y3 ... yN]
%                
%                XY can also be a 3xNpts array of homogeneous coordinates.
%
% Returns: C    - 3x1 array of line coefficients in the form
%                 c(1)*X + c(2)*Y + c(3) = 0
%          dist - Array of distances from the fitted line to the supplied
%                 data points.  Note that dist is only calculated if the
%                 function is called with two output arguments.
%
% The magnitude of C is scaled so that line equation corresponds to
%   sin(theta)*X + (-cos(theta))*Y + rho = 0
% where theta is the angle between the line and the x axis and rho is the
% perpendicular distance from the origin to the line.  Rescaling the
% coefficients in this manner allows the perpendicular distance from any
% point (x,y) to the line to be simply calculated as
%   r = abs(c(1)*X + c(2)*Y + c(3))
%
% If you want to convert this line representation to the classical form 
%     Y = a*X + b
% use
%  a = -c(1)/c(2)
%  b = -c(3)/c(2)
%
% Note, however, that this assumes c(2) is not zero

% Copyright (c) 2003-2008 Peter Kovesi
% School of Computer Science & Software Engineering
% The University of Western Australia
% http://www.csse.uwa.edu.au/
% 
% Permission is hereby granted, free of charge, to any person obtaining a copy
% of this software and associated documentation files (the "Software"), to deal
% in the Software without restriction, subject to the following conditions:
% 
% The above copyright notice and this permission notice shall be included in 
% all copies or substantial portions of the Software.
%
% The Software is provided "as is", without warranty of any kind.

% June      2003 - Original version
% September 2004 - Rescaling to allow simple distance calculation.
% November  2008 - Normalising of coordinates added to condition the
%                  solution.
% December  2016 - Simplified rescaling of coefficients to allow line
%                  distances to be calculated


function [C, dist] = fitline(XY)
  
  [rows,npts] = size(XY);    

  if npts < 2
      error('Too few points to fit line');
  end  
  
  if rows ==2    % Add homogeneous scale coordinate of 1 
      XY = [XY; ones(1,npts)];
  end

  if npts == 2    % Pad XY with a third column of zeros
    XY = [XY zeros(3,1)]; 
  end
  
  % Normalise points so that centroid is at origin and mean distance from
  % origin is sqrt(2).  This conditions the equations. 
  [XYn, T] = normalise2dpts(XY);
  
  % Set up constraint equations of the form  XYn'*C = 0,
  % where C is a column vector of the line coefficients
  % in the form   c(1)*X + c(2)*Y + c(3) = 0.

  [u d v] = svd(XYn',0);   % Singular value decomposition.
  C = v(:,3);              % Solution is last column of v.

  % Denormalise the solution
  C = T'*C;
  
  % Rescale coefficients so that line equation corresponds to
  %   sin(theta)*X + (-cos(theta))*Y + rho = 0
  % so that the perpendicular distance from any point (x,y) to the line
  % to be simply calculated as 
  %   r = abs(c(1)*X + c(2)*Y + c(3))
  C = C / sqrt(C(1)^2 + C(2)^2);
  
  % If requested, calculate the distances from the fitted line to
  % the supplied data points 
  if nargout==2   
      dist = abs(C(1)*XY(1,:) + C(2)*XY(2,:) + C(3));
  end

slika = imread('Image_rect2.tif'); 
slika = repmat(slika, [1, 1, 3]);

figure;
image(slika);
title('Izberite 4 točke na sliki');
[x, y] = ginput(4); 

% risanje točk
hold on;
plot(x, y, 'ro', 'MarkerSize', 5); %rdeči krogi
hold off;

% Definiranje vhodnih in idelnih točk za projektivno transformacijo
input = [x(1) y(1); x(2) y(2); x(3) y(3); x(4) y(4)]; 
ideal = [0 0; 0 255; 255 255; 255 0]; 

% projektivna transformacija
T = maketform("projective", input, ideal); 
transf = imtransform(slika, T, 'XData', [0, 255], 'YData', [0, 255]);

% sahovnica
korakX = 255 / 11;
korakY = 255 / 12;
[X, Y] = meshgrid(0:korakX:255, 0:korakY:255);
vogal = [X(:), Y(:)];

figure;
imshow(transf);
title('Korigirana slika');
hold on;
plot(vogal(:,1), vogal(:,2), 'rx');
hold off;

% inverzna transformacija
T_inverz = maketform('projective', ideal, input);

% prikaz oglišč na osnovni sliki
vogal_ = tformfwd(T_inverz, vogal);

figure;
imshow(slika);
title('Označeni vsi vogali');
hold on;
plot(vogal_(:,1), vogal_(:,2), 'rx');
hold off;

==== BEGIN Amar Šaranović | Amar Šaranović_18913_assignsubmission_file | Naloga3_final.m ====
slika = imread('Image15.tif');
rgb_slika = repmat(slika, 1, 1, 3);
figure;
imshow(rgb_slika);
title('Kliknite 12 točk: prvih 6 na robu, naslednjih 6 v središču');

[x_koordinate, y_koordinate] = ginput(12);
disp('Izbrane točke (x, y):');
tabela_tock = [x_koordinate, y_koordinate];
disp(tabela_tock);
[visina, sirina, ~] = size(slika);
tocke_rob = [(sirina / 2 - x_koordinate(1:6))'; (visina / 2 - y_koordinate(1:6))'];
tocke_center = [(sirina / 2 - x_koordinate(7:12))'; (visina / 2 - y_koordinate(7:12))'];
[theta_rub, r_rub] = cart2pol(tocke_rob(1,:), tocke_rob(2,:));
[theta_center, r_center] = cart2pol(tocke_center(1,:), tocke_center(2,:));


[koef_rub, pocetna_greska_rub] = fitline(tocke_rob);
[koef_srediste, pocetna_greska_srediste] = fitline(tocke_center);
ukupna_greska_rub = sqrt(sum(pocetna_greska_rub.^2));
ukupna_greska_srediste = sqrt(sum(pocetna_greska_srediste.^2));
suma_odstupanja_rub = ukupna_greska_rub;
suma_odstupanja_srediste = ukupna_greska_srediste;

for f = 400:20:800
    optimizirani_rob_r = (-f / 2) .* ((exp(-2 .* r_rub ./ f) - 1) ./ exp(-r_rub ./ f));
    optimizirani_center_r = (-f / 2) .* ((exp(-2 .* r_center ./ f) - 1) ./ exp(-r_center ./ f));

    % polarni v krtezicni
    [x_rub_opt, y_rub_opt] = pol2cart(theta_rub, optimizirani_rob_r);
    [x_srediste_opt, y_srediste_opt] = pol2cart(theta_center, optimizirani_center_r);

    rub_opt_tocke = [x_rub_opt; y_rub_opt];
    srediste_opt_tocke = [x_srediste_opt; y_srediste_opt];

    % Izračun grešaka za optimizirane točke
    [~, greska_rub] = fitline(rub_opt_tocke);
    [~, greska_srediste] = fitline(srediste_opt_tocke);
    
    suma_odstupanja_rub = [suma_odstupanja_rub, sqrt(sum(greska_rub.^2))];
    suma_odstupanja_srediste = [suma_odstupanja_srediste, sqrt(sum(greska_srediste.^2))];
end

% prikaz
f_values = 400:20:800;
figure;
plot(f_values, suma_odstupanja_rub(2:end) + suma_odstupanja_srediste(2:end));
title('Skupna napaka v odvisnosti od f');
xlabel('f');
ylabel('Skupna vsota kvadratov napak');

==== BEGIN Amar Šaranović | Amar Šaranović_18913_assignsubmission_file | Naloga4.m ====
slika = imread('Image15.tif');
rgb_slika = repmat(slika, 1, 1, 3);
%figure;
%imshow(rgb_slika);
%title('Kliknite 12 točk: prvih 6 na robu, naslednjih 6 v središču');

% Izberi 12 točk na sliki
%[x_koordinate, y_koordinate] = ginput(12);
x_koordinate = [43.8898, 42.0124, 40.7608, 38.8833, 43.2640, 43.8898,274.8155, 271.6864, 266.6799, 259.1701, 254.1636, 251.6604]';
y_koordinate = [67.1493, 144.7503, 221.0997, 257.3970, 326.2366, 392.5730,82.1688, 120.3435, 194.1897, 266.1584, 333.7464, 396.3279]';
[visina, sirina, ~] = size(slika);


% Razdeli točke na robne in sredinske
tocke_rob = [(sirina / 2 - x_koordinate(1:6))'; (visina / 2 - y_koordinate(1:6))'];
tocke_center = [(sirina / 2 - x_koordinate(7:12))'; (visina / 2 - y_koordinate(7:12))'];

% Pretvori točke iz kartezičnih v polarne koordinate
[theta_rub, r_rub] = cart2pol(tocke_rob(1,:), tocke_rob(2,:));
[theta_center, r_center] = cart2pol(tocke_center(1,:), tocke_center(2,:));
% Začetni izračun napak brez optimizacije
[koef_rub, pocetna_greska_rub] = fitline(tocke_rob);
[koef_srediste, pocetna_greska_srediste] = fitline(tocke_center);
ukupna_greska_rub = sqrt(sum(pocetna_greska_rub.^2));
ukupna_greska_srediste = sqrt(sum(pocetna_greska_srediste.^2));
suma_odstupanja_rub = ukupna_greska_rub;
suma_odstupanja_srediste = ukupna_greska_srediste;

% Funkcija za izračun skupne napake glede na f
function skupna_napaka = izracunNapake(f, theta_rub, r_rub, theta_center, r_center)
    % Optimiziraj robne točke
    optimizirani_rob_r = (-f / 2) .* ((exp(-2 .* r_rub ./ f) - 1) ./ exp(-r_rub ./ f));
    optimizirani_center_r = (-f / 2) .* ((exp(-2 .* r_center ./ f) - 1) ./ exp(-r_center ./ f));
    
    % Pretvori nazaj v kartezične koordinate
    [x_rub_opt, y_rub_opt] = pol2cart(theta_rub, optimizirani_rob_r);
    [x_srediste_opt, y_srediste_opt] = pol2cart(theta_center, optimizirani_center_r);
    
    rub_opt_tocke = [x_rub_opt; y_rub_opt];
    srediste_opt_tocke = [x_srediste_opt; y_srediste_opt];

    % Izračunaj napake
    [~, greska_rub] = fitline(rub_opt_tocke);
    [~, greska_srediste] = fitline(srediste_opt_tocke);
    
    % Skupna napaka
    skupna_napaka = sqrt(sum(greska_rub.^2)) + sqrt(sum(greska_srediste.^2));
end

% Optimizacija z uporabo fminsearch
f_optimalno = fminsearch(@(f) izracunNapake(f, theta_rub, r_rub, theta_center, r_center), 400);

% Izpis optimalne vrednosti f
disp(['Optimalna vrednost f: ', num2str(f_optimalno)]);

==== END Amar Šaranović | Amar Šaranović_18913_assignsubmission_file | Naloga4.m ====

==== BEGIN Amar Šaranović | Amar Šaranović_18913_assignsubmission_file | normalise2dpts.m ====
% NORMALISE2DPTS - normalises 2D homogeneous points
%
% Function translates and normalises a set of 2D homogeneous points 
% so that their centroid is at the origin and their mean distance from 
% the origin is sqrt(2).  This process typically improves the
% conditioning of any equations used to solve homographies, fundamental
% matrices etc.
%
% Usage:   [newpts, T] = normalise2dpts(pts)
%
% Argument:
%   pts -  3xN array of 2D homogeneous coordinates
%
% Returns:
%   newpts -  3xN array of transformed 2D homogeneous coordinates.  The
%             scaling parameter is normalised to 1 unless the point is at
%             infinity. 
%   T      -  The 3x3 transformation matrix, newpts = T*pts
%           
% If there are some points at infinity the normalisation transform
% is calculated using just the finite points.  Being a scaling and
% translating transform this will not affect the points at infinity.
% Peter Kovesi
% School of Computer Science & Software Engineering
% The University of Western Australia
% pk at csse uwa edu au
% http://www.csse.uwa.edu.au/~pk
%
% May 2003      - Original version
% February 2004 - Modified to deal with points at infinity.
% December 2008 - meandist calculation modified to work with Octave 3.0.1
%                 (thanks to Ron Parr)
function [newpts, T] = normalise2dpts(pts)
    if size(pts,1) ~= 3
        error('pts must be 3xN');
    end
    
    % Find the indices of the points that are not at infinity
    finiteind = find(abs(pts(3,:)) > eps);
    
    if length(finiteind) ~= size(pts,2)
        warning('Some points are at infinity');
    end
    
    % For the finite points ensure homogeneous coords have scale of 1
    pts(1,finiteind) = pts(1,finiteind)./pts(3,finiteind);
    pts(2,finiteind) = pts(2,finiteind)./pts(3,finiteind);
    pts(3,finiteind) = 1;
    
    c = mean(pts(1:2,finiteind)')';            % Centroid of finite points
    newp(1,finiteind) = pts(1,finiteind)-c(1); % Shift origin to centroid.
    newp(2,finiteind) = pts(2,finiteind)-c(2);
    
    dist = sqrt(newp(1,finiteind).^2 + newp(2,finiteind).^2);
    meandist = mean(dist(:));  % Ensure dist is a column vector for Octave 3.0.1
    
    scale = sqrt(2)/meandist;
    
    T = [scale   0   -scale*c(1)
         0     scale -scale*c(2)
         0       0      1      ];
    
    newpts = T*pts;
==== END Amar Šaranović | Amar Šaranović_18913_assignsubmission_file | normalise2dpts.m ====

img = imread('Image15.tif');

if size(img, 3) == 3
    img = rgb2gray(img);
end
img = im2double(img);

 
disp('Označite 6 tacaka na rubu slike.');
figure();
imshow(img);
[x1,y1] = ginput(6);
close();

disp('Označi 6 tacaka blizu centra.');
figure();
imshow(img);
[x2,y2] = ginput(6);
close();

%Centar slike
[height, width] = size(img);  % Za sivu sliku, visina i širina
x = width / 2; 
y = height / 2;

% Koordinate tačaka sa ivice
xy1 = zeros(2, 6);
for i = 1:6
    xy1(1,i) = x1(i) - x;  % Premesti X-koordinate
    xy1(2,i) = y1(i) - y;  % Premesti Y-koordinate
end

% Koordinate tačaka sa centra
xy2 = zeros(2, 6);
for i = 1:6
    xy2(1,i) = x2(i) - x;  % Premesti X-koordinate
    xy2(2,i) = y2(i) - y;  % Premesti Y-koordinate
end

    
[c1,d1] = fitline(xy1);
[c2,d2] = fitline(xy2);

D_1 = sum(d1 .^ 2);
D_2 = sum(d2 .^ 2);

%Polarne koordinate
r_rub = zeros(1,6);
r_centar = zeros(1,6);
ugao_rob = zeros(1,6);
ugao_centar = zeros(1,6);

[ugao_rob, r_rub] = cart2pol(xy1(1,:), xy1(2,:));
[ugao_centar, r_centar]= cart2pol(xy2(1,:), xy2(2,:));

f_vals = 400:20:800;
disp(length(f_vals));

rn_rob = zeros(1,6);
xy1_n = zeros(2,6);
r_n_centar = zeros(1,6);
xy2_n = zeros(2,6);

%i=1; %brojac
%Greske
D1 = zeros(1, length(f_vals));
D2 = zeros(1, length(f_vals));

for i = 1:length(f_vals)
    f=f_vals(i);
    rn_rob = (- f / 2) .* (exp(-2 * r_rub ./ f) - 1) ./ exp(-r_rub / f);
    r_n_centar = (- f / 2) .* (exp(-2 * r_centar ./ f) - 1) ./ exp(-r_centar / f);

    [xy1_n(1,:),xy1_n(2,:)] = pol2cart(ugao_rob,rn_rob);
    [xy2_n(1,:),xy2_n(2,:)] = pol2cart(ugao_centar,r_n_centar); 

    [c1n, d1n] = fitline(xy1_n);
    [c2n, d2n] = fitline(xy2_n);
    
    D_1n = sum(d1n .^2);
    D1(i) = D_1n;
    D_2n = sum(d2n .^2);
    D2(i) = D_2n;
end

Dsum = D1 + D2;
[minsweep, idxmin] = min(Dsum);
f_min = f_vals(idxmin);

figure; hold on; grid on;
f = 400:20:800;
plot(f, D1, 'g-', f, D2, 'b-', f, Dsum, 'r-', 'LineWidth', 2);

%Oznacen minimum
plot(f_min, minsweep, 'ko', 'MarkerSize', 8, 'MarkerFaceColor', 'y');
xline(f_min, '--k');
xlabel('Vrednosti parametra f');
ylabel('Suma kvadrata udaljenosti');
legend('Rub','Centar','Zbir','Min(Zbir)');

fprintf('Minimum pri f_min = %.1f\n', f_min);



% Optimizacija
f0=400;
cos_function = @(f)cost_fcn(f, ugao_rob,r_rub, ugao_centar, r_centar); %cos radijalne distorzije
[f_opt,e] = fminsearch(cos_function,f0); 
fprintf('Najboljše korigirana radijalna distrozija je pri f = %d',round(f_opt))

==== BEGIN Bine Sever | Bine Sever_18899_assignsubmission_file | fitline.m ====
% FITLINE - Least squares fit of a line to a set of points
%
% Usage:   [C, dist] = fitline(XY)
%
% Where:   XY  - 2xNpts array of xy coordinates to fit line to data of
%                the form 
%                [x1 x2 x3 ... xN
%                 y1 y2 y3 ... yN]
%                
%                XY can also be a 3xNpts array of homogeneous coordinates.
%
% Returns: C    - 3x1 array of line coefficients in the form
%                 c(1)*X + c(2)*Y + c(3) = 0
%          dist - Array of distances from the fitted line to the supplied
%                 data points.  Note that dist is only calculated if the
%                 function is called with two output arguments.
%
% The magnitude of C is scaled so that line equation corresponds to
%   sin(theta)*X + (-cos(theta))*Y + rho = 0
% where theta is the angle between the line and the x axis and rho is the
% perpendicular distance from the origin to the line.  Rescaling the
% coefficients in this manner allows the perpendicular distance from any
% point (x,y) to the line to be simply calculated as
%   r = abs(c(1)*X + c(2)*Y + c(3))
%
% If you want to convert this line representation to the classical form 
%     Y = a*X + b
% use
%  a = -c(1)/c(2)
%  b = -c(3)/c(2)
%
% Note, however, that this assumes c(2) is not zero

% Copyright (c) 2003-2008 Peter Kovesi
% www.peterkovesi.com
% 
% Permission is hereby granted, free of charge, to any person obtaining a copy
% of this software and associated documentation files (the "Software"), to deal
% in the Software without restriction, subject to the following conditions:
% 
% The above copyright notice and this permission notice shall be included in 
% all copies or substantial portions of the Software.
%
% The Software is provided "as is", without warranty of any kind.

% June      2003 - Original version
% September 2004 - Rescaling to allow simple distance calculation.
% November  2008 - Normalising of coordinates added to condition the
%                  solution.
% December  2016 - Simplified rescaling of coefficients to allow line
%                  distances to be calculated


function [C, dist] = fitline(XY)
  
  [rows,npts] = size(XY);    

  if npts < 2
      error('Too few points to fit line');
  end  
  
  if rows ==2    % Add homogeneous scale coordinate of 1 
      XY = [XY; ones(1,npts)];
  end

  if npts == 2    % Pad XY with a third column of zeros
    XY = [XY zeros(3,1)]; 
  end
  
  % Normalise points so that centroid is at origin and mean distance from
  % origin is sqrt(2).  This conditions the equations. 
  [XYn, T] = normalise2dpts(XY);
  
  % Set up constraint equations of the form  XYn'*C = 0,
  % where C is a column vector of the line coefficients
  % in the form   c(1)*X + c(2)*Y + c(3) = 0.

  [u d v] = svd(XYn',0);   % Singular value decomposition.
  C = v(:,3);              % Solution is last column of v.

  % Denormalise the solution
  C = T'*C;
  
  % Rescale coefficients so that line equation corresponds to
  %   sin(theta)*X + (-cos(theta))*Y + rho = 0
  % so that the perpendicular distance from any point (x,y) to the line
  % to be simply calculated as 
  %   r = abs(c(1)*X + c(2)*Y + c(3))
  C = C / sqrt(C(1)^2 + C(2)^2);
  
  % If requested, calculate the distances from the fitted line to
  % the supplied data points 
  if nargout==2   
      dist = abs(C(1)*XY(1,:) + C(2)*XY(2,:) + C(3));
  end




  
       
       
==== END Bine Sever | Bine Sever_18899_assignsubmission_file | fitline.m ====

==== BEGIN Bine Sever | Bine Sever_18899_assignsubmission_file | normalise2dpts.m ====
% NORMALISE2DPTS - normalises 2D homogeneous points
%
% Function translates and normalises a set of 2D homogeneous points 
% so that their centroid is at the origin and their mean distance from 
% the origin is sqrt(2).  This process typically improves the
% conditioning of any equations used to solve homographies, fundamental
% matrices etc.
%
% Usage:   [newpts, T] = normalise2dpts(pts)
%
% Argument:
%   pts -  3xN array of 2D homogeneous coordinates
%
% Returns:
%   newpts -  3xN array of transformed 2D homogeneous coordinates.  The
%             scaling parameter is normalised to 1 unless the point is at
%             infinity. 
%   T      -  The 3x3 transformation matrix, newpts = T*pts
%           
% If there are some points at infinity the normalisation transform
% is calculated using just the finite points.  Being a scaling and
% translating transform this will not affect the points at infinity.

% Peter Kovesi
% www.peterkovesi.com
%
% May 2003      - Original version
% February 2004 - Modified to deal with points at infinity.
% December 2008 - meandist calculation modified to work with Octave 3.0.1
%                 (thanks to Ron Parr)
% December 2016 - Disabled warning messgae for points at infinity.


function [newpts, T] = normalise2dpts(pts)

    if size(pts,1) ~= 3
        error('pts must be 3xN');
    end
    
    % Find the indices of the points that are not at infinity
    finiteind = find(abs(pts(3,:)) > eps);
    
%    if length(finiteind) ~= size(pts,2)
%        warning('Some points are at infinity');
%    end
    
    % For the finite points ensure homogeneous coords have scale of 1
    pts(1,finiteind) = pts(1,finiteind)./pts(3,finiteind);
    pts(2,finiteind) = pts(2,finiteind)./pts(3,finiteind);
    pts(3,finiteind) = 1;
    
    c = mean(pts(1:2,finiteind)')';            % Centroid of finite points
    newp(1,finiteind) = pts(1,finiteind)-c(1); % Shift origin to centroid.
    newp(2,finiteind) = pts(2,finiteind)-c(2);
    
    dist = sqrt(newp(1,finiteind).^2 + newp(2,finiteind).^2);
    meandist = mean(dist(:));  % Ensure dist is a column vector for Octave 3.0.1
    
    scale = sqrt(2)/meandist;
    
    T = [scale   0   -scale*c(1)
         0     scale -scale*c(2)
         0       0      1      ];
    
    newpts = T*pts;
    
    
    
==== END Bine Sever | Bine Sever_18899_assignsubmission_file | normalise2dpts.m ====

y_kvadratov = 11;

sizey = y_kvadratov*dxy;

                 sizex 0;
                 sizex sizey;
                 0 sizey];

I_corrected = imtransform(sahovnica, T, 'XData', [0 sizex], 'YData', [0 sizey]);

num_corners_y = y_kvadratov + 1;

figure;
imshow(sahovnica)
hold on;

title('popravljene točke')

figure
ImDistorsion = imread("Image15.tif");

title("Označi 6 presečišč vertikalnih stranic šahovnice to izriše premico, nariši 2 premice")

cx = 303.44701;
cy = 242.59023;

for i = 1:2
    [x2,y2] = ginput(6);
    XY = [x2'; y2'];
    C = fitline(XY);

    ypoints = linspace(min(y2), max(y2), 500);
    xpoints = (-(ypoints*C(2)) - C(3)) / C(1);

    if i == 1
        plot(xpoints,ypoints,"b")
        alpha1  = atan2(XY(2,:) - cy, XY(1,:) - cx);
        r_dist1 = sqrt((cx-XY(1,:)).^2 + (cy-XY(2,:)).^2);
    elseif i == 2
        plot(xpoints,ypoints,"g")
        alpha2 = atan2(XY(2,:) - cy, XY(1,:) - cx);
        r_dist2 = sqrt((cx-XY(1,:)).^2 + (cy-XY(2,:)).^2);
    end
 
end

r2_popr = r_dist2;

Y1_popr = XY(2);

f_values = 400:20:800;

sumSq2 = zeros(size(f_values));
SummAll = zeros(size(f_values));


for k = 1:numel(f_values)
    f = f_values(k);

        r1_popr = (-f/2) .* ((exp(-(2*r_dist1)./f) - 1) ./ exp(-(r_dist1)./f));
        r2_popr = (-f/2) .* ((exp(-(2*r_dist2)./f) - 1) ./ exp(-(r_dist2)./f));

        X1_popr = cx + r1_popr .* cos(alpha1);
        Y1_popr = cy + r1_popr .* sin(alpha1);
        XY1 = [X1_popr; Y1_popr];
        [~,dst1] = fitline(XY1);
        sumSq1(k) = sum(dst1.^2);

        X2_popr = cx + r2_popr .* cos(alpha2);
        Y2_popr = cy + r2_popr .* sin(alpha2);
        XY2 = [X2_popr; Y2_popr];
        [~,dst2] = fitline(XY2);
        sumSq2(k) = sum(dst2.^2);

        SummAll(k) = sumSq1(k) + sumSq2(k);

end
      
figure
title("Napaka distorzije")
plot(f_values,sumSq1,'b')
hold on
plot(f_values,sumSq2,'g');
hold on
plot(f_values,SummAll,'y')
legend('Napaka prve črte','Napaka druge črte', 'skupna napaka')
xlabel("f")
ylabel("Napaka")

%Naloga 4
function skupna_napaka = PopraviDistorzijo(f, cx, cy, r_dists, alphas)

    skupna_napaka = 0;

    for i = 1:numel(r_dists)
        r_corr = (-f/2) .* ((exp(-(2*r_dists{i})./f) - 1) ./ exp(-(r_dists{i})./f));

        X_corr = cx + r_corr .* cos(alphas{i});
        Y_corr = cy + r_corr .* sin(alphas{i});

        [~, dist] = fitline([X_corr; Y_corr]);

        skupna_napaka = skupna_napaka + sum(dist.^2);
    end
end


r_dists = {r_dist1, r_dist2};
alphas = {alpha1, alpha2};

f0 = 600;

f_opt = fminsearch(@(f) PopraviDistorzijo(f, cx, cy, r_dists, alphas), f0);

fprintf('Optimalni parameter f = %.2f\n', f_opt);

% Intrinsic and Extrinsic Camera Parameters
%
% This script file can be directly excecuted under Matlab to recover the camera intrinsic and extrinsic parameters.
% IMPORTANT: This file contains neither the structure of the calibration objects nor the image coordinates of the calibration points.
%            All those complementary variables are saved in the complete matlab data file Calib_Results.mat.
% For more information regarding the calibration model visit http://www.vision.caltech.edu/bouguetj/calib_doc/


%-- Focal length:
fc = [ 657.453175445359875 ; 658.137763319905275 ];

%-- Principal point:

%-- Distortion coefficients:

%-- Image size:
nx = 640;
ny = 480;


%-- Various other variables (may be ignored if you do not use the Matlab Calibration Toolbox):
%-- Those variables are used to control which intrinsic parameters should be optimized

n_ima = 20;						% Number of calibration images
est_fc = [ 1 ; 1 ];					% Estimation indicator of the two focal variables
est_aspect_ratio = 1;				% Estimation indicator of the aspect ratio fc(2)/fc(1)

%-- Extrinsic parameters:
%-- The rotation (omc_kk) and the translation (Tc_kk) vectors for every calibration image and their uncertainties

%-- Image #1:

Tc_error_1  = [ NaN ; NaN ; NaN ];

clear all;

%% Druga naloga:
A = imread('Image_rect2.tif');
A = repmat(A,[1,1,3]);

figure;
image(A);                  % prikaz slike
%axis image;               % ohrani razmerje stranic
axis off;                  % skrij osi (enako kot imshow)
%set(gca,'YDir','reverse') % obvezno! da se Y-os obrne kot pri imshow
title('Klikni 4 vogale šahovnice v pravilnem vrstnem redu (npr. levo zgoraj, desno zgoraj, desno spodaj, levo spodaj)');
hold on;
for i = 1:4
    [x(i), y(i)] = ginput(1); % vrne vektor x in y koordinat klikov
    scatter(x(i), y(i), 500, 'red', 'x', 'LineWidth', 2);
    drawnow; % osveži prikaz takoj
end
hold off; % končamo dodajanje, naslednja risba bo nova figura

vhodne_tocke_vogali = [x(:), y(:)]; % 4×2 matrika
[visina, sirina] = size(A); % višina in širina nove slike
izhodne_tocke_vogali = [0 0; sirina-1 0; sirina-1 visina-1; 0 visina-1];

projektivna_transformacija = maketform('projective', vhodne_tocke_vogali, izhodne_tocke_vogali);
poravnana_slika = imtransform(A, projektivna_transformacija, 'XData', [0 sirina-1], 'YData', [0 visina-1]);

figure;
image(poravnana_slika);
%axis image;
axis off;
%set(gca,'YDir','reverse');
title('Poravnana (projektivno popravljena) šahovnica');

st_kvadratkov_X = 12; % število kvadratkov horizontalno
st_kvadratkov_Y = 13; % število kvadratkov vertikalno

vsi_vogali_x = linspace(0, sirina-1, st_kvadratkov_X+1); % +1 ker vogalov je vedno za ena več kot kvadratov
vsi_vogali_y = linspace(0, visina-1, st_kvadratkov_Y+1);

[x, y] = meshgrid(vsi_vogali_x, vsi_vogali_y);
vsi_vogali = [x(:), y(:)];  % vse vogalne točke v poravnani sliki

% Poravnana šahovnica
figure;
image(poravnana_slika);
%axis image;
axis off;
%set(gca,'YDir','reverse');
axis([0 sirina 0 visina]);

scatter(vsi_vogali(:,1), vsi_vogali(:,2), 'r', 'filled'); % označimo vse vogale
title('Vsi vogali kvadratov na poravnani sliki');
hold off; % zaključimo risanje čez sliko

image(A);

%set(gca,'YDir','reverse');

%% Tretja naloga:
A = imread('Image_rect15.tif');
A = repmat(A,[1,1,3]);

f1 = figure;
image(A);
%axis image;
axis off;
%set(gca,'YDir','reverse');
hold on;
calib = load('/home/risbo/Dokumenti/RV_vaje/RV_LV_2/Calib_Results.mat', 'cc'); % središčno točko slike naložimo iz kalibracijskih rezultatov prve naloge
scatter(calib.cc(1), calib.cc(2), 'filled'); 
legend('Optični center');

st_tock = 12; % število točk za vsako vertikalno linijo
uiwait(msgbox('Označi eno vertikalno linijo oglišč na robu šahovnice (12 oglišč).', 'Navodila', 'modal'));
for i = 1:st_tock
    [xe(i), ye(i)] = ginput(1); % vrne vektor x in y koordinat klikov
    scatter(xe(i), ye(i), 500, 'red', 'x', 'LineWidth', 2);
    drawnow; % osveži prikaz takoj
end
uiwait(msgbox('Označi vertikalno linijo oglišč najbližje sredini slike (12 oglišč).', 'Navodila', 'modal'));
for i = 1:st_tock
    [xc(i), yc(i)] = ginput(1); % vrne vektor x in y koordinat klikov
    scatter(xc(i), yc(i), 500, 'green', 'x', 'LineWidth', 2);
    drawnow; % osveži prikaz takoj
end

% Naredimo fit premice na točke samo za prikaz na sliki:
edge_line = fitline([xe; ye]);
center_line = fitline([xc; yc]);

hold on;
% Risanje premice za robne točke:
fplot(@(x) -edge_line(1)/edge_line(2)*x - edge_line(3)/edge_line(2), [0, size(A,2)], 'r', 'LineWidth', 1.5);
ylim([0, size(A,1)]);

% Risanje premice za centralne točke:
fplot(@(x) -center_line(1)/center_line(2)*x - center_line(3)/center_line(2), [0, size(A,2)], 'b', 'LineWidth', 1.5);
ylim([0, size(A,1)]);

% Premik koordinat v center slike:
xe_c = xe - calib.cc(1);
ye_c = ye - calib.cc(2);
xc_c = xc - calib.cc(1);
yc_c = yc - calib.cc(2);

% Fitanje premic v koordinatnem sistemu s središčem v optičnem centru:
[edge_line, edge_dist] = fitline([xe_c(:)'; ye_c(:)']);  
[center_line, center_dist] = fitline([xc_c; yc_c]);

% Izračun skupne napake:
err(1,1) = sqrt(sum(edge_dist.^2));         
err(1,2) = sqrt(sum(center_dist.^2));
err(1,3) = err(1,1) + err(1,2);

% Definicija vektorja parametra f:
f = 400:20:800;

r_e = zeros(st_tock, 1); % radiji robnih točk (edge points), računani od optičnega središča
r_e_cor = zeros(size(f,2), st_tock, 1); % korigirani radiji robnih točk
% Za vsako možno vrednost f bomo shranili nov niz popravljenih radijev r_e_cor.
% Na koncu bomo to uporabili za iskanje optimalnega f, ki manjša napako.

theta_e = zeros(st_tock, 1);
% theta_e bodo koti (v radianih) vsake robne točke glede na optično središče.
% Z njimi bomo kasneje rekonstruirali položaje točk po korekciji (v polarni obliki).

% Pretvorba iz kartezičnih koordinat v polarne koordinate:
r_c = zeros(st_tock, 1);
theta_c = zeros(st_tock, 1);

for i = 1 : st_tock
    r_e(i) = sqrt(xe_c(i)^2 + ye_c(i)^2);
    theta_e(i) = atan2(ye_c(i), xe_c(i));

    r_c(i) = sqrt(xc_c(i)^2 + yc_c(i)^2);
    theta_c(i) = atan2(yc_c(i), xc_c(i));
end

% Izračun korigiranih radijev vseh točk in napake za vsak definiran f:
r_c_cor = zeros(size(f, 2), st_tock, 1);

for j = 1 : size(f, 2) % size(f,2) je dolžina vektorja f, v tem primeru 21 različnih vrednosti.
    for i = 1 : st_tock % Gre čez vse točke, ki smo jih predhodno pretvorili v polarne koordinate (r_e(i) in r_c(i)).
        r_e_cor(j, i) = -f(j)/2 * (exp(-2*r_e(i)/f(j)) - 1) / (exp(-r_e(i)/f(j)));
        r_c_cor(j, i) = -f(j)/2 * (exp(-2*r_c(i)/f(j))-1)/(exp(-r_c(i)/f(j)));
    end

    % Pretvorba nazaj iz polarnih v kartezicne koordinate:
    [x_novi_tmp, y_novi_tmp] = pol2cart(theta_e, r_e_cor(j,:));
    
    % Prileganje premice skozi popravljene robne točke:
    [edge_line, edge_dist] = fitline([x_novi_tmp(:)'; y_novi_tmp(:)']);
    
    % Izračun napake prileganja:
    err(j+1, 1) = sqrt(sum(edge_dist.^2));
    
    % Isto za središčne točke:
    [x_novi_tmp, y_novi_tmp] = pol2cart(theta_c, r_c_cor(j,:));
    [edge_line, edge_dist] = fitline([x_novi_tmp(:)'; y_novi_tmp(:)']);
    err(j+1, 2) = sqrt(sum(edge_dist.^2));
    
    % Skupna napaka za obe liniji:
    err(j+1, 3) = err(j+1, 1) + err(j+1, 2);
end

% Prikaz izračunane napake glede na f:
figure;
plot(f, err(2:end,1), f, err(2:end,2), f, err(2:end,3));
% Zakaj 2:end?
%Ker je bila prva vrstica v err(1,:) rezervirana za izvorno, nekorigirano napako (brez popravka f = 0).
xlabel('f')
ylabel('error')
legend('error - edge','error - center', 'error - sum')

% Prikaz robnih točk pri različnih f:
figure;
polarscatter(theta_e, r_e);
hold on;
polarscatter(theta_e, r_e_cor(:,:));
legend(['original', string(f)]);
hold off;

% Nove korigirane vrednosti:
[minimum, min_index] = min(err(2:end,3));
figure(f1);
hold on;
[x_novi_tmp, y_novi_tmp] = pol2cart(theta_e, r_e_cor(min_index - 1,:)');
x_novi_tmp = x_novi_tmp + calib.cc(1);
y_novi_tmp = y_novi_tmp + calib.cc(2);
scatter(x_novi_tmp, y_novi_tmp, 500, 'yellow', 'x');
[x_novi_tmp, y_novi_tmp]=pol2cart(theta_c, r_c_cor(min_index - 1,:)');
x_novi_tmp = x_novi_tmp + calib.cc(1);
y_novi_tmp = y_novi_tmp + calib.cc(2);
scatter(x_novi_tmp, y_novi_tmp, 500, 'blue', 'x');
legend('optical center', 'uncorrected edge points (red)', 'uncorrected center points (green)', '', '', 'corrected edge points (yellow)', 'corrected center points (blue)');
hold off;

==== BEGIN Domen Hauko | Domen Hauko_18900_assignsubmission_file | nonlin_calib.m ====
function nonlin_calib = nonlin_calib(f, corners_edge, corners_middle, principal_point)
% Izračuna vsoto kvadratov napak za dano vrednost f.
% Uporablja enačbo: r' = (f/2)*(exp(r/f) - exp(-r/f)) = f*sinh(r/f)
% in metodo phi–cos/sin za popravljanje koordinat.
    
    % center
    cx = principal_point(1);
    cy = principal_point(2); 

    % x,y od principal point do tock na robu
    dx_e = corners_edge(:,1)   - cx;   
    dy_e = corners_edge(:,2)   - cy;
    % x,y od principal point do tock na sredini
    dx_m = corners_middle(:,1) - cx;   
    dy_m = corners_middle(:,2) - cy;
    
    % radij do tock na robu
    r_e  = sqrt(dx_e.^2 + dy_e.^2);  
    % phi za tocke na robu
    phi_e = atan2(dy_e, dx_e);

    % radij do tock na sredini
    r_m  = sqrt(dx_m.^2 + dy_m.^2);  
    % phi do tock na sredini
    phi_m = atan2(dy_m, dx_m);

    % corrected distortion (po formuli)
    rl_e = (f/2) * (exp(r_e./f) - exp(-r_e./f));
    rl_m = (f/2) * (exp(r_m./f) - exp(-r_m./f));

    % razlika v dolzini ko je r popravljen
    dr_e = rl_e - r_e; % za robne piksle
    dr_m = rl_m - r_m; % za sredinske piksle

    
    % robne tocke
    x_e_corr = corners_edge(:,1)   + dr_e .* cos(phi_e); % x za kolikor se mora premakniti originalno tocko
    y_e_corr = corners_edge(:,2)   + dr_e .* sin(phi_e); % y za kolikor se mora premakniti originalno tocko
    % sredinske tocke
    x_m_corr = corners_middle(:,1) + dr_m .* cos(phi_m); % x za kolikor se mora premakniti originalno tocko
    y_m_corr = corners_middle(:,2) + dr_m .* sin(phi_m); % y za kolikor se mora premakniti originalno tocko

    [~, dist_e] = fitline([x_e_corr'; y_e_corr']);
    [~, dist_m] = fitline([x_m_corr'; y_m_corr']);
    nonlin_calib = sum(dist_e.^2) + sum(dist_m.^2);

end
==== END Domen Hauko | Domen Hauko_18900_assignsubmission_file | nonlin_calib.m ====

%% 2.

img = imread("Image_rect13.tif");

figure;
imshow(img);
%title("Izberi 4 točke, 12x11 kvadratov. (Zgoraj levo -> Zgoraj desno -> Spodaj desno -> Spodaj levo)");
%corners = ginput(4);
%poklikane tocke
corners = [
    [143.8017,   70.1774];
    [549.1148,  111.1641];
    [467.1414,  398.9820];
    [138.3368,  389.8738]  
];

Nx = 12; % št. kvadratov po x (vodoravno)
Ny = 11; % št. kvadratov po y (navpično)
sq = 30; % velikost kvadrata

W = Nx * sq;    % širina slike mreže
H = Ny * sq;

corners_transformed = [1, 1; W, 1; W, H; 1, H];


T = maketform('projective', corners, corners_transformed);
T_inv = maketform('projective', corners_transformed, corners);

transformed = imtransform(img, T, 'XData', [1 W], 'YData', [1 H]);
figure;
imshow(transformed);
title("transformirana šahovnica");

x_ogl = [];
y_ogl = [];
for x = 1:sq:W+1
    for y = 1:sq:H+1
        [x_orig, y_orig] = tformfwd(T_inv, x, y);
        x_ogl = [x_ogl; x_orig]; % doda na konec
        y_ogl = [y_ogl; y_orig];
    end
end

% druga moznost
%[xg, yg] = meshgrid(1:sq:W+1, 1:sq:H+1);   % (Nx+1) x (Ny+1) oglišč
%[x_ogl, y_ogl] = tformfwd(T_inv, xg(:), yg(:));

figure; 
imshow(img, []); hold on;
plot(x_ogl, y_ogl, 'r.', 'MarkerSize', 12);
title("Označena oglišča na originalni sliki");

% Y = -c(1)*x + c(3)/c(2)
figure;
imshow(img); hold on;
plot(xlim, -(c_edge(1)*xlim + c_edge(3))/c_edge(2), 'r-', 'LineWidth', 1.5);
plot(xlim, -(c_middle(1)*xlim + c_middle(3))/c_middle(2), 'g-', 'LineWidth', 1.5);


principal_point = [ 319.50000   239.50000 ];
center_x = 319.50000;
center_y = 239.50000;
r_edge   = sqrt( (corners_edge(:,1)   - center_x).^2 + (corners_edge(:,2)   - center_y).^2 );
r_middle = sqrt( (corners_middle(:,1) - center_x).^2 + (corners_middle(:,2) - center_y).^2 );

SSE_total = [];
for f = 400:20:800
    corrected_edge_x = [];
    corrected_edge_y = [];
    corrected_middle_x = [];
    corrected_middle_y = [];
    for i = 1 : size(r_edge,1)
        % robni piksli
        x_edge = corners_edge(i,1); % x tocke
        y_edge = corners_edge(i,2); % y tocke
        x_pp_edge = x_edge - center_x; % x od tocke do principal point
        y_pp_edge = y_edge - center_y; % y od tocke do pp
        phi_edge = atan2(y_pp_edge, x_pp_edge); % kot med vodoravno osjo in r
        r_dist_edge = r_edge(i,:); % calculated radial distortion for distorted image
       
        rl_edge = (-f/2) * (exp(-2*r_dist_edge/f) -1) / exp(-r_dist_edge/f); % corrected distortion (po formuli)
        
        dr_edge = rl_edge - r_dist_edge; % razlika v dolzini ko je r popravljen
        dx_edge = dr_edge * cos(phi_edge); % x za kolikor se mora premakniti originalno tocko
        dy_edge = dr_edge * sin(phi_edge); % y za kolikor se mora premakniti originalno tocko

        % shrani novo tocko
        corrected_edge_x = [corrected_edge_x ; x_edge + dx_edge]; 
        corrected_edge_y = [corrected_edge_y ; y_edge + dy_edge];

        % sredinski piksli (enak postopek)
        x_middle = corners_middle(i,1);
        y_middle = corners_middle(i,2);
        x_pp_middle = x_middle - center_x; % x od tocke do principal point
        y_pp_middle = y_middle - center_y; % y od tocke do pp
        phi_middle = atan2(y_pp_middle, x_pp_middle);
        r_dist_middle = r_middle(i,:); % calculated radial distortion for distorted image
       
        rl_middle = (-f/2) * (exp(-2*r_dist_middle/f) -1) / exp(-r_dist_middle/f); %corrected distortion

        dr_middle = rl_middle - r_dist_middle;
        dx_middle = dr_middle * cos(phi_middle);
        dy_middle = dr_middle * sin(phi_middle);


        corrected_middle_x = [corrected_middle_x ; x_middle + dx_middle];
        corrected_middle_y = [corrected_middle_y ; y_middle + dy_middle];
    end
    % izracunane razdalje od premice ki je fittana po novih tockah
    [~, dist_e] = fitline([corrected_edge_x'; corrected_edge_y']);
    [~, dist_m] = fitline([corrected_middle_x';  corrected_middle_y']);
    SSE_total = [SSE_total ; sum(dist_e.^2) + sum(dist_m.^2)]; % vsota kvadratnih napak za popravljene tocke
end

f_values  = (400:20:800).';
figure; 
plot(f_values, SSE_total, '-o', 'LineWidth', 1.5);
grid on; 
xlabel('f'); ylabel('SSE');
title('error');


f0 = 500;
f_opt = fminsearch(@(f) nonlin_calib(f, corners_edge, corners_middle, principal_point), f0);
f_opt


%% DOC za fitline.m
% Usage:   [C, dist] = fitline(XY)
%
% Where:   XY  - 2xNpts array of xy coordinates to fit line to data of
%                the form 
%                [x1 x2 x3 ... xN
%                 y1 y2 y3 ... yN]
%                
%                XY can also be a 3xNpts array of homogeneous coordinates.
%
% Returns: C    - 3x1 array of line coefficients in the form
%                 c(1)*X + c(2)*Y + c(3) = 0
%          dist - Array of distances from the fitted line to the supplied
%                 data points.  Note that dist is only calculated if the
%                 function is called with two output arguments.
%
% The magnitude of C is scaled so that line equation corresponds to
%   sin(theta)*X + (-cos(theta))*Y + rho = 0
% where theta is the angle between the line and the x axis and rho is the
% perpendicular distance from the origin to the line.  Rescaling the
% coefficients in this manner allows the perpendicular distance from any
% point (x,y) to the line to be simply calculated as
%   r = abs(c(1)*X + c(2)*Y + c(3))
%
% If you want to convert this line representation to the classical form 
%     Y = a*X + b
% use
%  a = -c(1)/c(2)
%  b = -c(3)/c(2)
%
% Note, however, that this assumes c(2) is not zero
==== END Domen Hauko | Domen Hauko_18900_assignsubmission_file | vaja2.m ====

    % Definiraj idealnu geometriju ploce
    %12 kvadrata po sirini, 11 po visini, mreza uglova je (13 x 12)
    s  = 30;
    nx = 12;
    ny = 11;

    % 4 ugla idealne (ispravljene) ploce
    ip = [0 0; s*nx 0; s*nx s*ny; 0 s*ny];

    T = maketform('projective', k, ip);

    %Ispravljena slika
    I_rect = imtransform(I, T, 'XData',[0 s*nx],'YData',[0 s*ny]);
    figure; imshow(I_rect,[]); axis image;
    title('Projektivno ispravljena sahovnica');

    %Svi uglovi mreze u idealnom prostoru
    [U,V] = meshgrid(0:nx, 0:ny);
    ideal_pts = [U(:)*s, V(:)*s];

    %Inverzna transformacija: idealno -> original
    T_inv = maketform('projective', ip, k);
    [xo, yo] = tformfwd(T_inv, ideal_pts(:,1), ideal_pts(:,2));

    % Nacrtaj sve uglove na originalnoj slici
    figure; imshow(I,[]); axis image; hold on;
    plot(xo, yo, 'r+', 'MarkerSize',5, 'LineWidth',1);

==== BEGIN Dunja Mihajlović | Dunja Mihajlović_18901_assignsubmission_file | radialna_distorzija.m ====
img = imread('Image15.tif');

if size(img, 3) == 3
    img = rgb2gray(img);
end
img = im2double(img);

 
disp('Označite 6 tacaka na rubu slike.');
figure();
imshow(img);
[x1,y1] = ginput(6);
close();

disp('Označi 6 tacaka blizu centra.');
figure();
imshow(img);
[x2,y2] = ginput(6);
close();

%Centar slike
[height, width] = size(img);  % Za sivu sliku, visina i širina
x = width / 2; 
y = height / 2;

% Koordinate tačaka sa ivice
xy1 = zeros(2, 6);
for i = 1:6
    xy1(1,i) = x1(i) - x;  % Premesti X-koordinate
    xy1(2,i) = y1(i) - y;  % Premesti Y-koordinate
end

% Koordinate tačaka sa centra
xy2 = zeros(2, 6);
for i = 1:6
    xy2(1,i) = x2(i) - x;  % Premesti X-koordinate
    xy2(2,i) = y2(i) - y;  % Premesti Y-koordinate
end

    
[c1,d1] = fitline(xy1);
[c2,d2] = fitline(xy2);

D_1 = sum(d1 .^ 2);
D_2 = sum(d2 .^ 2);

%Polarne koordinate
r_rub = zeros(1,6);
r_centar = zeros(1,6);
ugao_rob = zeros(1,6);
ugao_centar = zeros(1,6);

[ugao_rob, r_rub] = cart2pol(xy1(1,:), xy1(2,:));
[ugao_centar, r_centar]= cart2pol(xy2(1,:), xy2(2,:));

f_vals = 400:20:800;
disp(length(f_vals));

rn_rob = zeros(1,6);
xy1_n = zeros(2,6);
r_n_centar = zeros(1,6);
xy2_n = zeros(2,6);

%i=1; %brojac
%Greske
D1 = zeros(1, length(f_vals));
D2 = zeros(1, length(f_vals));

for i = 1:length(f_vals)
    f=f_vals(i);
    rn_rob = (- f / 2) .* (exp(-2 * r_rub ./ f) - 1) ./ exp(-r_rub / f);
    r_n_centar = (- f / 2) .* (exp(-2 * r_centar ./ f) - 1) ./ exp(-r_centar / f);

    [xy1_n(1,:),xy1_n(2,:)] = pol2cart(ugao_rob,rn_rob);
    [xy2_n(1,:),xy2_n(2,:)] = pol2cart(ugao_centar,r_n_centar); 

    [c1n, d1n] = fitline(xy1_n);
    [c2n, d2n] = fitline(xy2_n);
    
    D_1n = sum(d1n .^2);
    D1(i) = D_1n;
    D_2n = sum(d2n .^2);
    D2(i) = D_2n;
end

Dsum = D1 + D2;
[minsweep, idxmin] = min(Dsum);
f_min = f_vals(idxmin);

figure; hold on; grid on;
f = 400:20:800;
plot(f, D1, 'g-', f, D2, 'b-', f, Dsum, 'r-', 'LineWidth', 2);

%Oznacen minimum
plot(f_min, minsweep, 'ko', 'MarkerSize', 8, 'MarkerFaceColor', 'y');
xline(f_min, '--k');
xlabel('Vrednosti parametra f');
ylabel('Suma kvadrata udaljenosti');
legend('Rub','Centar','Zbir','Min(Zbir)');

fprintf('Minimum pri f_min = %.1f\n', f_min);



% Optimizacija
f0=400;
cos_function = @(f)cost_fcn(f, ugao_rob,r_rub, ugao_centar, r_centar); %cos radijalne distorzije
[f_opt,e] = fminsearch(cos_function,f0); 
fprintf('Najboljše korigirana radijalna distrozija je pri f = %d',round(f_opt))

%% RV2_VSE_NALOGE
close all; clear; clc;

%% ---- nastavitve slik ----
projImgName = 'Image_rect6.tif';   % 2. naloga -> prilagodi po potrebi
radImgName  = 'Image15.tif';   % 3. in 4. naloga

%% ---- središče distorzije iz Calib_Results.mat ----
R = load('Calib_Results.mat');

cx = R.cc(1); cy = R.cc(2);

%% ===================== 2. NALOGA: PROJEKTIVNA =====================
Ip = imread(projImgName);

figure; imshow(Ip,[]); axis image;
title({'2. naloga: klikni 4 vogale šahovnice v zaporedju:', ...
       '1: levo zgoraj, 2: desno zgoraj, 3: desno spodaj, 4: levo spodaj'});
[xv, yv] = ginput(4);
moving = [xv yv];

% prilagodi glede na svojo šahovnico:
numSquaresX = 12;
numSquaresY = 11;
squareSize  = 30;
W = numSquaresX * squareSize;
H = numSquaresY * squareSize;

fixed = [0 0; W 0; W H; 0 H];

T = maketform('projective', moving, fixed);
[J, ~, ~] = imtransform(Ip, T, 'XData',[0 W], 'YData',[0 H]);

figure; imshow(J,[]); axis image;
title('2. naloga: korigirana (projektivna) slika');

% oglišča v idealni mreži + preslikava nazaj na original
[Xg, Yg] = meshgrid(0:squareSize:W, 0:squareSize:H);
ideal_pts = [Xg(:) Yg(:)];
invT = maketform('projective', fixed, moving);
[xg, yg] = tformfwd(invT, ideal_pts(:,1), ideal_pts(:,2));

figure; imshow(Ip,[]); axis image; hold on;
plot(xg, yg, 'r.', 'MarkerSize', 6);
title('2. naloga: oglišča šahovnice na originalni sliki');


%% ========== 3. & 4. NALOGA: RADIALNA DISTORZIJA (Image15) ==========

Ir = imread(radImgName);

figure; imshow(Ir,[]); axis image;
title({'3./4. naloga:', ...
       'Najprej klikni ROBNO vertikalno vrsto oglišč (Enter za konec),', ...
       'nato SREDINSKO vertikalno vrsto (Enter za konec).'});

disp('3./4. naloga: robna vrsta, Enter za konec.');
[x1, y1] = ginput;
disp('3./4. naloga: sredinska vrsta, Enter za konec.');
[x2, y2] = ginput;
close;

%% ---- 3. naloga: diskretno iskanje f v [400:20:800] ----

f_vals = 400:20:800;
E_vals = zeros(size(f_vals));

for k = 1:length(f_vals)
    E_vals(k) = radial_error(f_vals(k), x1,y1,x2,y2, cx,cy);
end

[Emin, idxMin] = min(E_vals);
f_best_grid = f_vals(idxMin);

fprintf('3. naloga: najboljši f (diskretno) = %.4f, napaka = %.4f\n', ...
        f_best_grid, Emin);

figure;
plot(f_vals, E_vals, '-o'); grid on;
xlabel('f'); ylabel('Vsota kvadratov razdalj');
title('3. naloga: napaka glede na f (diskretno)');
    


%% ---- 4. naloga: nelinearno iskanje f z fminsearch ----

f0 = f_best_grid;                 % začnemo pri diskretnem minimumu
opts = optimset('Display','off'); % minimalno, brez spama v konzoli

[f_best_cont, E_best_cont] = fminsearch( ...
    @(f) radial_error(f, x1,y1,x2,y2, cx,cy), f0, opts);

fprintf('4. naloga: optimalni f (fminsearch) = %.6f, napaka = %.6f\n', ...
        f_best_cont, E_best_cont);

% (neobvezno, a pregledno) lokalni prikaz okoli optimuma
f_loc = linspace(max(100, f_best_cont-200), min(1000, f_best_cont+200), 30);
E_loc = arrayfun(@(f) radial_error(f, x1,y1,x2,y2, cx,cy), f_loc);

figure;
plot(f_loc, E_loc, '-o'); grid on;
xlabel('f'); ylabel('Vsota kvadratov razdalj');
title('4. naloga: napaka okoli optimalnega f');


%% ===== LOKALNE FUNKCIJE (OSTANEJO V ISTEM .m, TO JE ŠE VEDNO SKRIPTA) =====

function E = radial_error(f, x1,y1,x2,y2, cx,cy)
    % Napaka za dano f: vsota kvadratov razdalj obeh stolpcev do svojih premic
    if f <= 0
        E = 1e12;
        return;
    end

    [xu1, yu1] = undistort_points(x1, y1, cx, cy, f);
    [xu2, yu2] = undistort_points(x2, y2, cx, cy, f);

    [~, d1] = fitline([xu1.'; yu1.']);   % 2xN
    [~, d2] = fitline([xu2.'; yu2.']);

    E = sum(d1.^2) + sum(d2.^2);
end


function [xu, yu] = undistort_points(x, y, cx, cy, f)
    % Inverz radialne distorzije:
    % r' -> r_l = f * sinh(r'/f)

    dx = x - cx;
    dy = y - cy;

    rp = sqrt(dx.^2 + dy.^2);       % popačen radij
    rl = f .* sinh(rp ./ f);        % nepopačen radij

    scale = ones(size(rp));
    mask = (rp > 0);
    scale(mask) = rl(mask) ./ rp(mask);

    xu = cx + dx .* scale;
    yu = cy + dy .* scale;
end
==== END Filip Kocjančič | Filip Kocjančič_18915_assignsubmission_file | rv2_vse_naloge.m ====


==== BEGIN Gašper Lončar | Gašper Lončar_18906_assignsubmission_file | labvaja2.m ====
clc; close all;

slika15 = imread("Image15.tif");

if size(slika15,3) == 3
    slika15 = rgb2gray(slika15);
end

[visina, sirina] = size(slika15);
% optično središče iz 1. naloge
v_sredina = 319.5;        
s_sredina = 239.5;

[x, y] = meshgrid(1:sirina, 1:visina);

% figure; image(slika15); axis image; title('označi središčno premico')
% [x_sred,y_sred] = ginput();
% 
% figure; image(slika15); axis image; title('označi premico najbolj na levem robu');
% [x_rob_levo,y_rob_levo] = ginput();
% 
% figure; image(slika15); axis image; title('označi premico najbolj na desnem robu');
% [x_rob_desno,y_rob_desno] = ginput();

% tabela s točkami
sred_tocke = [x_sred'; y_sred'];
rob_tocke_levo = [x_rob_levo'; y_rob_levo'];
rob_tocke_desno = [x_rob_desno'; y_rob_desno'];

% koeficienti premice a*x + b*y + c = 0, vektor razdalj vsake točke od te premice
[sredina, dolzina_sred] = fitline(sred_tocke);
[rob_levo, dolzina_rob_levo] = fitline(rob_tocke_levo);
[rob_desno, dolzina_rob_desno] = fitline(rob_tocke_desno);

% vsota kvadratov
vs_sred = sum(dolzina_sred.^2); 
vs_rob_levo = sum(dolzina_rob_levo.^2);
vs_rob_desno = sum(dolzina_rob_desno.^2);

% izračun popačenih radijev
r0_sred = sqrt((x_sred - s_sredina).^2 + (y_sred - v_sredina).^2);
r0_rob_levo = sqrt((x_rob_levo - s_sredina).^2 + (y_rob_levo - v_sredina).^2);
r0_rob_desno = sqrt((x_rob_desno - s_sredina).^2 + (y_rob_desno - v_sredina).^2);

f_interval = 400:20:800;
vsota_kvadratov = zeros(size(f_interval));

for i = 1:length(f_interval)
    f = f_interval(i);
    
    % nov radij po inverzni formuli
    r1_sred = -f/2 .* ((exp(-2*r0_sred./f) - 1) ./ exp(-r0_sred./f));
    r1_rob_levo = -f/2 .* ((exp(-2*r0_rob_levo./f) - 1) ./ exp(-r0_rob_levo./f));
    r1_rob_desno = -f/2 .* ((exp(-2*r0_rob_desno./f) - 1) ./ exp(-r0_rob_desno./f));


    % normalizacija x-os ()horizontalno z r1/r0
    k_sred = r1_sred ./ r0_sred;
    k_rob_levo = r1_rob_levo ./ r0_rob_levo;
    k_rob_desno = r1_rob_desno ./ r0_rob_desno;

    % nove koordinate obeh premic
    x_nov_sred = s_sredina + (x_sred - s_sredina) .* k_sred;
    y_nov_sred = v_sredina + (y_sred - v_sredina) .* k_sred;

    x_nov_rob_levo = s_sredina + (x_rob_levo - s_sredina) .* k_rob_levo;
    y_nov_rob_levo = v_sredina + (y_rob_levo - v_sredina) .* k_rob_levo;

    x_nov_rob_desno = s_sredina + (x_rob_desno - s_sredina) .* k_rob_desno;
    y_nov_rob_desno = v_sredina + (y_rob_desno - v_sredina) .* k_rob_desno;

    [~, razdalja_nov_sred] = fitline([x_nov_sred'; y_nov_sred']);
    [~, razdalja_nov_rob_levo] = fitline([x_nov_rob_levo'; y_nov_rob_levo']);
    [~, razdalja_nov_rob_desno] = fitline([x_nov_rob_desno'; y_nov_rob_desno']);

    % vsota kvadratov po korekciji
    vk_sred(i) = sum(razdalja_nov_sred.^2);
    vk_levo(i) = sum(razdalja_nov_rob_levo.^2);
    vk_desno(i) = sum(razdalja_nov_rob_desno.^2);
    vsota_kvadratov(i) = vk_sred(i) + vk_levo(i) + vk_desno(i);
    % fprintf('f=%4d, vsota sred=%.4f, vsota levo=%.4f, vsota desno=%.4f, skupna vsota=%.4f\n', ...
    %     f, vk_sred, vk_levo, vk_desno, vsota_kvadratov);
end

% prikaz vsote kvadratov glede na f - parameter distorzije
figure;
plot(f_interval, vsota_kvadratov);
xlabel('f (parameter distorzije)');
ylabel('Vsota kvadratov razdalj');

% Poiščemo optimalni f
[~, f_opt] = min(vsota_kvadratov);
f_optimal = f_interval(f_opt);
disp(['Optimalna vrednost parametra f = ', num2str(f_optimal)]);

%% Shrani podatke za 4. nalogo
save('radial_data.mat', 'x_rob_desno','y_rob_desno','x_sred', 'y_sred', 'x_rob_levo', 'y_rob_levo', ...
    'r0_sred', 'r0_rob_levo', 'r0_rob_desno','s_sredina', 'v_sredina', ...
    'slika15');
disp('Podatki za 4. nalogo uspešno shranjeni v radial_data.mat');

%% prikaz z odpravljeno distorzijo

f = f_optimal;

r0 = sqrt((x - s_sredina).^2 + (y - v_sredina).^2);
% iz nepopačenih nazaj v popačene
r1 = f .* log( r0./f + sqrt( 1 + (r0./f).^2 ) );
K = r1 ./ r0;

x_korig = (x - s_sredina).*K + s_sredina;
y_korig = (y - v_sredina).*K + v_sredina;

x_korig = min(max(x_korig, 1), sirina);
y_korig = min(max(y_korig, 1), visina);

% uporabi interp2 za preoblikovanje slike
korig = double(slika15);
korigirana_slika = interp2(korig, x_korig, y_korig, 'linear', 0);

figure;
subplot(1,2,1);
imshow(slika15, []); title('Originalna slika');

subplot(1,2,2);
imshow(uint8(korigirana_slika), 'XData', [1 sirina], 'YData', [1 visina]); 
title('Korigirana slika optimal pri:', num2str(f_optimal, '%.2f'));

%% direktno z fminsearch

f_zacetni = 400;

obj_fun = @(f) ...
    local_vsota_kvadratov(f, ...
    x_sred, y_sred, r0_sred, ...
    x_rob_levo,  y_rob_levo,  r0_rob_levo, ...
    x_rob_desno, y_rob_desno, r0_rob_desno, ...
    s_sredina, v_sredina);

% izvedemo optimizacijo
f_opt_nelinearno = fminsearch(obj_fun, f_zacetni);

fprintf('Optimalna vrednost parametra f (nelinearna optimizacija): %.2f\n', f_opt_nelinearno);

%%
function vsota = local_vsota_kvadratov(f, ...
    x_sred, y_sred, r0_sred, ...
    x_robL, y_robL, r0_robL, ...
    x_robD, y_robD, r0_robD, ...
    s_sredina, v_sredina)

    % izračun korekcije za središčno linijo
    r1_sred = -f/2 .* ((exp(-2*r0_sred./f) - 1) ./ exp(-r0_sred./f));
    r1_robL = -f/2 .* ((exp(-2*r0_robL./f) - 1) ./ exp(-r0_robL./f));
    r1_robD = -f/2 .* ((exp(-2*r0_robD./f) - 1) ./ exp(-r0_robD./f));

    k_sred = r1_sred ./ r0_sred;
    k_robL = r1_robL ./ r0_robL;
    k_robD = r1_robD ./ r0_robD;

    x_nov_sred = s_sredina + (x_sred - s_sredina) .* k_sred;
    y_nov_sred = v_sredina + (y_sred - v_sredina) .* k_sred;

    x_nov_robL = s_sredina + (x_robL - s_sredina) .* k_robL;
    y_nov_robL = v_sredina + (y_robL - v_sredina) .* k_robL;

    x_nov_robD = s_sredina + (x_robD - s_sredina) .* k_robD;
    y_nov_robD = v_sredina + (y_robD - v_sredina) .* k_robD;

    % prileganje premic in vsota kvadratov razdalj
    if numel(x_nov_sred) < 3 || numel(x_nov_robL) < 3 || numel(x_nov_robD) < 3
        vsota = 1e9; % če je premalo točk, vrni veliko napako
        return;
    end

    [~, raz_sred] = fitline([x_nov_sred'; y_nov_sred']);
    [~, raz_robL]  = fitline([x_nov_robL';  y_nov_robL']);
    [~, raz_robD]  = fitline([x_nov_robD';  y_nov_robD']);
    vsota = sum(raz_sred.^2) + sum(raz_robL.^2) + sum(raz_robD.^2);
end
