==== BEGIN Gregor Turk | Gregor Turk_18905_assignsubmission_file | fitline.m ====
% FITLINE - Least squares fit of a line to a set of points
%
% Usage:   [C, dist] = fitline(XY)
%
% Where:   XY  - 2xNpts array of xy coordinates to fit line to data of
%                the form 
%                [x1 x2 x3 ... xN
%                 y1 y2 y3 ... yN]
%                
%                XY can also be a 3xNpts array of homogeneous coordinates.
%
% Returns: C    - 3x1 array of line coefficients in the form
%                 c(1)*X + c(2)*Y + c(3) = 0
%          dist - Array of distances from the fitted line to the supplied
%                 data points.  Note that dist is only calculated if the
%                 function is called with two output arguments.
%
% The magnitude of C is scaled so that line equation corresponds to
%   sin(theta)*X + (-cos(theta))*Y + rho = 0
% where theta is the angle between the line and the x axis and rho is the
% perpendicular distance from the origin to the line.  Rescaling the
% coefficients in this manner allows the perpendicular distance from any
% point (x,y) to the line to be simply calculated as
%   r = abs(c(1)*X + c(2)*Y + c(3))
%
% If you want to convert this line representation to the classical form 
%     Y = a*X + b
% use
%  a = -c(1)/c(2)
%  b = -c(3)/c(2)
%
% Note, however, that this assumes c(2) is not zero

% Copyright (c) 2003-2008 Peter Kovesi
% www.peterkovesi.com
% 
% Permission is hereby granted, free of charge, to any person obtaining a copy
% of this software and associated documentation files (the "Software"), to deal
% in the Software without restriction, subject to the following conditions:
% 
% The above copyright notice and this permission notice shall be included in 
% all copies or substantial portions of the Software.
%
% The Software is provided "as is", without warranty of any kind.

% June      2003 - Original version
% September 2004 - Rescaling to allow simple distance calculation.
% November  2008 - Normalising of coordinates added to condition the
%                  solution.
% December  2016 - Simplified rescaling of coefficients to allow line
%                  distances to be calculated


function [C, dist] = fitline(XY)
  
  [rows,npts] = size(XY);    

  if npts < 2
      error('Too few points to fit line');
  end  
  
  if rows ==2    % Add homogeneous scale coordinate of 1 
      XY = [XY; ones(1,npts)];
  end

  if npts == 2    % Pad XY with a third column of zeros
    XY = [XY zeros(3,1)]; 
  end
  
  % Normalise points so that centroid is at origin and mean distance from
  % origin is sqrt(2).  This conditions the equations. 
  [XYn, T] = normalise2dpts(XY);
  
  % Set up constraint equations of the form  XYn'*C = 0,
  % where C is a column vector of the line coefficients
  % in the form   c(1)*X + c(2)*Y + c(3) = 0.

  [u d v] = svd(XYn',0);   % Singular value decomposition.
  C = v(:,3);              % Solution is last column of v.

  % Denormalise the solution
  C = T'*C;
  
  % Rescale coefficients so that line equation corresponds to
  %   sin(theta)*X + (-cos(theta))*Y + rho = 0
  % so that the perpendicular distance from any point (x,y) to the line
  % to be simply calculated as 
  %   r = abs(c(1)*X + c(2)*Y + c(3))
  C = C / sqrt(C(1)^2 + C(2)^2);
  
  % If requested, calculate the distances from the fitted line to
  % the supplied data points 
  if nargout==2   
      dist = abs(C(1)*XY(1,:) + C(2)*XY(2,:) + C(3));
  end




  
       
       
==== END Gregor Turk | Gregor Turk_18905_assignsubmission_file | fitline.m ====

==== BEGIN Gregor Turk | Gregor Turk_18905_assignsubmission_file | nonlin_calib.m ====
function nonlin_calib = nonlin_calib(f, corners_edge, corners_middle, principal_point)
% Izračuna vsoto kvadratov napak za dano vrednost f.
% Uporablja enačbo: r' = (f/2)*(exp(r/f) - exp(-r/f)) = f*sinh(r/f)
% in metodo phi–cos/sin za popravljanje koordinat.
    
    % center
    cx = principal_point(1);
    cy = principal_point(2); 

    % x,y od principal point do tock na robu
    dx_e = corners_edge(:,1)   - cx;   
    dy_e = corners_edge(:,2)   - cy;
    % x,y od principal point do tock na sredini
    dx_m = corners_middle(:,1) - cx;   
    dy_m = corners_middle(:,2) - cy;
    
    % radij do tock na robu
    r_e  = sqrt(dx_e.^2 + dy_e.^2);  
    % phi za tocke na robu
    phi_e = atan2(dy_e, dx_e);

    % radij do tock na sredini
    r_m  = sqrt(dx_m.^2 + dy_m.^2);  
    % phi do tock na sredini
    phi_m = atan2(dy_m, dx_m);

    % corrected distortion (po formuli)
    rl_e = (f/2) * (exp(r_e./f) - exp(-r_e./f));
    rl_m = (f/2) * (exp(r_m./f) - exp(-r_m./f));

    % razlika v dolzini ko je r popravljen
    dr_e = rl_e - r_e; % za robne piksle
    dr_m = rl_m - r_m; % za sredinske piksle

    
    % robne tocke
    x_e_corr = corners_edge(:,1)   + dr_e .* cos(phi_e); % x za kolikor se mora premakniti originalno tocko
    y_e_corr = corners_edge(:,2)   + dr_e .* sin(phi_e); % y za kolikor se mora premakniti originalno tocko
    % sredinske tocke
    x_m_corr = corners_middle(:,1) + dr_m .* cos(phi_m); % x za kolikor se mora premakniti originalno tocko
    y_m_corr = corners_middle(:,2) + dr_m .* sin(phi_m); % y za kolikor se mora premakniti originalno tocko

    [~, dist_e] = fitline([x_e_corr'; y_e_corr']);
    [~, dist_m] = fitline([x_m_corr'; y_m_corr']);
    nonlin_calib = sum(dist_e.^2) + sum(dist_m.^2);

end
==== END Gregor Turk | Gregor Turk_18905_assignsubmission_file | nonlin_calib.m ====

==== BEGIN Gregor Turk | Gregor Turk_18905_assignsubmission_file | normalise2dpts.m ====
% NORMALISE2DPTS - normalises 2D homogeneous points
%
% Function translates and normalises a set of 2D homogeneous points 
% so that their centroid is at the origin and their mean distance from 
% the origin is sqrt(2).  This process typically improves the
% conditioning of any equations used to solve homographies, fundamental
% matrices etc.
%
% Usage:   [newpts, T] = normalise2dpts(pts)
%
% Argument:
%   pts -  3xN array of 2D homogeneous coordinates
%
% Returns:
%   newpts -  3xN array of transformed 2D homogeneous coordinates.  The
%             scaling parameter is normalised to 1 unless the point is at
%             infinity. 
%   T      -  The 3x3 transformation matrix, newpts = T*pts
%           
% If there are some points at infinity the normalisation transform
% is calculated using just the finite points.  Being a scaling and
% translating transform this will not affect the points at infinity.

% Peter Kovesi
% www.peterkovesi.com
%
% May 2003      - Original version
% February 2004 - Modified to deal with points at infinity.
% December 2008 - meandist calculation modified to work with Octave 3.0.1
%                 (thanks to Ron Parr)
% December 2016 - Disabled warning messgae for points at infinity.


function [newpts, T] = normalise2dpts(pts)

    if size(pts,1) ~= 3
        error('pts must be 3xN');
    end
    
    % Find the indices of the points that are not at infinity
    finiteind = find(abs(pts(3,:)) > eps);
    
%    if length(finiteind) ~= size(pts,2)
%        warning('Some points are at infinity');
%    end
    
    % For the finite points ensure homogeneous coords have scale of 1
    pts(1,finiteind) = pts(1,finiteind)./pts(3,finiteind);
    pts(2,finiteind) = pts(2,finiteind)./pts(3,finiteind);
    pts(3,finiteind) = 1;
    
    c = mean(pts(1:2,finiteind)')';            % Centroid of finite points
    newp(1,finiteind) = pts(1,finiteind)-c(1); % Shift origin to centroid.
    newp(2,finiteind) = pts(2,finiteind)-c(2);
    
    dist = sqrt(newp(1,finiteind).^2 + newp(2,finiteind).^2);
    meandist = mean(dist(:));  % Ensure dist is a column vector for Octave 3.0.1
    
    scale = sqrt(2)/meandist;
    
    T = [scale   0   -scale*c(1)
         0     scale -scale*c(2)
         0       0      1      ];
    
    newpts = T*pts;
    
    
    
==== END Gregor Turk | Gregor Turk_18905_assignsubmission_file | normalise2dpts.m ====

%% 2.

img = imread("Image_rect13.tif");

figure;
imshow(img);
%title("Izberi 4 točke, 12x11 kvadratov. (Zgoraj levo -> Zgoraj desno -> Spodaj desno -> Spodaj levo)");
%corners = ginput(4);
%poklikane tocke
corners = [
    [143.8017,   70.1774];
    [549.1148,  111.1641];
    [467.1414,  398.9820];
    [138.3368,  389.8738]  
];

Nx = 12; % št. kvadratov po x (vodoravno)
Ny = 11; % št. kvadratov po y (navpično)
sq = 30; % velikost kvadrata

W = Nx * sq;    % širina slike mreže
H = Ny * sq;

corners_transformed = [1, 1; W, 1; W, H; 1, H];


T = maketform('projective', corners, corners_transformed);
T_inv = maketform('projective', corners_transformed, corners);

    for y = 1:sq:H+1

        x_ogl = [x_ogl; x_orig]; % doda na konec
        y_ogl = [y_ogl; y_orig];

% figure;

% corners_edge = ginput(12);

% poklikane tocke
corners_edge = [
   [43.6120,   69.2666];
   [41.7903,  107.5209];
   [42.7011,  146.6860];
   [41.7903,  184.0294];
   [41.7903,  220.4620];
   [39.9687,  256.8947];
   [39.9687,  293.3273];
   [39.9687,  327.9383];
   [41.7903,  360.7277];
   [44.5228,  392.6063];
   [44.5228,  423.5740];
   [48.1660,  453.6309]
];

    [311.3918,   87.4829];
    [306.8378,  123.9156];
    [303.1945,  161.2590];
    [301.3729,  196.7808];
    [297.7296,  233.2135];

plot(corners_middle(:,1), corners_middle(:,2),'r.', 'MarkerSize', 12);

XY_edge   = [corners_edge(:,1)';   corners_edge(:,2)'];
XY_middle = [corners_middle(:,1)'; corners_middle(:,2)'];

[c_edge, dist_edge] = fitline(XY_edge);
[c_middle, dist_middle] = fitline(XY_middle);

% c(1)*X + c(2)*Y + c(3) = 0 (iz dokumentacije fitline.m)
% Y = -c(1)*x + c(3)/c(2)
figure;
imshow(img); hold on;
plot(xlim, -(c_edge(1)*xlim + c_edge(3))/c_edge(2), 'r-', 'LineWidth', 1.5);
plot(xlim, -(c_middle(1)*xlim + c_middle(3))/c_middle(2), 'g-', 'LineWidth', 1.5);


principal_point = [ 319.50000   239.50000 ];
center_x = 319.50000;
center_y = 239.50000;
r_edge   = sqrt( (corners_edge(:,1)   - center_x).^2 + (corners_edge(:,2)   - center_y).^2 );
r_middle = sqrt( (corners_middle(:,1) - center_x).^2 + (corners_middle(:,2) - center_y).^2 );

SSE_total = [];
for f = 400:20:800
    corrected_edge_x = [];
    corrected_edge_y = [];
    corrected_middle_x = [];
    corrected_middle_y = [];
    for i = 1 : size(r_edge,1)
        % robni piksli
        x_edge = corners_edge(i,1); % x tocke
        y_edge = corners_edge(i,2); % y tocke
        x_pp_edge = x_edge - center_x; % x od tocke do principal point
        y_pp_edge = y_edge - center_y; % y od tocke do pp
        phi_edge = atan2(y_pp_edge, x_pp_edge); % kot med vodoravno osjo in r
        r_dist_edge = r_edge(i,:); % calculated radial distortion for distorted image
       
        rl_edge = (-f/2) * (exp(-2*r_dist_edge/f) -1) / exp(-r_dist_edge/f); % corrected distortion (po formuli)
        
        dr_edge = rl_edge - r_dist_edge; % razlika v dolzini ko je r popravljen
        dx_edge = dr_edge * cos(phi_edge); % x za kolikor se mora premakniti originalno tocko
        dy_edge = dr_edge * sin(phi_edge); % y za kolikor se mora premakniti originalno tocko

        % shrani novo tocko
        corrected_edge_x = [corrected_edge_x ; x_edge + dx_edge]; 
        corrected_edge_y = [corrected_edge_y ; y_edge + dy_edge];

        % sredinski piksli (enak postopek)
        x_middle = corners_middle(i,1);
        y_middle = corners_middle(i,2);
        x_pp_middle = x_middle - center_x; % x od tocke do principal point
        y_pp_middle = y_middle - center_y; % y od tocke do pp
        phi_middle = atan2(y_pp_middle, x_pp_middle);
        r_dist_middle = r_middle(i,:); % calculated radial distortion for distorted image
       
        rl_middle = (-f/2) * (exp(-2*r_dist_middle/f) -1) / exp(-r_dist_middle/f); %corrected distortion

        dr_middle = rl_middle - r_dist_middle;
        dx_middle = dr_middle * cos(phi_middle);
        dy_middle = dr_middle * sin(phi_middle);


        corrected_middle_x = [corrected_middle_x ; x_middle + dx_middle];
        corrected_middle_y = [corrected_middle_y ; y_middle + dy_middle];
    end
    % izracunane razdalje od premice ki je fittana po novih tockah
    [~, dist_e] = fitline([corrected_edge_x'; corrected_edge_y']);
    [~, dist_m] = fitline([corrected_middle_x';  corrected_middle_y']);
    SSE_total = [SSE_total ; sum(dist_e.^2) + sum(dist_m.^2)]; % vsota kvadratnih napak za popravljene tocke
end

f_values  = (400:20:800).';
figure; 
plot(f_values, SSE_total, '-o', 'LineWidth', 1.5);
grid on; 
xlabel('f'); ylabel('SSE');
title('error');


f0 = 500;
f_opt = fminsearch(@(f) nonlin_calib(f, corners_edge, corners_middle, principal_point), f0);
f_opt


%% DOC za fitline.m
% Usage:   [C, dist] = fitline(XY)
%
% Where:   XY  - 2xNpts array of xy coordinates to fit line to data of
%                the form 
%                [x1 x2 x3 ... xN
%                 y1 y2 y3 ... yN]
%                
%                XY can also be a 3xNpts array of homogeneous coordinates.
%
% Returns: C    - 3x1 array of line coefficients in the form
%                 c(1)*X + c(2)*Y + c(3) = 0
%          dist - Array of distances from the fitted line to the supplied
%                 data points.  Note that dist is only calculated if the
%                 function is called with two output arguments.
%
% The magnitude of C is scaled so that line equation corresponds to
%   sin(theta)*X + (-cos(theta))*Y + rho = 0
% where theta is the angle between the line and the x axis and rho is the
% perpendicular distance from the origin to the line.  Rescaling the
% coefficients in this manner allows the perpendicular distance from any
% point (x,y) to the line to be simply calculated as
%   r = abs(c(1)*X + c(2)*Y + c(3))
%
% If you want to convert this line representation to the classical form 
%     Y = a*X + b
% use
%  a = -c(1)/c(2)
%  b = -c(3)/c(2)
%
% Note, however, that this assumes c(2) is not zero
==== END Gregor Turk | Gregor Turk_18905_assignsubmission_file | vaja2.m ====


==== BEGIN Jakob Ian Pintarič | Jakob Ian Pintarič_18916_assignsubmission_file | fitline.m ====
% FITLINE - Least squares fit of a line to a set of points
%
% Usage:   [C, dist] = fitline(XY)
%
% Where:   XY  - 2xNpts array of xy coordinates to fit line to data of
%                the form 
%                [x1 x2 x3 ... xN
%                 y1 y2 y3 ... yN]
%                
%                XY can also be a 3xNpts array of homogeneous coordinates.
%
% Returns: C    - 3x1 array of line coefficients in the form
%                 c(1)*X + c(2)*Y + c(3) = 0
%          dist - Array of distances from the fitted line to the supplied
%                 data points.  Note that dist is only calculated if the
%                 function is called with two output arguments.
%
% The magnitude of C is scaled so that line equation corresponds to
%   sin(theta)*X + (-cos(theta))*Y + rho = 0
% where theta is the angle between the line and the x axis and rho is the
% perpendicular distance from the origin to the line.  Rescaling the
% coefficients in this manner allows the perpendicular distance from any
% point (x,y) to the line to be simply calculated as
%   r = abs(c(1)*X + c(2)*Y + c(3))
%
% If you want to convert this line representation to the classical form 
%     Y = a*X + b
% use
%  a = -c(1)/c(2)
%  b = -c(3)/c(2)
%
% Note, however, that this assumes c(2) is not zero

% Copyright (c) 2003-2008 Peter Kovesi
% School of Computer Science & Software Engineering
% The University of Western Australia
% http://www.csse.uwa.edu.au/
% 
% Permission is hereby granted, free of charge, to any person obtaining a copy
% of this software and associated documentation files (the "Software"), to deal
% in the Software without restriction, subject to the following conditions:
% 
% The above copyright notice and this permission notice shall be included in 
% all copies or substantial portions of the Software.
%
% The Software is provided "as is", without warranty of any kind.

% June      2003 - Original version
% September 2004 - Rescaling to allow simple distance calculation.
% November  2008 - Normalising of coordinates added to condition the
%                  solution.
% December  2016 - Simplified rescaling of coefficients to allow line
%                  distances to be calculated


function [C, dist] = fitline(XY)
  
  [rows,npts] = size(XY);    

  if npts < 2
      error('Too few points to fit line');
  end  
  
  if rows ==2    % Add homogeneous scale coordinate of 1 
      XY = [XY; ones(1,npts)];
  end

  if npts == 2    % Pad XY with a third column of zeros
    XY = [XY zeros(3,1)]; 
  end
  
  % Normalise points so that centroid is at origin and mean distance from
  % origin is sqrt(2).  This conditions the equations. 
  [XYn, T] = normalise2dpts(XY);
  
  % Set up constraint equations of the form  XYn'*C = 0,
  % where C is a column vector of the line coefficients
  % in the form   c(1)*X + c(2)*Y + c(3) = 0.

  [u d v] = svd(XYn',0);   % Singular value decomposition.
  C = v(:,3);              % Solution is last column of v.

  % Denormalise the solution
  C = T'*C;
  
  % Rescale coefficients so that line equation corresponds to
  %   sin(theta)*X + (-cos(theta))*Y + rho = 0
  % so that the perpendicular distance from any point (x,y) to the line
  % to be simply calculated as 
  %   r = abs(c(1)*X + c(2)*Y + c(3))
  C = C / sqrt(C(1)^2 + C(2)^2);
  
  % If requested, calculate the distances from the fitted line to
  % the supplied data points 
  if nargout==2   
      dist = abs(C(1)*XY(1,:) + C(2)*XY(2,:) + C(3));
  end
==== END Jakob Ian Pintarič | Jakob Ian Pintarič_18916_assignsubmission_file | fitline.m ====

==== BEGIN Jakob Ian Pintarič | Jakob Ian Pintarič_18916_assignsubmission_file | normalise2dpts.m ====
% NORMALISE2DPTS - normalises 2D homogeneous points
%
% Function translates and normalises a set of 2D homogeneous points 
% so that their centroid is at the origin and their mean distance from 
% the origin is sqrt(2).  This process typically improves the
% conditioning of any equations used to solve homographies, fundamental
% matrices etc.
%
% Usage:   [newpts, T] = normalise2dpts(pts)
%
% Argument:
%   pts -  3xN array of 2D homogeneous coordinates
%
% Returns:
%   newpts -  3xN array of transformed 2D homogeneous coordinates.  The
%             scaling parameter is normalised to 1 unless the point is at
%             infinity. 
%   T      -  The 3x3 transformation matrix, newpts = T*pts
%           
% If there are some points at infinity the normalisation transform
% is calculated using just the finite points.  Being a scaling and
% translating transform this will not affect the points at infinity.

% Peter Kovesi
% Centre for Exploration Targeting
% The University of Western Australia
% peter.kovesi at uwa edu au
%
% May 2003      - Original version
% February 2004 - Modified to deal with points at infinity.
% December 2008 - meandist calculation modified to work with Octave 3.0.1
%                 (thanks to Ron Parr)
% December 2016 - Disabled warning messgae for points at infinity.


function [newpts, T] = normalise2dpts(pts)

    if size(pts,1) ~= 3
        error('pts must be 3xN');
    end
    
    % Find the indices of the points that are not at infinity
    finiteind = find(abs(pts(3,:)) > eps);
    
%    if length(finiteind) ~= size(pts,2)
%        warning('Some points are at infinity');
%    end
    
    % For the finite points ensure homogeneous coords have scale of 1
    pts(1,finiteind) = pts(1,finiteind)./pts(3,finiteind);
    pts(2,finiteind) = pts(2,finiteind)./pts(3,finiteind);
    pts(3,finiteind) = 1;
    
    c = mean(pts(1:2,finiteind)')';            % Centroid of finite points
    newp(1,finiteind) = pts(1,finiteind)-c(1); % Shift origin to centroid.
    newp(2,finiteind) = pts(2,finiteind)-c(2);
    
    dist = sqrt(newp(1,finiteind).^2 + newp(2,finiteind).^2);
    meandist = mean(dist(:));  % Ensure dist is a column vector for Octave 3.0.1
    
    scale = sqrt(2)/meandist;
    
    T = [scale   0   -scale*c(1)
         0     scale -scale*c(2)
         0       0      1      ];
    
    newpts = T*pts;
    
    
    
==== END Jakob Ian Pintarič | Jakob Ian Pintarič_18916_assignsubmission_file | normalise2dpts.m ====

  311.6429   85.9286;

f_values = 400:20:800;
sumSq = zeros(size(f_values));

for i = 1:length(f_values)
    f = f_values(i);

    rl_edge = -(f/2) * ((exp(-2*r_edge/f) - 1) ./ exp(-r_edge/f));
    rl_center = -(f/2) * ((exp(-2*r_center/f) - 1) ./ exp(-r_center/f));

    x1_corr = cx + (x1 - cx) .* (rl_edge ./ r_edge);
    y1_corr = cy + (y1 - cy) .* (rl_edge ./ r_edge);
    x2_corr = cx + (x2 - cx) .* (rl_center ./ r_center);
    y2_corr = cy + (y2 - cy) .* (rl_center ./ r_center);

    [~, dist1] = fitline([x1_corr'; y1_corr']);
    [~, dist2] = fitline([x2_corr'; y2_corr']);

    sumSq(i) = sum(dist1.^2) + sum(dist2.^2);
end

xlabel('Parameter f');

[~, idx] = min(sumSq);
best_f = f_values(idx);
disp(['Najboljša vrednost parametra f = ', num2str(best_f)]);

%% 4. fminsearch

errorFun = @(f) computeError(f, x1, y1, x2, y2, cx, cy, r_edge, r_center);

f0 = 600;

options = optimset('Display', 'iter', 'TolX', 1e-6);

[f_opt, fval] = fminsearch(errorFun, f0, options);

disp('----------------------------------------------------');
fprintf('Optimalna vrednost parametra f = %.4f\n', f_opt);
fprintf('Minimalna vsota kvadratov razdalj = %.6f\n', fval);

rl_edge = -(f_opt/2) * ((exp(-2*r_edge/f_opt) - 1) ./ exp(-r_edge/f_opt));
rl_center = -(f_opt/2) * ((exp(-2*r_center/f_opt) - 1) ./ exp(-r_center/f_opt));

x1_corr = cx + (x1 - cx) .* (rl_edge ./ r_edge);
y1_corr = cy + (y1 - cy) .* (rl_edge ./ r_edge);
x2_corr = cx + (x2 - cx) .* (rl_center ./ r_center);
y2_corr = cy + (y2 - cy) .* (rl_center ./ r_center);

figure;
imshow(img), hold on;
plot(x1_corr, y1_corr, 'go-', 'LineWidth', 1.2);
plot(x2_corr, y2_corr, 'ro-', 'LineWidth', 1.2);
title(['Korigirane točke za optimalni f = ', num2str(f_opt)]);
legend('Leva linija', 'Sredinska linija');

%% ce
function E = computeError(f, x1, y1, x2, y2, cx, cy, r_edge, r_center)
    rl_edge = -(f/2) * ((exp(-2*r_edge/f) - 1) ./ exp(-r_edge/f));
    rl_center = -(f/2) * ((exp(-2*r_center/f) - 1) ./ exp(-r_center/f));

    x1_corr = cx + (x1 - cx) .* (rl_edge ./ r_edge);
    y1_corr = cy + (y1 - cy) .* (rl_edge ./ r_edge);
    x2_corr = cx + (x2 - cx) .* (rl_center ./ r_center);
    y2_corr = cy + (y2 - cy) .* (rl_center ./ r_center);

    [~, dist1] = fitline([x1_corr'; y1_corr']);
    [~, dist2] = fitline([x2_corr'; y2_corr']);

    E = sum(dist1.^2) + sum(dist2.^2);
end

% FITLINE - Least squares fit of a line to a set of points
%
% Usage:   [C, dist] = fitline(XY)
%
% Where:   XY  - 2xNpts array of xy coordinates to fit line to data of
%                the form 
%                [x1 x2 x3 ... xN
%                 y1 y2 y3 ... yN]
%                
%                XY can also be a 3xNpts array of homogeneous coordinates.
%
% Returns: C    - 3x1 array of line coefficients in the form
%                 c(1)*X + c(2)*Y + c(3) = 0
%          dist - Array of distances from the fitted line to the supplied
%                 data points.  Note that dist is only calculated if the
%                 function is called with two output arguments.
%
% The magnitude of C is scaled so that line equation corresponds to
%   sin(theta)*X + (-cos(theta))*Y + rho = 0
% where theta is the angle between the line and the x axis and rho is the
% perpendicular distance from the origin to the line.  Rescaling the
% coefficients in this manner allows the perpendicular distance from any
% point (x,y) to the line to be simply calculated as
%   r = abs(c(1)*X + c(2)*Y + c(3))
%
% If you want to convert this line representation to the classical form 
%     Y = a*X + b
% use
%  a = -c(1)/c(2)
%  b = -c(3)/c(2)
%
% Note, however, that this assumes c(2) is not zero

% Copyright (c) 2003-2008 Peter Kovesi
% School of Computer Science & Software Engineering
% The University of Western Australia
% http://www.csse.uwa.edu.au/
% 
% Permission is hereby granted, free of charge, to any person obtaining a copy
% of this software and associated documentation files (the "Software"), to deal
% in the Software without restriction, subject to the following conditions:
% 
% The above copyright notice and this permission notice shall be included in 
% all copies or substantial portions of the Software.
%
% The Software is provided "as is", without warranty of any kind.

% June      2003 - Original version
% September 2004 - Rescaling to allow simple distance calculation.
% November  2008 - Normalising of coordinates added to condition the
%                  solution.
% December  2016 - Simplified rescaling of coefficients to allow line
%                  distances to be calculated


function [C, dist] = fitline(XY)
  
  [rows,npts] = size(XY);    

  if npts < 2
      error('Too few points to fit line');
  end  
  
  if rows ==2    % Add homogeneous scale coordinate of 1 
      XY = [XY; ones(1,npts)];
  end

  if npts == 2    % Pad XY with a third column of zeros
    XY = [XY zeros(3,1)]; 
  end
  
  % Normalise points so that centroid is at origin and mean distance from
  % origin is sqrt(2).  This conditions the equations. 
  [XYn, T] = normalise2dpts(XY);
  
  % Set up constraint equations of the form  XYn'*C = 0,
  % where C is a column vector of the line coefficients
  % in the form   c(1)*X + c(2)*Y + c(3) = 0.

  [u d v] = svd(XYn',0);   % Singular value decomposition.
  C = v(:,3);              % Solution is last column of v.

  % Denormalise the solution
  C = T'*C;
  
  % Rescale coefficients so that line equation corresponds to
  %   sin(theta)*X + (-cos(theta))*Y + rho = 0
  % so that the perpendicular distance from any point (x,y) to the line
  % to be simply calculated as 
  %   r = abs(c(1)*X + c(2)*Y + c(3))
  C = C / sqrt(C(1)^2 + C(2)^2);
  
  % If requested, calculate the distances from the fitted line to
  % the supplied data points 
  if nargout==2   
      dist = abs(C(1)*XY(1,:) + C(2)*XY(2,:) + C(3));
  end
==== END Jakob Melkić Sitar | Jakob Melkić Sitar_18903_assignsubmission_file | fitline.m ====

==== BEGIN Jakob Melkić Sitar | Jakob Melkić Sitar_18903_assignsubmission_file | normalise2dpts.m ====
% NORMALISE2DPTS - normalises 2D homogeneous points
%
% Function translates and normalises a set of 2D homogeneous points 
% so that their centroid is at the origin and their mean distance from 
% the origin is sqrt(2).  This process typically improves the
% conditioning of any equations used to solve homographies, fundamental
% matrices etc.
%
% Usage:   [newpts, T] = normalise2dpts(pts)
%
% Argument:
%   pts -  3xN array of 2D homogeneous coordinates
%
% Returns:
%   newpts -  3xN array of transformed 2D homogeneous coordinates.  The
%             scaling parameter is normalised to 1 unless the point is at
%             infinity. 
%   T      -  The 3x3 transformation matrix, newpts = T*pts
%           
% If there are some points at infinity the normalisation transform
% is calculated using just the finite points.  Being a scaling and
% translating transform this will not affect the points at infinity.

% Peter Kovesi
% Centre for Exploration Targeting
% The University of Western Australia
% peter.kovesi at uwa edu au
%
% May 2003      - Original version
% February 2004 - Modified to deal with points at infinity.
% December 2008 - meandist calculation modified to work with Octave 3.0.1
%                 (thanks to Ron Parr)
% December 2016 - Disabled warning messgae for points at infinity.


function [newpts, T] = normalise2dpts(pts)

    if size(pts,1) ~= 3
        error('pts must be 3xN');
    end
    
    % Find the indices of the points that are not at infinity
    finiteind = find(abs(pts(3,:)) > eps);
    
%    if length(finiteind) ~= size(pts,2)
%        warning('Some points are at infinity');
%    end
    
    % For the finite points ensure homogeneous coords have scale of 1
    pts(1,finiteind) = pts(1,finiteind)./pts(3,finiteind);
    pts(2,finiteind) = pts(2,finiteind)./pts(3,finiteind);
    pts(3,finiteind) = 1;
    
    c = mean(pts(1:2,finiteind)')';            % Centroid of finite points
    newp(1,finiteind) = pts(1,finiteind)-c(1); % Shift origin to centroid.
    newp(2,finiteind) = pts(2,finiteind)-c(2);
    
    dist = sqrt(newp(1,finiteind).^2 + newp(2,finiteind).^2);
    meandist = mean(dist(:));  % Ensure dist is a column vector for Octave 3.0.1
    
    scale = sqrt(2)/meandist;
    
    T = [scale   0   -scale*c(1)
         0     scale -scale*c(2)
         0       0      1      ];
    
    newpts = T*pts;
    
    
    
==== END Jakob Melkić Sitar | Jakob Melkić Sitar_18903_assignsubmission_file | normalise2dpts.m ====

for i = 1:length(f_values)
    f = f_values(i);

    rl_edge = -(f/2) * ((exp(-2*r_edge/f) - 1) ./ exp(-r_edge/f));
    rl_center = -(f/2) * ((exp(-2*r_center/f) - 1) ./ exp(-r_center/f));

    x1_corr = cx + (x1 - cx) .* (rl_edge ./ r_edge);
    y1_corr = cy + (y1 - cy) .* (rl_edge ./ r_edge);
    x2_corr = cx + (x2 - cx) .* (rl_center ./ r_center);
    y2_corr = cy + (y2 - cy) .* (rl_center ./ r_center);

    [~, dist1] = fitline([x1_corr'; y1_corr']);
    [~, dist2] = fitline([x2_corr'; y2_corr']);

    sumSq(i) = sum(dist1.^2) + sum(dist2.^2);
end

rl_center = -(f_opt/2) * ((exp(-2*r_center/f_opt) - 1) ./ exp(-r_center/f_opt));

x1_corr = cx + (x1 - cx) .* (rl_edge ./ r_edge);
y1_corr = cy + (y1 - cy) .* (rl_edge ./ r_edge);

y2_corr = cy + (y2 - cy) .* (rl_center ./ r_center);

plot(x1_corr, y1_corr, 'go-', 'LineWidth', 1.2);

legend('Leva linija', 'Sredinska linija');

%% ce
function E = computeError(f, x1, y1, x2, y2, cx, cy, r_edge, r_center)
    rl_edge = -(f/2) * ((exp(-2*r_edge/f) - 1) ./ exp(-r_edge/f));
    rl_center = -(f/2) * ((exp(-2*r_center/f) - 1) ./ exp(-r_center/f));

    x1_corr = cx + (x1 - cx) .* (rl_edge ./ r_edge);
    y1_corr = cy + (y1 - cy) .* (rl_edge ./ r_edge);
    x2_corr = cx + (x2 - cx) .* (rl_center ./ r_center);
    y2_corr = cy + (y2 - cy) .* (rl_center ./ r_center);

    [~, dist1] = fitline([x1_corr'; y1_corr']);
    [~, dist2] = fitline([x2_corr'; y2_corr']);

    E = sum(dist1.^2) + sum(dist2.^2);
end

==== BEGIN Jakob Suhadolc | Jakob Suhadolc_18895_assignsubmission_file | fitline.m ====
% FITLINE - Least squares fit of a line to a set of points
%
% Usage:   [C, dist] = fitline(XY)
%
% Where:   XY  - 2xNpts array of xy coordinates to fit line to data of
%                the form 
%                [x1 x2 x3 ... xN
%                 y1 y2 y3 ... yN]
%                
%                XY can also be a 3xNpts array of homogeneous coordinates.
%
% Returns: C    - 3x1 array of line coefficients in the form
%                 c(1)*X + c(2)*Y + c(3) = 0
%          dist - Array of distances from the fitted line to the supplied
%                 data points.  Note that dist is only calculated if the
%                 function is called with two output arguments.
%
% The magnitude of C is scaled so that line equation corresponds to
%   sin(theta)*X + (-cos(theta))*Y + rho = 0
% where theta is the angle between the line and the x axis and rho is the
% perpendicular distance from the origin to the line.  Rescaling the
% coefficients in this manner allows the perpendicular distance from any
% point (x,y) to the line to be simply calculated as
%   r = abs(c(1)*X + c(2)*Y + c(3))
%
% If you want to convert this line representation to the classical form 
%     Y = a*X + b
% use
%  a = -c(1)/c(2)
%  b = -c(3)/c(2)
%
% Note, however, that this assumes c(2) is not zero

% Copyright (c) 2003-2008 Peter Kovesi
% www.peterkovesi.com
% 
% Permission is hereby granted, free of charge, to any person obtaining a copy
% of this software and associated documentation files (the "Software"), to deal
% in the Software without restriction, subject to the following conditions:
% 
% The above copyright notice and this permission notice shall be included in 
% all copies or substantial portions of the Software.
%
% The Software is provided "as is", without warranty of any kind.

% June      2003 - Original version
% September 2004 - Rescaling to allow simple distance calculation.
% November  2008 - Normalising of coordinates added to condition the
%                  solution.
% December  2016 - Simplified rescaling of coefficients to allow line
%                  distances to be calculated


function [C, dist] = fitline(XY)
  
  [rows,npts] = size(XY);    

  if npts < 2
      error('Too few points to fit line');
  end  
  
  if rows ==2    % Add homogeneous scale coordinate of 1 
      XY = [XY; ones(1,npts)];
  end

  if npts == 2    % Pad XY with a third column of zeros
    XY = [XY zeros(3,1)]; 
  end
  
  % Normalise points so that centroid is at origin and mean distance from
  % origin is sqrt(2).  This conditions the equations. 
  [XYn, T] = normalise2dpts(XY);
  
  % Set up constraint equations of the form  XYn'*C = 0,
  % where C is a column vector of the line coefficients
  % in the form   c(1)*X + c(2)*Y + c(3) = 0.

  [u d v] = svd(XYn',0);   % Singular value decomposition.
  C = v(:,3);              % Solution is last column of v.

  % Denormalise the solution
  C = T'*C;
  
  % Rescale coefficients so that line equation corresponds to
  %   sin(theta)*X + (-cos(theta))*Y + rho = 0
  % so that the perpendicular distance from any point (x,y) to the line
  % to be simply calculated as 
  %   r = abs(c(1)*X + c(2)*Y + c(3))
  C = C / sqrt(C(1)^2 + C(2)^2);
  
  % If requested, calculate the distances from the fitted line to
  % the supplied data points 
  if nargout==2   
      dist = abs(C(1)*XY(1,:) + C(2)*XY(2,:) + C(3));
  end




  
       
       
==== END Jakob Suhadolc | Jakob Suhadolc_18895_assignsubmission_file | fitline.m ====

%Nelinearna optimizacija fminsearch
%Mihaela Temelkova & Jakob Suhadolc
inputImage = imread('Image15.tif');
imshow(inputImage)
imageCenterX = 319.5;
imageCenterY = 239.5;

disp('Oznaci tocke za robno navpicno linijo')
[selectedEdgeX, selectedEdgeY] = ginput;
edgePoints = [selectedEdgeX'; selectedEdgeY'];

disp('Oznaci tocke za centralno navpicno linijo:')
[selectedCenterX, selectedCenterY] = ginput;
centerPoints = [selectedCenterX'; selectedCenterY'];
%%
%preveris ali je f=540

selectedEdgeX = [43 44 40 43 41 39 41 45 44 49];
selectedEdgeY = [69 106 146 181  221  259  290  392 423  451];
selectedCenterY=[84 124 157 195 231 332 394 428 454];
selectedCenterX=[275 272 269 265 262 256 251 250 250];
edgePoints   = [selectedEdgeX; selectedEdgeY];
centerPoints = [selectedCenterX; selectedCenterY];

objective = @(f) total_error_for_f(f, edgePoints, centerPoints, imageCenterX, imageCenterY);

initialGuess = 600;
bestFocalLength = fminsearch(objective, initialGuess);

fprintf('Najboljsi: %.3f\n', bestFocalLength);

f = bestFocalLength;
[xGrid, yGrid] = meshgrid(1:size(inputImage,2), 1:size(inputImage,1));
radialDistanceOriginal = sqrt((xGrid - imageCenterX).^2 + (yGrid - imageCenterY).^2);
radialDistanceMapped = f .* log((radialDistanceOriginal ./ f) + sqrt(1 + (radialDistanceOriginal ./ f).^2));
xGridCorrected = imageCenterX + (xGrid - imageCenterX) .* (radialDistanceMapped ./ radialDistanceOriginal);
yGridCorrected = imageCenterY + (yGrid - imageCenterY) .* (radialDistanceMapped ./ radialDistanceOriginal);

correctedImage = interp2(double(inputImage), xGridCorrected, yGridCorrected, 'linear', 0);
imshow(uint8(correctedImage))
title(sprintf('Popravljena slika (f = %.0f)', f))



function totalError = total_error_for_f(f, edgePoints, centerPoints, imageCenterX, imageCenterY)
    edgeError = compute_line_error(edgePoints, f, imageCenterX, imageCenterY);
    centerError = compute_line_error(centerPoints, f, imageCenterX, imageCenterY);
    totalError = edgeError + 1.0 * centerError;
end


function lineError = compute_line_error(points, f, imageCenterX, imageCenterY)
    x = points(1, :);
    y = points(2, :);
    radialDistanceOriginal = sqrt((x - imageCenterX).^2 + (y - imageCenterY).^2);
    radialDistanceCorrected = -(f/2) .* ((exp(-2*radialDistanceOriginal/f)) - 1) ./ exp(-radialDistanceOriginal/f);
    correctedX = imageCenterX + (x - imageCenterX) .* (radialDistanceCorrected ./ radialDistanceOriginal);
    correctedY = imageCenterY + (y - imageCenterY) .* (radialDistanceCorrected ./ radialDistanceOriginal);
    [~, distancesToLine] = fitline([correctedX; correctedY]);
    lineError = sum(distancesToLine.^2);
end
==== END Jakob Suhadolc | Jakob Suhadolc_18895_assignsubmission_file | NelinearnaOptimizacija_fminsearch_N4.m ====

==== BEGIN Jakob Suhadolc | Jakob Suhadolc_18895_assignsubmission_file | NelinearnaOptimizacija_fminsearch_part2N4.m ====
%Nelinearna optimizacija - fminsearch
%Mihaela Temelkova & Jakob Suhadolc

close all; clear; clc;

inputImage = imread('Image15.tif');
imshow(inputImage)
  
imageCenterX = 319.5;
imageCenterY = 239.5;

disp('Označi točke za robno navpično linijo in pritisni ENTER')
[selectedEdgeX, selectedEdgeY] = ginput;
edgePoints = [selectedEdgeX'; selectedEdgeY'];

disp('Označi točke za centralno navpično linijo in pritisni ENTER')
[selectedCenterX, selectedCenterY] = ginput;
centerPoints = [selectedCenterX'; selectedCenterY'];

selectedPoints = [selectedEdgeX'; selectedEdgeY'];
size(selectedPoints)
%%
% varovalo, da ne optimiziramo s praznimi podatki
if isempty(edgePoints) || isempty(centerPoints)
    error('Klikni nekaj tock na obeh linijah in pritisni Enter.');
end

%Preveris ce se ujema f = 540
selectedEdgeX = [43 44 40 43 41 39 41 45 44 49];
selectedEdgeY = [69 106 146 181  221  259  290  392 423  451];

selectedCenterY=[84 124 157 195 231 332 394 428 454];
selectedCenterX=[275 272 269 265 262 256 251 250 250];

edgePoints   = [selectedEdgeX; selectedEdgeY];
centerPoints = [selectedCenterX; selectedCenterY];

% minimizacija napake preko fminsearch 
objective = @(f) ...
    line_error(edgePoints,f,imageCenterX,imageCenterY) + ...
    line_error(centerPoints,f,imageCenterX,imageCenterY);

initialGuess = 600;
bestF = fminsearch(objective, initialGuess);

%  total error pri najboljšem f (in razbij po linijah) 
errEdge   = line_error(edgePoints,   bestF, imageCenterX, imageCenterY);
errCenter = line_error(centerPoints, bestF, imageCenterX, imageCenterY);
totalErr  = errEdge + errCenter;

fprintf('Najboljsi f = %.3f | total error = %.6f (edge=%.6f, center=%.6f)\n', ...
        bestF, totalErr, errEdge, errCenter);

%  popravek slike 
f = bestF;
[xGrid, yGrid] = meshgrid(1:size(inputImage,2), 1:size(inputImage,1));
r = sqrt((xGrid - imageCenterX).^2 + (yGrid - imageCenterY).^2);
r = max(r, eps);
r_mapped = f .* log((r./f) + sqrt(1 + (r./f).^2));
ratio = r_mapped ./ r;
xCorr = imageCenterX + (xGrid - imageCenterX).*ratio;
yCorr = imageCenterY + (yGrid - imageCenterY).*ratio;

correctedImage = interp2(double(inputImage), xCorr, yCorr, 'linear', 0);
figure; imshow(uint8(correctedImage));
title(sprintf('Popravljena slika (f = %.0f)', f));

% funcija ki racuna koliko je linija iskrivljena
function err = line_error(points, f, cx, cy)
    % prisili f v smiseln interval (da fminsearch ne pobegne)
    f = max(400, min(800, f));

    x = points(1,:);  y = points(2,:);
    r = sqrt((x - cx).^2 + (y - cy).^2);
    r = max(r, eps);  % varovalo proti deljenju z 0

    % inverse model: r = f^{-1}(r')
    r_corr = -(f/2) .* (exp(-2*r/f) - 1) ./ exp(-r/f);

    % popravljene koordinate
    scale = r_corr ./ r;
    x_corr = cx + (x - cx) .* scale;
    y_corr = cy + (y - cy) .* scale;

    % razdalje tock do najboljse premice
    [~, d] = fitline([x_corr; y_corr]);

    err = sum(d.^2);
end

==== END Jakob Suhadolc | Jakob Suhadolc_18895_assignsubmission_file | NelinearnaOptimizacija_fminsearch_part2N4.m ====

==== BEGIN Jakob Suhadolc | Jakob Suhadolc_18895_assignsubmission_file | normalise2dpts.m ====
% NORMALISE2DPTS - normalises 2D homogeneous points
%
% Function translates and normalises a set of 2D homogeneous points 
% so that their centroid is at the origin and their mean distance from 
% the origin is sqrt(2).  This process typically improves the
% conditioning of any equations used to solve homographies, fundamental
% matrices etc.
%
% Usage:   [newpts, T] = normalise2dpts(pts)
%
% Argument:
%   pts -  3xN array of 2D homogeneous coordinates
%
% Returns:
%   newpts -  3xN array of transformed 2D homogeneous coordinates.  The
%             scaling parameter is normalised to 1 unless the point is at
%             infinity. 
%   T      -  The 3x3 transformation matrix, newpts = T*pts
%           
% If there are some points at infinity the normalisation transform
% is calculated using just the finite points.  Being a scaling and
% translating transform this will not affect the points at infinity.

% Peter Kovesi
% www.peterkovesi.com
%
% May 2003      - Original version
% February 2004 - Modified to deal with points at infinity.
% December 2008 - meandist calculation modified to work with Octave 3.0.1
%                 (thanks to Ron Parr)
% December 2016 - Disabled warning messgae for points at infinity.


function [newpts, T] = normalise2dpts(pts)

    if size(pts,1) ~= 3
        error('pts must be 3xN');
    end
    
    % Find the indices of the points that are not at infinity
    finiteind = find(abs(pts(3,:)) > eps);
    
%    if length(finiteind) ~= size(pts,2)
%        warning('Some points are at infinity');
%    end
    
    % For the finite points ensure homogeneous coords have scale of 1
    pts(1,finiteind) = pts(1,finiteind)./pts(3,finiteind);
    pts(2,finiteind) = pts(2,finiteind)./pts(3,finiteind);
    pts(3,finiteind) = 1;
    
    c = mean(pts(1:2,finiteind)')';            % Centroid of finite points
    newp(1,finiteind) = pts(1,finiteind)-c(1); % Shift origin to centroid.
    newp(2,finiteind) = pts(2,finiteind)-c(2);
    
    dist = sqrt(newp(1,finiteind).^2 + newp(2,finiteind).^2);
    meandist = mean(dist(:));  % Ensure dist is a column vector for Octave 3.0.1
    
    scale = sqrt(2)/meandist;
    
    T = [scale   0   -scale*c(1)
         0     scale -scale*c(2)
         0       0      1      ];
    
    newpts = T*pts;
    
    
    
==== END Jakob Suhadolc | Jakob Suhadolc_18895_assignsubmission_file | normalise2dpts.m ====

% Mihaela Temelkova & Jakob Suhadolc
% Projektivna transformacija sahovnice
clear; close all; clc;
I = imread('Image1.tif');  

figure; imshow(I); title('Klikni: TL, TR, BR, BL'); 
[x,y] = ginput(4);                    
tocke_slika = [x y];   

st_kvad_x = 12;  % kvadratki po širini
st_kvad_y = 11;  % kvadratki po višini
dx = 30;  % [px]

W = st_kvad_x*dx;
H = st_kvad_y*dx;

% Idealne točke 
tocke_ideal = [0 0; W 0; W H; 0 H];

% Izračun projektivne transformacije
tform = maketform('projective', tocke_slika, tocke_ideal);

% Izvedemo transformacijo slike (poravnamo sahovnico)
[korigirana, xdata, ydata] = imtransform(I, tform, 'XData', [0 W], 'YData', [0 H]);
figure;
imshow(korigirana, []);
title('Korigirana (poravnana) sahovnica');

% Ustvarimo mrežo vseh oglišč (v idealni poravnani ravnini)
nx = st_kvad_x + 1;  % število stolpcev oglišč
ny = st_kvad_y + 1;  % število vrstic oglišč

[xg, yg] = meshgrid(linspace(0, W, nx), linspace(0, H, ny));

% Inverzna transformacija – iz idealnih koordinat v originalno sliko
tform_inv = maketform('projective', tocke_ideal, tocke_slika);
[mx, my] = tformfwd(tform_inv, xg, yg);

% 9. Narišemo mrežo oglišč na originalni sliki
figure;
imshow(I, []);
hold on;
title('Oglisca sahovnice');
axis image;

for j = 1:ny
    for i = 1:nx
        % Rdeče pike za oglišča
        plot(mx(j,i), my(j,i), 'x', 'MarkerSize', 4, 'LineWidth', 1);
    end
end

hold off;

%Radialna distorzija 
%Jakob Suhadolc & Mihaela Temelkova
inputImage = imread('Image15.tif');
imshow(inputImage)

imageCenterX = 319.5;
imageCenterY = 239.5;

%selectamo dve navpicni liniji in potrdimo s ENTER
disp('Oznaci tocke za robno navpicno linijo')
[selectedEdgeX, selectedEdgeY] = ginput;
edgePoints = [selectedEdgeX'; selectedEdgeY'];

disp('Oznaci tocke za centralno navpicno linijo:')
[selectedCenterX, selectedCenterY] = ginput;
centerPoints = [selectedCenterX'; selectedCenterY'];

selectedPoints = [selectedEdgeX'; selectedEdgeY'];
size(selectedPoints)
%%
%Preveris ce se ujema f = 540
selectedEdgeX = [43 44 40 43 41 39 41 45 44 49]';
selectedEdgeY = [69 106 146 181  221  259  290  392 423  451]';

selectedCenterY=[84 124 157 195 231 332 394 428 454]';
selectedCenterX=[275 272 269 265 262 256 251 250 250]';

edgePoints   = [selectedEdgeX; selectedEdgeY];
centerPoints = [selectedCenterX; selectedCenterY];

focalLengthCandidates = 400:20:800;
totalErrorPerCandidate = zeros(size(focalLengthCandidates));


for idx = 1:length(focalLengthCandidates)
    currentFocalLength = focalLengthCandidates(idx);

    edgeRadialDistanceOriginal = sqrt((selectedEdgeX - imageCenterX).^2 + (selectedEdgeY - imageCenterY).^2);
    edgeRadialDistanceCorrected = -(currentFocalLength/2) .* ((exp(-2*edgeRadialDistanceOriginal/currentFocalLength)) - 1) ./ exp(-edgeRadialDistanceOriginal/currentFocalLength);
    correctedEdgeX = imageCenterX + (selectedEdgeX - imageCenterX) .* (edgeRadialDistanceCorrected ./ edgeRadialDistanceOriginal);
    correctedEdgeY = imageCenterY + (selectedEdgeY - imageCenterY) .* (edgeRadialDistanceCorrected ./ edgeRadialDistanceOriginal);
    [~, edgePointLineDistances] = fitline([correctedEdgeX'; correctedEdgeY']);
    edgeLineError = sum(edgePointLineDistances.^2);

    centerRadialDistanceOriginal = sqrt((selectedCenterX - imageCenterX).^2 + (selectedCenterY - imageCenterY).^2);
    centerRadialDistanceCorrected = -(currentFocalLength/2) .* ((exp(-2*centerRadialDistanceOriginal/currentFocalLength)) - 1) ./ exp(-centerRadialDistanceOriginal/currentFocalLength);
    correctedCenterX = imageCenterX + (selectedCenterX - imageCenterX) .* (centerRadialDistanceCorrected ./ centerRadialDistanceOriginal);
    correctedCenterY = imageCenterY + (selectedCenterY - imageCenterY) .* (centerRadialDistanceCorrected ./ centerRadialDistanceOriginal);
    [~, centerPointLineDistances] = fitline([correctedCenterX'; correctedCenterY']);
    centerLineError = sum(centerPointLineDistances.^2);

    totalErrorPerCandidate(idx) = edgeLineError + 1.0 * centerLineError;
end

figure('Name','SSD vs f'); 
plot(focalLengthCandidates, totalErrorPerCandidate, '-o');
grid on; xlabel('f'); ylabel('Vsota kvadratov razdalje'); title('Iskanje najboljšega f');


[~, bestIndex] = min(totalErrorPerCandidate);
bestFocalLength = focalLengthCandidates(bestIndex)

f = bestFocalLength;
[xGrid, yGrid] = meshgrid(1:size(inputImage,2), 1:size(inputImage,1));
radialDistanceOriginal = sqrt((xGrid - imageCenterX).^2 + (yGrid - imageCenterY).^2);

radialDistanceMapped = f .* log((radialDistanceOriginal ./ f) + sqrt(1 + (radialDistanceOriginal ./ f).^2));
xGridCorrected = imageCenterX + (xGrid - imageCenterX) .* (radialDistanceMapped ./ radialDistanceOriginal);
yGridCorrected = imageCenterY + (yGrid - imageCenterY) .* (radialDistanceMapped ./ radialDistanceOriginal);

figure('Name','Popravljena slika');
correctedImage = interp2(double(inputImage), xGridCorrected, yGridCorrected, 'linear', 0);
imshow(uint8(correctedImage))
title(sprintf('Popravljena slika (f = %.0f)', f))
==== END Jakob Suhadolc | Jakob Suhadolc_18895_assignsubmission_file | radialna_distorzija.m ====

==== BEGIN Jakob Suhadolc | Jakob Suhadolc_18895_assignsubmission_file | Radialna_distorzija_part2_N3.m ====

%Radialna distorzija 
%Jakob Suhadolc & Mihaela Temelkova

inputImage = imread('Image15.tif');
imshow(inputImage)

imageCenterX = 319.5;
imageCenterY = 239.5;

% izberi dve navpični liniji (Enter za konec)
disp('Označi točke za robno navpično linijo in pritisni ENTER')
[selectedEdgeX, selectedEdgeY] = ginput;
edgePoints = [selectedEdgeX; selectedEdgeY];
% Xedge=[43 44 40 43 41 39 41 45 44 49]
% Yedge=[69 106 146 181  221  259  290  392 423  451]

disp('Označi točke za centralno navpično linijo in pritisni ENTER')
[selectedCenterX, selectedCenterY] = ginput;
centerPoints = [selectedCenterX; selectedCenterY];
%Xcen=[275 272 269 265 262 256 251 250 250]
%Ycen=[84 124 157 195 231 332 394 428 454]

selectedPoints = [selectedEdgeX; selectedEdgeY];
size(selectedPoints)

%%
%Preveris ce se ujema f = 540
% selectedEdgeX = [43 44 40 43 41 39 41 45 44 49]';
% selectedEdgeY = [69 106 146 181  221  259  290  392 423  451]';
% 
% selectedCenterY=[84 124 157 195 231 332 394 428 454]';
% selectedCenterX=[275 272 269 265 262 256 251 250 250]';
% 
% edgePoints   = [selectedEdgeX; selectedEdgeY];
% centerPoints = [selectedCenterX; selectedCenterY];
% 
focalLengthCandidates = 400:20:800;
totalErrorPerCandidate = zeros(size(focalLengthCandidates));

for idx = 1:length(focalLengthCandidates)
    f = focalLengthCandidates(idx);

    %  ROBNA linija
    % radialna razdalja r' (od centra) za vsako robno točko
    rE = sqrt((selectedEdgeX - imageCenterX).^2 + (selectedEdgeY - imageCenterY).^2);
    rE = max(rE, eps); % varovalo
    rE_corr = -(f/2) .* (exp(-2*rE/f) - 1) ./ exp(-rE/f);  % podana enačba

    scaleE = rE_corr ./ rE;
    xE = imageCenterX + (selectedEdgeX - imageCenterX).*scaleE;
    yE = imageCenterY + (selectedEdgeY - imageCenterY).*scaleE;
    [~, dE] = fitline([xE'; yE']);
    errE = sum(dE.^2);

 
    % CENTRALNA linija
    rC = sqrt((selectedCenterX - imageCenterX).^2 + (selectedCenterY - imageCenterY).^2);
    rC = max(rC, eps);
    rC_corr = -(f/2) .* (exp(-2*rC/f) - 1) ./ exp(-rC/f);

    scaleC = rC_corr ./ rC;
    xC = imageCenterX + (selectedCenterX - imageCenterX).*scaleC;
    yC = imageCenterY + (selectedCenterY - imageCenterY).*scaleC;

    [~, dC] = fitline([xC'; yC']);
    errC = sum(dC.^2);

    % skupna SSD napaka (rob + center)
    totalErrorPerCandidate(idx) = errE + errC;
end

% graf SSD
figure('Name','SSD vs f'); 
plot(focalLengthCandidates, totalErrorPerCandidate, '-o'); grid on;
xlabel('f'); ylabel('Vsota kvadratov razdalj (SSD)');
title('Iskanje najboljšega f');

[~, bestIndex] = min(totalErrorPerCandidate);
bestFocalLength = focalLengthCandidates(bestIndex)
hold on;

plot(bestFocalLength, totalErrorPerCandidate(bestIndex), 'rx', 'MarkerSize', 12, 'LineWidth', 2);
xline(bestFocalLength, '--r', sprintf('  best f = %d', bestFocalLength), 'LabelVerticalAlignment','bottom');
hold off;

% popravek slike
f = bestFocalLength;
[xGrid, yGrid] = meshgrid(1:size(inputImage,2), 1:size(inputImage,1));
r_new = sqrt((xGrid - imageCenterX).^2 + (yGrid - imageCenterY).^2);
r_new = max(r_new, eps); % varovalo

% inverzni model: r' = f * log( r/f + sqrt(1 + (r/f)^2) )
r_old = f .* log((r_new./f) + sqrt(1 + (r_new./f).^2));
ratio = r_old ./ r_new;
xGridCorrected = imageCenterX + (xGrid - imageCenterX).*ratio;
yGridCorrected = imageCenterY + (yGrid - imageCenterY).*ratio;

figure('Name','Popravljena slika');
correctedImage = interp2(double(inputImage), xGridCorrected, yGridCorrected, 'linear', 0);
imshow(uint8(correctedImage));
title(sprintf('Popravljena slika (f = %d)', f));
==== END Jakob Suhadolc | Jakob Suhadolc_18895_assignsubmission_file | Radialna_distorzija_part2_N3.m ====


==== BEGIN Lina Josifovska | Lina Josifovska_18918_assignsubmission_file | funkcija_za_fminsearch_from_points.m ====
function napaka = funkcija_za_fminsearch_from_points(f, x_rel, y_rel)
    x_rel = x_rel(:); y_rel = y_rel(:);
    n = numel(x_rel);
    r = sqrt(x_rel.^2 + y_rel.^2);
    theta = atan2(y_rel, x_rel);
    r_pop = f .* sinh(r ./ f);   

    [x_corr, y_corr] = pol2cart(theta, r_pop); 

    XY = [x_corr.'; y_corr.'];

    finiteIdx = all(isfinite(XY),1);
    XY = XY(:, finiteIdx);
    if size(XY,2) < 2
        napaka = Inf; return;
    end

    [~, dist] = fitline(XY);
    napaka = sqrt(sum(dist.^2));
end
==== END Lina Josifovska | Lina Josifovska_18918_assignsubmission_file | funkcija_za_fminsearch_from_points.m ====

==== BEGIN Lina Josifovska | Lina Josifovska_18918_assignsubmission_file | Naloga3.m ====
clear; close all; clc;

I = imread('Image15.tif');
figure; imshow(I); title('Select points: first column (edge), then second column (center)');
hold on;

stevilo_tock = 10;
disp('Izberi točk v prvi stolpec, nato pritisni Enter.');
[x1_zajete, y1_zajete] = ginput(stevilo_tock);
disp('Izberi točk v drugi stolpec, nato pritisni Enter.');
[x2_zajete, y2_zajete] = ginput(stevilo_tock);

plot(x1_zajete,y1_zajete,'ro','MarkerFaceColor','r');
plot(x2_zajete,y2_zajete,'go','MarkerFaceColor','g');

cc = [319.5, 239.5];   
xc = cc(1);             
yc = cc(2);             
   
x1 = x1_zajete - xc;
y1 = y1_zajete - yc;
x2 = x2_zajete - xc;
y2 = y2_zajete - yc;

r1 = sqrt(x1.^2 + y1.^2);     
r2 = sqrt(x2.^2 + y2.^2);
theta1 = atan2(y1, x1);
theta2 = atan2(y2, x2);

f = 400:20:800;   
napaka = zeros(size(f));     

for aind = 1:length(f)
    ff = f(aind);
    rl1 = ff .* sinh(r1 ./ ff);   
    rl2 = ff .* sinh(r2 ./ ff);
   
    [X1, Y1] = pol2cart(theta1, rl1);  
    [X2, Y2] = pol2cart(theta2, rl2);
    
    X1_img = X1 + xc;  Y1_img = Y1 + yc;
    X2_img = X2 + xc;  Y2_img = Y2 + yc;
    
    XY1 = [X1_img(:)'; Y1_img(:)'];
    XY2 = [X2_img(:)'; Y2_img(:)'];
   
   [~, d1] = fitline(XY1);
   [~, d2] = fitline(XY2);
   
    napaka(aind) = sum(d1.^2) + sum(d2.^2);   
    
end

figure;
plot(f, napaka, '-o', 'LineWidth', 1.4);
xlabel('f'); ylabel('Sum of squared distances'); grid on;
title('f vs napaka');

[bestVal, idx] = min(napaka);
bestF = f(idx);
fprintf('Best f = %.3f (sumsq = %.6g) at index %d\n', bestF, bestVal, idx);

% ----- optional: show corrected points for best f -----
ff = bestF;
rl1 = ff .* sinh(r1 ./ ff); rl2 = ff .* sinh(r2 ./ ff);
[X1b, Y1b] = pol2cart(theta1, rl1); [X2b, Y2b] = pol2cart(theta2, rl2);
X1b = X1b + xc; Y1b = Y1b + yc; X2b = X2b + xc; Y2b = Y2b + yc;

figure; imshow(I); hold on;
plot(x1_zajete, y1_zajete, 'ro', 'MarkerFaceColor','r');    % originals
plot(x2_zajete, y2_zajete, 'go', 'MarkerFaceColor','g');
plot(X1b, Y1b, 'rx','MarkerSize',10,'LineWidth',1.2);       % corrected
plot(X2b, Y2b, 'gx','MarkerSize',10,'LineWidth',1.2);

% draw fitted straight lines on corrected points
XY1b = [X1b(:)'; Y1b(:)']; XY2b = [X2b(:)'; Y2b(:)'];
[C1b, d1b] = fitline(XY1b); [C2b, d2b] = fitline(XY2b);
draw_line(C1b, 'y-'); draw_line(C2b, 'y-');
title(sprintf('Corrected points for f = %.3f', bestF));

% helper to draw line (same as before)
function draw_line(C, style)
    a = C(1); b = C(2); c = C(3);
    ax = axis;
    xs = linspace(ax(1), ax(2), 2);
    if abs(b) > 1e-9
        ys = -(a*xs + c)/b;
    else
        xs = -c/a * ones(1,2);
        ys = linspace(ax(3), ax(4), 2);
    end
    plot(xs, ys, style, 'LineWidth', 1.5);
end

% Intrinsic and Extrinsic Camera Parameters
%
% This script file can be directly excecuted under Matlab to recover the camera intrinsic and extrinsic parameters.
% IMPORTANT: This file contains neither the structure of the calibration objects nor the image coordinates of the calibration points.
%            All those complementary variables are saved in the complete matlab data file Calib_Results.mat.
% For more information regarding the calibration model visit http://www.vision.caltech.edu/bouguetj/calib_doc/


%-- Focal length:
fc = [ 658.258484286437351 ; 658.470068776869994 ];

%-- Distortion coefficients:

nx = 640;

%-- Those variables are used to control which intrinsic parameters should be optimized

n_ima = 20;						% Number of calibration images
est_fc = [ 1 ; 1 ];					% Estimation indicator of the two focal variables
est_aspect_ratio = 1;				% Estimation indicator of the aspect ratio fc(2)/fc(1)

%-- Image #1:

%% Naloga 2 - projektivna transformacija
close all;
clear all;
clc;

checkerboard = imread('Image_rect18.tif');
f1 = figure;
image(repmat(checkerboard,[1,1,3]));
axis image;
uiwait(msgbox('Označi 4 vogale v smeri urinega kazalca, začni zgoraj levo', 'Navodila', 'modal'));
hold on;            %da lahko čez sliko narišemo še scatter za točke
[x,y] = ginput(4);
scatter(x,y,500,'red','x');           %500 je velikost križcev, x je za obliko
hold off;

in_corners = [x,y];

img_size = 480;         %velikost izhodne slike
out_corners = [0 0; img_size-1 0; img_size-1 img_size-1; 0 img_size-1];

T = maketform("projective",in_corners,out_corners);

projected_checkerboard = imtransform(checkerboard, T,'Xdata',[0 img_size-1],'Ydata',[0 img_size-1]);
f2 = figure;
image(repmat(projected_checkerboard,[1,1,3]));
axis image;

% CORNER DETECTION:
% Naredimo gradient sivinske slike. nato izberemo gradient po x in naredimo še enkrat gradient, da dobimo drugi odvod, sedaj gledamo gradient po y
% naredimo binarno sliko, kjer porečemo vse vrhove in tako dobimo območja vrhov. S pomočjo regionprops dobimo središča teh območij, nato samo dodamo točke na slike
% nato še inverzni transform in inverzno transformacijo točk, ki jih overlayamo čez začetno sliko

[gradient_x, gradient_y] = imgradientxy(projected_checkerboard, "prewitt");
f3 = figure;
surf(gradient_x);
[gradient2_x, gradient2_y] = imgradientxy(gradient_x, "prewitt");
f4 = figure;
surf(gradient2_y);
binary_peaks = gradient2_y > 80 | gradient2_y < -80;
f5 = figure;
image(repmat(binary_peaks,[1,1,3]));
axis image;
peak_stats = regionprops(binary_peaks,'Centroid');
peak_centers = cat(1, peak_stats.Centroid);
hold on;
scatter(peak_centers(:,1),peak_centers(:,2),'filled','r');
hold off;
figure(f2);
hold on;
scatter(peak_centers(:,1),peak_centers(:,2),'filled','r');
hold off;
%inverzna tranformacija in transform vogalov na prvotne koordinate
T_inv = fliptform(T);
peaks_original = tformfwd(T_inv,peak_centers);
figure(f1);
hold on;
scatter(peaks_original(:,1),peaks_original(:,2),'filled','r');
hold off;

uiwait(msgbox('Potrdi za nadaljevanje na nalogo 3 in 4', 'Potrditev'));

%% Naloga 3 - Radialna distorzija

close all;
clear all;
clc;

checkerboard = imread('Image15.tif');
f1 = figure;
image(repmat(checkerboard,[1,1,3]));
axis image;
hold on;
%fetch optičnega središča in prikaz na sliki
calib = load("Calib_Results.mat", "cc");    %središčno točko slike naložimo iz kalibracijskih rezultatov prve naloge
scatter(calib.cc(1), calib.cc(2), 'filled');
legend('optical center');

num_points = 12;
uiwait(msgbox('Označi eno vertikalno linijo oglišč na robu šahovnice (12 oglišč)', 'Navodila', 'modal'));
[xe,ye] = ginput(num_points);
scatter(xe,ye,500,'red','x');           %500 je velikost križcev, x je za obliko
legend('optical center', 'uncorrected edge points');

uiwait(msgbox('Označi vertikalno linijo oglišč najbližje sredini slike (12 oglišč)', 'Navodila', 'modal'));
[xc,yc] = ginput(num_points);
scatter(xc,yc,500,'green','x');           %500 je velikost križcev, x je za obliko
legend('optical center', 'uncorrected edge points', 'uncorrected center points');
hold off;

%naredimo fit premice na točke samo za prikaz na sliki
edge_line = fitline([xe.';ye.']);      % transponirane matrike, da so prave oblike za fitline funkcijo
center_line = fitline([xc.';yc.']);
hold on;

%prikaz premic na sliki
fplot(@(x) -edge_line(1)/edge_line(2)*x - edge_line(3)/edge_line(2), [0, size(checkerboard,2)]);
ylim([0, size(checkerboard,1)]);
fplot(@(x) -center_line(1)/center_line(2)*x - center_line(3)/center_line(2), [0, size(checkerboard,2)]);
ylim([0, size(checkerboard,1)]);
legend('optical center', 'uncorrected edge points', 'uncorrected center points'); %še enkrat, drugače začen zraven dodajat še legendo za premice

% IZRAČUN IN KOREKCIJA RADIJEV:
% zapis s koordinatnim središčem v optičnem središču slike
xe_c = xe - calib.cc(1);
ye_c = ye - calib.cc(2);
xc_c = xc - calib.cc(1);
yc_c = yc - calib.cc(2);

% f ki jih bomo sprobali za korekcijo radijev
f = 400:20:800;

% osnovne premice, tokrat s središčnim koordinatnim sistemom, da izračunamo osnovno napako brez korekcij
[edge_line, edge_dist] = fitline([xe_c.';ye_c.']);      % transponirane matrike, da so prave oblike za fitline funkcijo
[center_line, center_dist] = fitline([xc_c.';yc_c.']);
err = zeros(size(f,2) + 1, 3);  %za eno daljši od vektorja f, ker zapišem v 0 originalno napako, druga dimenzija 3 za robne, centralne in seštevek točk
err(1,1) = sqrt(sum(edge_dist.^2));         % v prvo vrstico arraya err zapišemo nekorigirane napake
err(1,2) = sqrt(sum(center_dist.^2));
err(1,3) = err(1,1) + err(1,2);

% preddefiniranje arrayev za hitrejše delovanje
r_e = zeros(num_points, 1);
r_e_cor = zeros(size(f,2), num_points);
theta_e = zeros(num_points, 1);
r_c = zeros(num_points, 1);
r_c_cor = zeros(size(f,2), num_points);
theta_c = zeros(num_points, 1);

% pretvorba v polarni koordinatni sistem s središčem v optičnem središču slike
for i = 1 : num_points
    r_e(i) = sqrt(xe_c(i).^2 + ye_c(i).^2);
    theta_e(i) = atan2(ye_c(i), xe_c(i));
    
    r_c(i) = sqrt(xc_c(i).^2 + yc_c(i).^2);
    theta_c(i) = atan2(yc_c(i), xc_c(i));
end

% Izračun korigiranih radijev vseh točk in napake za vsak definiran f
for j = 1 : size(f,2)
    for i = 1 : num_points
        r_e_cor(j, i) = -f(j)/2 * (exp(-2*r_e(i)/f(j))-1)/(exp(-r_e(i)/f(j)));
        r_c_cor(j, i) = -f(j)/2 * (exp(-2*r_c(i)/f(j))-1)/(exp(-r_c(i)/f(j)));
    end
    
    %fitanje premice in izračun napake za robne točke
    [x_temp,y_temp]=pol2cart(theta_e,r_e_cor(j,:).');
    [edge_line, edge_dist] = fitline([x_temp.';y_temp.']);
    err(j+1,1) = sqrt(sum(edge_dist.^2));
    
    %fitanje premice in izračun napake za središčne točke
    [x_temp,y_temp]=pol2cart(theta_c,r_c_cor(j,:).');
    [edge_line, edge_dist] = fitline([x_temp.';y_temp.']); 
    err(j+1,2) = sqrt(sum(edge_dist.^2));
    
    %seštevek obeh napak, da gledamo najboljši popravek na celi sliki
    err(j+1,3) = err(j+1,1) + err(j+1,2);
end

% prikaz izračunane napake glede na f
f2 = figure;
plot(f, err(2:end,1), f, err(2:end,2), f, err(2:end,3));     % od drugega elementa naprej, saj je v prvem shranjena nekorigirana napaka
xlabel('f')
ylabel('error')
legend('error - edge','error - center', 'error - sum')

% prikaz robnih točk pri različnih f
f3 = figure;
polarscatter(theta_e,r_e);
hold on;
polarscatter(theta_e,r_e_cor(:,:));
legend(['original',string(f)]);
hold off;

% iskanje najmanjše napake in f pri te napaki
[minimum, min_index] = min(err(2:end,3));
fprintf('Najboljši f pri 3. nalogi je: %.0f \n', f(min_index));

% prikaz najbolje korigiranih točk na prvotni sliki
figure(f1);
hold on;
[x_temp,y_temp] = pol2cart(theta_e, r_e_cor(min_index, :).');
x_temp = x_temp + calib.cc(1);
y_temp = y_temp + calib.cc(2);
scatter(x_temp,y_temp,500,'yellow','x');

[x_temp,y_temp] = pol2cart(theta_c, r_c_cor(min_index, :).');
x_temp = x_temp + calib.cc(1);
y_temp = y_temp + calib.cc(2);
scatter(x_temp,y_temp,500,'blue','x');
legend('optical center', 'uncorrected edge points', 'uncorrected center points', '', '', 'corrected edge points (naloga 3)', 'corrected center points (naloga 3)');
hold off;


%% Naloga 4 - nelinearna optimizacija radialne distorzije
%najprej je treba pognat nalogo 3
[best4f, min4error] = fminsearch(@(f) min_finder(xe, ye, calib.cc(1), calib.cc(2), f), 500);
fprintf('Najboljši f pri 4. nalogi je: %f \n', best4f);


function error = min_finder(x_array, y_array, center_x, center_y,f)
    x_array = x_array - center_x;
    y_array = y_array - center_y;

    r = zeros(length(x_array), 1);
    r_cor = zeros(length(x_array), 1);
    theta = zeros(length(x_array), 1);
    
    for i = 1 : length(x_array)
        r(i) = sqrt(x_array(i)^2 + y_array(i)^2);
        theta(i) = atan2(y_array(i), x_array(i));
        r_cor(i) = -f/2 * (exp(-2*r(i)/f)-1)/(exp(-r(i)/f));
    end
    
    [x_temp,y_temp]=pol2cart(theta,r_cor);
    [edge_line, edge_dist] = fitline([x_temp.';y_temp.']); 
    error = sqrt(sum(edge_dist.^2));  
end

==== BEGIN Lovro Perme | Lovro Perme_18908_assignsubmission_file | fitline.m ====
% FITLINE - Least squares fit of a line to a set of points
%
% Usage:   [C, dist] = fitline(XY)
%
% Where:   XY  - 2xNpts array of xy coordinates to fit line to data of
%                the form 
%                [x1 x2 x3 ... xN
%                 y1 y2 y3 ... yN]
%                
%                XY can also be a 3xNpts array of homogeneous coordinates.
%
% Returns: C    - 3x1 array of line coefficients in the form
%                 c(1)*X + c(2)*Y + c(3) = 0
%          dist - Array of distances from the fitted line to the supplied
%                 data points.  Note that dist is only calculated if the
%                 function is called with two output arguments.
%
% The magnitude of C is scaled so that line equation corresponds to
%   sin(theta)*X + (-cos(theta))*Y + rho = 0
% where theta is the angle between the line and the x axis and rho is the
% perpendicular distance from the origin to the line.  Rescaling the
% coefficients in this manner allows the perpendicular distance from any
% point (x,y) to the line to be simply calculated as
%   r = abs(c(1)*X + c(2)*Y + c(3))
%
% If you want to convert this line representation to the classical form 
%     Y = a*X + b
% use
%  a = -c(1)/c(2)
%  b = -c(3)/c(2)
%
% Note, however, that this assumes c(2) is not zero

% Copyright (c) 2003-2008 Peter Kovesi
% www.peterkovesi.com
% 
% Permission is hereby granted, free of charge, to any person obtaining a copy
% of this software and associated documentation files (the "Software"), to deal
% in the Software without restriction, subject to the following conditions:
% 
% The above copyright notice and this permission notice shall be included in 
% all copies or substantial portions of the Software.
%
% The Software is provided "as is", without warranty of any kind.

% June      2003 - Original version
% September 2004 - Rescaling to allow simple distance calculation.
% November  2008 - Normalising of coordinates added to condition the
%                  solution.
% December  2016 - Simplified rescaling of coefficients to allow line
%                  distances to be calculated


function [C, dist] = fitline(XY)
  
  [rows,npts] = size(XY);    

  if npts < 2
      error('Too few points to fit line');
  end  
  
  if rows ==2    % Add homogeneous scale coordinate of 1 
      XY = [XY; ones(1,npts)];
  end

  if npts == 2    % Pad XY with a third column of zeros
    XY = [XY zeros(3,1)]; 
  end
  
  % Normalise points so that centroid is at origin and mean distance from
  % origin is sqrt(2).  This conditions the equations. 
  [XYn, T] = normalise2dpts(XY);
  
  % Set up constraint equations of the form  XYn'*C = 0,
  % where C is a column vector of the line coefficients
  % in the form   c(1)*X + c(2)*Y + c(3) = 0.

  [u d v] = svd(XYn',0);   % Singular value decomposition.
  C = v(:,3);              % Solution is last column of v.

  % Denormalise the solution
  C = T'*C;
  
  % Rescale coefficients so that line equation corresponds to
  %   sin(theta)*X + (-cos(theta))*Y + rho = 0
  % so that the perpendicular distance from any point (x,y) to the line
  % to be simply calculated as 
  %   r = abs(c(1)*X + c(2)*Y + c(3))
  C = C / sqrt(C(1)^2 + C(2)^2);
  
  % If requested, calculate the distances from the fitted line to
  % the supplied data points 
  if nargout==2   
      dist = abs(C(1)*XY(1,:) + C(2)*XY(2,:) + C(3));
  end




  
       
       
==== END Lovro Perme | Lovro Perme_18908_assignsubmission_file | fitline.m ====

==== BEGIN Lovro Perme | Lovro Perme_18908_assignsubmission_file | normalise2dpts.m ====
% NORMALISE2DPTS - normalises 2D homogeneous points
%
% Function translates and normalises a set of 2D homogeneous points 
% so that their centroid is at the origin and their mean distance from 
% the origin is sqrt(2).  This process typically improves the
% conditioning of any equations used to solve homographies, fundamental
% matrices etc.
%
% Usage:   [newpts, T] = normalise2dpts(pts)
%
% Argument:
%   pts -  3xN array of 2D homogeneous coordinates
%
% Returns:
%   newpts -  3xN array of transformed 2D homogeneous coordinates.  The
%             scaling parameter is normalised to 1 unless the point is at
%             infinity. 
%   T      -  The 3x3 transformation matrix, newpts = T*pts
%           
% If there are some points at infinity the normalisation transform
% is calculated using just the finite points.  Being a scaling and
% translating transform this will not affect the points at infinity.

% Peter Kovesi
% www.peterkovesi.com
%
% May 2003      - Original version
% February 2004 - Modified to deal with points at infinity.
% December 2008 - meandist calculation modified to work with Octave 3.0.1
%                 (thanks to Ron Parr)
% December 2016 - Disabled warning messgae for points at infinity.


function [newpts, T] = normalise2dpts(pts)

    if size(pts,1) ~= 3
        error('pts must be 3xN');
    end
    
    % Find the indices of the points that are not at infinity
    finiteind = find(abs(pts(3,:)) > eps);
    
%    if length(finiteind) ~= size(pts,2)
%        warning('Some points are at infinity');
%    end
    
    % For the finite points ensure homogeneous coords have scale of 1
    pts(1,finiteind) = pts(1,finiteind)./pts(3,finiteind);
    pts(2,finiteind) = pts(2,finiteind)./pts(3,finiteind);
    pts(3,finiteind) = 1;
    
    c = mean(pts(1:2,finiteind)')';            % Centroid of finite points
    newp(1,finiteind) = pts(1,finiteind)-c(1); % Shift origin to centroid.
    newp(2,finiteind) = pts(2,finiteind)-c(2);
    
    dist = sqrt(newp(1,finiteind).^2 + newp(2,finiteind).^2);
    meandist = mean(dist(:));  % Ensure dist is a column vector for Octave 3.0.1
    
    scale = sqrt(2)/meandist;
    
    T = [scale   0   -scale*c(1)
         0     scale -scale*c(2)
         0       0      1      ];
    
    newpts = T*pts;
    
    
    
==== END Lovro Perme | Lovro Perme_18908_assignsubmission_file | normalise2dpts.m ====


==== BEGIN Mihaela Temelkova | Mihaela Temelkova_18909_assignsubmission_file | fitline.m ====
% FITLINE - Least squares fit of a line to a set of points
%
% Usage:   [C, dist] = fitline(XY)
%
% Where:   XY  - 2xNpts array of xy coordinates to fit line to data of
%                the form 
%                [x1 x2 x3 ... xN
%                 y1 y2 y3 ... yN]
%                
%                XY can also be a 3xNpts array of homogeneous coordinates.
%
% Returns: C    - 3x1 array of line coefficients in the form
%                 c(1)*X + c(2)*Y + c(3) = 0
%          dist - Array of distances from the fitted line to the supplied
%                 data points.  Note that dist is only calculated if the
%                 function is called with two output arguments.
%
% The magnitude of C is scaled so that line equation corresponds to
%   sin(theta)*X + (-cos(theta))*Y + rho = 0
% where theta is the angle between the line and the x axis and rho is the
% perpendicular distance from the origin to the line.  Rescaling the
% coefficients in this manner allows the perpendicular distance from any
% point (x,y) to the line to be simply calculated as
%   r = abs(c(1)*X + c(2)*Y + c(3))
%
% If you want to convert this line representation to the classical form 
%     Y = a*X + b
% use
%  a = -c(1)/c(2)
%  b = -c(3)/c(2)
%
% Note, however, that this assumes c(2) is not zero

% Copyright (c) 2003-2008 Peter Kovesi
% www.peterkovesi.com
% 
% Permission is hereby granted, free of charge, to any person obtaining a copy
% of this software and associated documentation files (the "Software"), to deal
% in the Software without restriction, subject to the following conditions:
% 
% The above copyright notice and this permission notice shall be included in 
% all copies or substantial portions of the Software.
%
% The Software is provided "as is", without warranty of any kind.

% June      2003 - Original version
% September 2004 - Rescaling to allow simple distance calculation.
% November  2008 - Normalising of coordinates added to condition the
%                  solution.
% December  2016 - Simplified rescaling of coefficients to allow line
%                  distances to be calculated


function [C, dist] = fitline(XY)
  
  [rows,npts] = size(XY);    

  if npts < 2
      error('Too few points to fit line');
  end  
  
  if rows ==2    % Add homogeneous scale coordinate of 1 
      XY = [XY; ones(1,npts)];
  end

  if npts == 2    % Pad XY with a third column of zeros
    XY = [XY zeros(3,1)]; 
  end
  
  % Normalise points so that centroid is at origin and mean distance from
  % origin is sqrt(2).  This conditions the equations. 
  [XYn, T] = normalise2dpts(XY);
  
  % Set up constraint equations of the form  XYn'*C = 0,
  % where C is a column vector of the line coefficients
  % in the form   c(1)*X + c(2)*Y + c(3) = 0.

  [u d v] = svd(XYn',0);   % Singular value decomposition.
  C = v(:,3);              % Solution is last column of v.

  % Denormalise the solution
  C = T'*C;
  
  % Rescale coefficients so that line equation corresponds to
  %   sin(theta)*X + (-cos(theta))*Y + rho = 0
  % so that the perpendicular distance from any point (x,y) to the line
  % to be simply calculated as 
  %   r = abs(c(1)*X + c(2)*Y + c(3))
  C = C / sqrt(C(1)^2 + C(2)^2);
  
  % If requested, calculate the distances from the fitted line to
  % the supplied data points 
  if nargout==2   
      dist = abs(C(1)*XY(1,:) + C(2)*XY(2,:) + C(3));
  end




  
       
       
==== END Mihaela Temelkova | Mihaela Temelkova_18909_assignsubmission_file | fitline.m ====

==== BEGIN Mihaela Temelkova | Mihaela Temelkova_18909_assignsubmission_file | NelinearnaOptimizacija_fminsearch_N4.m ====
%Nelinearna optimizacija - fminsearch
%Mihaela Temelkova & Jakob Suhadolc

close all; clear; clc;

inputImage = imread('Image15.tif');
imshow(inputImage)
  
imageCenterX = 319.5;
imageCenterY = 239.5;

disp('Označi točke za robno navpično linijo in pritisni ENTER')
[selectedEdgeX, selectedEdgeY] = ginput;
edgePoints = [selectedEdgeX'; selectedEdgeY'];

disp('Označi točke za centralno navpično linijo in pritisni ENTER')
[selectedCenterX, selectedCenterY] = ginput;
centerPoints = [selectedCenterX'; selectedCenterY'];

selectedPoints = [selectedEdgeX'; selectedEdgeY'];
size(selectedPoints)
%%
% varovalo, da ne optimiziramo s praznimi podatki
if isempty(edgePoints) || isempty(centerPoints)
    error('Klikni nekaj tock na obeh linijah in pritisni Enter.');
end

%Preveris ce se ujema f = 540
selectedEdgeX = [43 44 40 43 41 39 41 45 44 49];
selectedEdgeY = [69 106 146 181  221  259  290  392 423  451];

selectedCenterY=[84 124 157 195 231 332 394 428 454];
selectedCenterX=[275 272 269 265 262 256 251 250 250];

edgePoints   = [selectedEdgeX; selectedEdgeY];
centerPoints = [selectedCenterX; selectedCenterY];

% minimizacija napake preko fminsearch 
objective = @(f) ...
    line_error(edgePoints,f,imageCenterX,imageCenterY) + ...
    line_error(centerPoints,f,imageCenterX,imageCenterY);

initialGuess = 600;
bestF = fminsearch(objective, initialGuess);

%  total error pri najboljšem f (in razbij po linijah) 
errEdge   = line_error(edgePoints,   bestF, imageCenterX, imageCenterY);
errCenter = line_error(centerPoints, bestF, imageCenterX, imageCenterY);
totalErr  = errEdge + errCenter;

fprintf('Najboljsi f = %.3f | total error = %.6f (edge=%.6f, center=%.6f)\n', ...
        bestF, totalErr, errEdge, errCenter);

%  popravek slike 
f = bestF;
[xGrid, yGrid] = meshgrid(1:size(inputImage,2), 1:size(inputImage,1));
r = sqrt((xGrid - imageCenterX).^2 + (yGrid - imageCenterY).^2);
r = max(r, eps);
r_mapped = f .* log((r./f) + sqrt(1 + (r./f).^2));
ratio = r_mapped ./ r;
xCorr = imageCenterX + (xGrid - imageCenterX).*ratio;
yCorr = imageCenterY + (yGrid - imageCenterY).*ratio;

correctedImage = interp2(double(inputImage), xCorr, yCorr, 'linear', 0);
figure; imshow(uint8(correctedImage));
title(sprintf('Popravljena slika (f = %.0f)', f));

% funcija ki racuna koliko je linija iskrivljena
function err = line_error(points, f, cx, cy)
    % prisili f v smiseln interval (da fminsearch ne pobegne)
    f = max(400, min(800, f));

    x = points(1,:);  y = points(2,:);
    r = sqrt((x - cx).^2 + (y - cy).^2);
    r = max(r, eps);  % varovalo proti deljenju z 0

    % inverse model: r = f^{-1}(r')
    r_corr = -(f/2) .* (exp(-2*r/f) - 1) ./ exp(-r/f);

    % popravljene koordinate
    scale = r_corr ./ r;
    x_corr = cx + (x - cx) .* scale;
    y_corr = cy + (y - cy) .* scale;

    % razdalje tock do najboljse premice
    [~, d] = fitline([x_corr; y_corr]);

    err = sum(d.^2);
end

==== END Mihaela Temelkova | Mihaela Temelkova_18909_assignsubmission_file | NelinearnaOptimizacija_fminsearch_N4.m ====

==== BEGIN Mihaela Temelkova | Mihaela Temelkova_18909_assignsubmission_file | normalise2dpts.m ====
% NORMALISE2DPTS - normalises 2D homogeneous points
%
% Function translates and normalises a set of 2D homogeneous points 
% so that their centroid is at the origin and their mean distance from 
% the origin is sqrt(2).  This process typically improves the
% conditioning of any equations used to solve homographies, fundamental
% matrices etc.
%
% Usage:   [newpts, T] = normalise2dpts(pts)
%
% Argument:
%   pts -  3xN array of 2D homogeneous coordinates
%
% Returns:
%   newpts -  3xN array of transformed 2D homogeneous coordinates.  The
%             scaling parameter is normalised to 1 unless the point is at
%             infinity. 
%   T      -  The 3x3 transformation matrix, newpts = T*pts
%           
% If there are some points at infinity the normalisation transform
% is calculated using just the finite points.  Being a scaling and
% translating transform this will not affect the points at infinity.

% Peter Kovesi
% www.peterkovesi.com
%
% May 2003      - Original version
% February 2004 - Modified to deal with points at infinity.
% December 2008 - meandist calculation modified to work with Octave 3.0.1
%                 (thanks to Ron Parr)
% December 2016 - Disabled warning messgae for points at infinity.


function [newpts, T] = normalise2dpts(pts)

    if size(pts,1) ~= 3
        error('pts must be 3xN');
    end
    
    % Find the indices of the points that are not at infinity
    finiteind = find(abs(pts(3,:)) > eps);
    
%    if length(finiteind) ~= size(pts,2)
%        warning('Some points are at infinity');
%    end
    
    % For the finite points ensure homogeneous coords have scale of 1
    pts(1,finiteind) = pts(1,finiteind)./pts(3,finiteind);
    pts(2,finiteind) = pts(2,finiteind)./pts(3,finiteind);
    pts(3,finiteind) = 1;
    
    c = mean(pts(1:2,finiteind)')';            % Centroid of finite points
    newp(1,finiteind) = pts(1,finiteind)-c(1); % Shift origin to centroid.
    newp(2,finiteind) = pts(2,finiteind)-c(2);
    
    dist = sqrt(newp(1,finiteind).^2 + newp(2,finiteind).^2);
    meandist = mean(dist(:));  % Ensure dist is a column vector for Octave 3.0.1
    
    scale = sqrt(2)/meandist;
    
    T = [scale   0   -scale*c(1)
         0     scale -scale*c(2)
         0       0      1      ];
    
    newpts = T*pts;
    
    
    
==== END Mihaela Temelkova | Mihaela Temelkova_18909_assignsubmission_file | normalise2dpts.m ====
