% Mihaela Temelkova & Jakob Suhadolc
% Projektivna transformacija sahovnice
clear; close all; clc;
I = imread('Image1.tif');  

figure; imshow(I); title('Klikni: TL, TR, BR, BL'); 
[x,y] = ginput(4);                    
tocke_slika = [x y];   

st_kvad_x = 12;  % kvadratki po širini
st_kvad_y = 11;  % kvadratki po višini
dx = 30;  % [px]

W = st_kvad_x*dx;
H = st_kvad_y*dx;

% Idealne točke 
tocke_ideal = [0 0; W 0; W H; 0 H];

% Izračun projektivne transformacije
tform = maketform('projective', tocke_slika, tocke_ideal);

% Izvedemo transformacijo slike (poravnamo sahovnico)
[korigirana, xdata, ydata] = imtransform(I, tform, 'XData', [0 W], 'YData', [0 H]);
figure;
imshow(korigirana, []);
title('Korigirana (poravnana) sahovnica');

% Ustvarimo mrežo vseh oglišč (v idealni poravnani ravnini)
nx = st_kvad_x + 1;  % število stolpcev oglišč
ny = st_kvad_y + 1;  % število vrstic oglišč

[xg, yg] = meshgrid(linspace(0, W, nx), linspace(0, H, ny));

% Inverzna transformacija – iz idealnih koordinat v originalno sliko
tform_inv = maketform('projective', tocke_ideal, tocke_slika);
[mx, my] = tformfwd(tform_inv, xg, yg);

% 9. Narišemo mrežo oglišč na originalni sliki
figure;
imshow(I, []);
hold on;
title('Oglisca sahovnice');
axis image;

for j = 1:ny
    for i = 1:nx
        % Rdeče pike za oglišča
        plot(mx(j,i), my(j,i), 'x', 'MarkerSize', 4, 'LineWidth', 1);
    end
end

hold off;

%Radialna distorzija 
%Jakob Suhadolc & Mihaela Temelkova

inputImage = imread('Image15.tif');
imshow(inputImage)

imageCenterX = 319.5;
imageCenterY = 239.5;

% izberi dve navpični liniji (Enter za konec)
disp('Označi točke za robno navpično linijo in pritisni ENTER')
[selectedEdgeX, selectedEdgeY] = ginput;
edgePoints = [selectedEdgeX; selectedEdgeY];
% Xedge=[43 44 40 43 41 39 41 45 44 49]
% Yedge=[69 106 146 181  221  259  290  392 423  451]

disp('Označi točke za centralno navpično linijo in pritisni ENTER')
[selectedCenterX, selectedCenterY] = ginput;
centerPoints = [selectedCenterX; selectedCenterY];
%Xcen=[275 272 269 265 262 256 251 250 250]
%Ycen=[84 124 157 195 231 332 394 428 454]

selectedPoints = [selectedEdgeX; selectedEdgeY];
size(selectedPoints)

%%
%Preveris ce se ujema f = 540
selectedEdgeX = [43 44 40 43 41 39 41 45 44 49]';
selectedEdgeY = [69 106 146 181  221  259  290  392 423  451]';

selectedCenterY=[84 124 157 195 231 332 394 428 454]';
selectedCenterX=[275 272 269 265 262 256 251 250 250]';

edgePoints   = [selectedEdgeX; selectedEdgeY];
centerPoints = [selectedCenterX; selectedCenterY];
% 
focalLengthCandidates = 400:20:800;
totalErrorPerCandidate = zeros(size(focalLengthCandidates));

for idx = 1:length(focalLengthCandidates)
    f = focalLengthCandidates(idx);

    %  ROBNA linija
    % radialna razdalja r' (od centra) za vsako robno točko
    rE = sqrt((selectedEdgeX - imageCenterX).^2 + (selectedEdgeY - imageCenterY).^2);
    rE = max(rE, eps); % varovalo
    rE_corr = -(f/2) .* (exp(-2*rE/f) - 1) ./ exp(-rE/f);  % podana enačba

    scaleE = rE_corr ./ rE;
    xE = imageCenterX + (selectedEdgeX - imageCenterX).*scaleE;
    yE = imageCenterY + (selectedEdgeY - imageCenterY).*scaleE;
    [~, dE] = fitline([xE'; yE']);
    errE = sum(dE.^2);

 
    % CENTRALNA linija
    rC = sqrt((selectedCenterX - imageCenterX).^2 + (selectedCenterY - imageCenterY).^2);
    rC = max(rC, eps);
    rC_corr = -(f/2) .* (exp(-2*rC/f) - 1) ./ exp(-rC/f);

    scaleC = rC_corr ./ rC;
    xC = imageCenterX + (selectedCenterX - imageCenterX).*scaleC;
    yC = imageCenterY + (selectedCenterY - imageCenterY).*scaleC;

    [~, dC] = fitline([xC'; yC']);
    errC = sum(dC.^2);

    % skupna SSD napaka (rob + center)
    totalErrorPerCandidate(idx) = errE + errC;
end

% graf SSD
figure('Name','SSD vs f'); 
plot(focalLengthCandidates, totalErrorPerCandidate, '-o'); grid on;
xlabel('f'); ylabel('Vsota kvadratov razdalj (SSD)');
title('Iskanje najboljšega f');

[~, bestIndex] = min(totalErrorPerCandidate);
bestFocalLength = focalLengthCandidates(bestIndex)
hold on;

plot(bestFocalLength, totalErrorPerCandidate(bestIndex), 'rx', 'MarkerSize', 12, 'LineWidth', 2);
xline(bestFocalLength, '--r', sprintf('  best f = %d', bestFocalLength), 'LabelVerticalAlignment','bottom');
hold off;

% popravek slike
f = bestFocalLength;
[xGrid, yGrid] = meshgrid(1:size(inputImage,2), 1:size(inputImage,1));
r_new = sqrt((xGrid - imageCenterX).^2 + (yGrid - imageCenterY).^2);
r_new = max(r_new, eps); % varovalo

% inverzni model: r' = f * log( r/f + sqrt(1 + (r/f)^2) )
r_old = f .* log((r_new./f) + sqrt(1 + (r_new./f).^2));
ratio = r_old ./ r_new;
xGridCorrected = imageCenterX + (xGrid - imageCenterX).*ratio;
yGridCorrected = imageCenterY + (yGrid - imageCenterY).*ratio;

figure('Name','Popravljena slika');
correctedImage = interp2(double(inputImage), xGridCorrected, yGridCorrected, 'linear', 0);
imshow(uint8(correctedImage));
title(sprintf('Popravljena slika (f = %d)', f));

==== BEGIN Nejc Ropič | Nejc Ropič_18898_assignsubmission_file | compute_error.m ====
function error = compute_error(f, x_robni, y_robni, x_sredinska, y_sredinska, x_sredina, y_sredina)
    
    % izracun obeh radijev - razdalja vsake tocke od sredisce
    r_rob = sqrt((x_robni - x_sredina).^2 + (y_robni - y_sredina).^2);
    r_center = sqrt((x_sredinska - x_sredina).^2 + (y_sredinska - y_sredina).^2);

    % Preslikava iz popačenih radijev r' v nepopačene r'_l po enačbi (2)
    r_rob_popacen = - (f/2) .* ((exp(-2 .* r_rob ./ f)) - 1) ./ exp(-r_rob ./ f);
    r_center_popacen = - (f/2) .* ((exp(-2 .* r_center ./ f)) - 1) ./ exp(-r_center ./ f);

    % korekcijski faktor
    korekcija_rob = r_rob_popacen ./ r_rob;
    korekcija_center = r_center_popacen ./ r_center;

    % korekcija tock po formuli korig_tocka = sredina + (tocka_premice - sredina) * korekcijski faktor
    x_rob_korigiran = x_sredina + (x_robni - x_sredina) .* korekcija_rob;
    y_rob_korigiran = y_sredina + (y_robni - y_sredina) .* korekcija_rob;

    x_center_korigiran = x_sredina + (x_sredinska - x_sredina) .* korekcija_center;
    y_center_korigiran = y_sredina + (y_sredinska - y_sredina) .* korekcija_center;

    % fit premice
    [C_rob, dist_rob] = fitline([x_rob_korigiran'; y_rob_korigiran']);
    [C_center, dist_center] = fitline([x_center_korigiran'; y_center_korigiran']);

    % vsota kvadratov napaka
    error = sum(dist_rob.^2) + sum(dist_center.^2);
end
==== END Nejc Ropič | Nejc Ropič_18898_assignsubmission_file | compute_error.m ====

%% dolocanje premice

==== BEGIN Rok Prezelj | Rok Prezelj_18893_assignsubmission_file | nal2.m ====
function rectify_chessboard(image_path)
% RECTIFY_CHESSBOARD  Projektivna korekcija šahovnice
% Uporaba:
%   rectify_chessboard('Image_rect1.tif')

    % ---- 1) Branje slike ----
    img = imread(image_path);
    img = double(img); % enoten tip

    % ---- 2) Uporabnik izbere kote ----
    figure('Name','Klikni 4 kote (TL, TR, BR, BL)');
    imshow(img, []); title('Klikni 4 kote: ZG-L, ZG-D, SP-D, SP-L');
    [x, y] = ginput(4);
    moving = [x y];
    plot(moving(:,1), moving(:,2), 'r+', 'MarkerSize', 10, 'LineWidth', 2);

    % ---- 3) Vnos parametrov šahovnice ----
    answ = inputdlg({'Širina (kvadrati):','Višina (kvadrati):','Velikost kvadrata [px]:'}, ...
                    'Parametri', 1, {'12','11','80'});
    if isempty(answ), return; end
    cols = str2double(answ{1}); rows = str2double(answ{2}); sq = str2double(answ{3});
    width = cols*sq;
    height = rows*sq;

    % ---- 4) Projektivna korekcija ----
    fixed = [1 1; width 1; width height; 1 height];
    tform = maketform('projective', moving, fixed);
    corrected = imtransform(img, tform, 'XData',[1 width],'YData',[1 height], ...
                            'FillValues', median(img(:)));

    figure('Name','Korigirana slika'); imshow(corrected, []); title('Projektivno popravljena šahovnica');

    % ---- 5) Izris mreže na originalno sliko ----
    [X, Y] = meshgrid(1:sq:width+1, 1:sq:height+1);
    X(X>width)=width; Y(Y>height)=height;
    tform_inv = maketform('projective', fixed, moving);
    [u, v] = tformfwd(tform_inv, X, Y);

    figure('Name','Original z oglišči');
    imshow(img, []); hold on;
    plot(u, v, 'g.', 'MarkerSize', 10);
    for r=1:size(u,1), plot(u(r,:), v(r,:), 'g-'); end
    for c=1:size(u,2), plot(u(:,c), v(:,c), 'g-'); end
    title('Vsa oglišča kvadratov');
end

% Klic:
rectify_chessboard('Image_rect1.tif');

==== BEGIN Rok Prezelj | Rok Prezelj_18893_assignsubmission_file | nal3.m ====
function radialna_distorzija(image_path)
% RADIALNA_DISTORZIJA – rešitev 3. naloge
% Uporaba:
%   radialna_distorzija('Image15.tif')   % ali pokličeš brez argumenta
%
% Postopek:
%  1) Prikaže sliko in te z ginput pozove, da klikneš točke dveh VERTIKALNIH kolon
%     vogalov: najprej kolona pri ROBU, potem kolona blizu SREDINE (Enter zaključi vsako kolono).
%  2) Za f = 400:20:800 izvede “odpopačenje” točk po danem inverznem modelu in
%     za vsako vrednost f izračuna vsoto kvadratov razdalj do najboljše premice (za obe koloni).
%  3) Nariše graf SSE(f) in izpiše optimalni f.
%
% Zahteve naloge in uporabljeni model so iz navodil vaje (glej PDF). 

    if nargin < 1 || isempty(image_path)
        image_path = 'Image15.tif';  % po navodilih
    end

    % --- Branje slike (v sivine) ---
    I = imread(image_path);
    if size(I,3) > 1, I = rgb2gray(I); end
    I = im2double(I);

    % --- Prikaz in zajem točk ---
    figure; imshow(I,[]); title('Klikni točke 1. (robne) VERTIKALNE kolone, nato Enter');
    [x1, y1] = ginput;  % prva kolona (pri robu)
    pts1 = [x1 y1];

    title('Zdaj klikni točke 2. (sredinske) VERTIKALNE kolone, nato Enter');
    [x2, y2] = ginput;  % druga kolona (blizu centra)
    pts2 = [x2 y2];

    if size(pts1,1) < 2 || size(pts2,1) < 2
        error('Za vsako kolono označi vsaj dve točki.');
    end

    % --- Središče slike (privzamemo približno v središču) ---
    [h, w] = size(I);
    C = [w/2, h/2];  % (cx, cy); če poznaš boljši principal point, ga vnesi tu

    % --- Interval f-jev po navodilih ---
    fs = 400:20:800;
    SSE = zeros(size(fs));

    use_fitline = exist('fitline','file') == 2;  % če imaš kovesi/fitline.m na poti

    % Glavna zanka po f
    for k = 1:numel(fs)
        f = fs(k);

        % Odpopači obe koloni točk
        und1 = undistort_points_radial(pts1, C, f);
        und2 = undistort_points_radial(pts2, C, f);

        % Oceni “linearno” naravnanost (vsota kvadratov razdalj do najboljše premice)
        if use_fitline
            [~, sse1] = fitline(und1); %#ok<ASGLU>  % vrne tudi koeficiente, a tu rabimo sse
            [~, sse2] = fitline(und2);
        else
            % Fallback: ortogonalna regresija (PCA) na točkah – vrne SSE
            sse1 = orth_line_sse(und1);
            sse2 = orth_line_sse(und2);
        end

        SSE(k) = sse1 + sse2;
    end

    % --- Najboljši f ---
    [SSEmin, idx] = min(SSE);
    f_opt = fs(idx);

    % --- Graf ---
    figure;
    plot(fs, SSE, 'o-','LineWidth',1.5); hold on;
    plot(f_opt, SSEmin, 'p', 'MarkerSize',12, 'MarkerFaceColor','auto');
    grid on; xlabel('f'); ylabel('Vsota kvadratov razdalj (SSE)');
    title(sprintf('Radialna distorzija – najboljše f = %d (SSE = %.4g)', f_opt, SSEmin));

    % --- Izhodna informacija ---
    fprintf('Optimalni f = %d (SSE = %.6g)\n', f_opt, SSEmin);
end

% ================= POMOŽNE FUNKCIJE =================

function und = undistort_points_radial(pts, C, f)
% Inverzni model iz navodil naloge:
% r' -> r'_l po:
%   r'_l = -f^2 * (exp(-2 r'/f) - 1) / exp(-r'/f)
% Kar je numerično stabilno izračunati kot:
%   r'_l = 2 f^2 sinh(r'/f)
%
% Nato točko prestavimo vzdolž radialne smeri:
%   und = C + (r'_l / r') * (pts - C)

    v = pts - C;                          % radialni vektorji
    r_prime = hypot(v(:,1), v(:,2));      % popačen radij r'
    t = r_prime / f;

    % Inverzna preslikava (po enačbi iz navodil; stabilno preko sinh)
    r_l_prime = 2 * (f^2) * sinh(t);      % r'_l

    % Izogni se deljenju z 0 v centru (r' == 0)
    scale = r_l_prime ./ max(r_prime, eps);

    und = C + [scale scale] .* v;
end

function sse = orth_line_sse(pts)
% Ortogonalna regresija: najboljša premica v smislu min. kvadratov
% SSE = vsota kvadratov ort. razdalj točk do premice
    mu = mean(pts,1);
    X = pts - mu;
    if size(X,1) < 2
        sse = 0;
        return
    end
    [U,~,~] = svd(X, 'econ');   %#ok<NASGU>
    dir = U(:,1);               % smer glavne komponente v prostoru točk (2D)
    % Aha, U je NxN; za 2D je bolje uporabiti eigenvektor kovariančne matrike:
    C = (X.'*X) / size(X,1);
    [V, D] = eig(C);            %#ok<ASGLU>
    [~, iMax] = max(diag(D));
    u = V(:, iMax);             % smer enotskega vektorja premice

    % Ortogonalna razdalja = norma (I - u u^T)(x - mu)
    Pperp = eye(2) - (u*u.');
    residuals = (Pperp * X.').';
    sse = sum(sum(residuals.^2, 2));
end
==== END Rok Prezelj | Rok Prezelj_18893_assignsubmission_file | nal3.m ====

==== BEGIN Rok Prezelj | Rok Prezelj_18893_assignsubmission_file | nal4.m ====
function radialna_distorzija(image_path)
% RADIALNA_DISTORZIJA – rešitev 3. naloge (z iskanjem optimalnega f preko fminsearch)
%
% Uporaba:
%   radialna_distorzija('Image15.tif')
%
% Postopek:
%   1) Prikaže sliko in te z ginput pozove, da klikneš točke dveh VERTIKALNIH kolon vogalov:
%        – najprej kolona pri ROBU
%        – nato kolona blizu SREDINE
%      (Vsako zaključiš s tipko Enter)
%   2) Izvede korekcijo po danem modelu in s pomočjo fminsearch poišče optimalni f,
%      ki minimizira vsoto kvadratov razdalj do najboljše premice (SSE).
%   3) Prikaže rezultat in izpiše optimalni f.

    if nargin < 1 || isempty(image_path)
        image_path = 'Image15.tif';  % privzeto ime slike
    end

    % --- Branje slike ---
    I = imread(image_path);
    if size(I,3) > 1, I = rgb2gray(I); end
    I = im2double(I);

    % --- Označevanje točk ---
    figure; imshow(I,[]); title('Klikni točke 1. (robne) vertikalne kolone, nato Enter');
    [x1, y1] = ginput; pts1 = [x1 y1];

    title('Zdaj klikni točke 2. (sredinske) vertikalne kolone, nato Enter');
    [x2, y2] = ginput; pts2 = [x2 y2];

    if size(pts1,1) < 2 || size(pts2,1) < 2
        error('Za vsako kolono označi vsaj dve točki.');
    end

    % --- Središče slike ---
    [h, w] = size(I);
    C = [w/2, h/2];

    % ============================================
    % Iskanje najboljšega f z optimizacijo
    % ============================================
    sse_fun = @(f) total_sse(f, pts1, pts2, C);

    f0 = 600;  % začetna ocena
    fprintf('Iščem optimalni f ...\n');
    f_opt = fminsearch(sse_fun, f0, optimset('Display','iter'));
    SSEmin = sse_fun(f_opt);

    fprintf('\nOptimalni f = %.3f (SSE = %.6g)\n', f_opt, SSEmin);
end

% ================================================================
% POMOŽNE FUNKCIJE
% ================================================================

function und = undistort_points_radial(pts, C, f)
% Inverzni model iz navodil naloge:
% r' -> r'_l po:
%   r'_l = -f^2 * (exp(-2r'/f) - 1) / exp(-r'/f)
% Numerično stabilna oblika:
%   r'_l = 2f^2 * sinh(r'/f)
% Nato točko prestavimo vzdolž radialne smeri.

    v = pts - C;                      
    r_prime = hypot(v(:,1), v(:,2));  
    t = r_prime / f;

    r_l_prime = 2 * (f^2) * sinh(t);  
    scale = r_l_prime ./ max(r_prime, eps);
    und = C + [scale scale] .* v;
end

function sse = orth_line_sse(pts)
% Ortogonalna regresija (najmanjši kvadrati)
    mu = mean(pts,1);
    X = pts - mu;
    if size(X,1) < 2
        sse = 0; return
    end
    C = (X.'*X) / size(X,1);
    [V,D] = eig(C);
    [~,iMax] = max(diag(D));
    u = V(:,iMax);
    Pperp = eye(2) - (u*u.');
    residuals = (Pperp * X.').';
    sse = sum(sum(residuals.^2,2));
end

function sse_total = total_sse(f, pts1, pts2, C)
% Skupna napaka za dano vrednost f
    if f <= 0
        sse_total = inf; return
    end
    und1 = undistort_points_radial(pts1, C, f);
    und2 = undistort_points_radial(pts2, C, f);
    sse_total = orth_line_sse(und1) + orth_line_sse(und2);
end

==== BEGIN Teodora Tanurkova | Teodora Tanurkova_18912_assignsubmission_file | fitline.m ====
% FITLINE - Least squares fit of a line to a set of points
%
% Usage:   [C, dist] = fitline(XY)
%
% Where:   XY  - 2xNpts array of xy coordinates to fit line to data of
%                the form 
%                [x1 x2 x3 ... xN
%                 y1 y2 y3 ... yN]
%                
%                XY can also be a 3xNpts array of homogeneous coordinates.
%
% Returns: C    - 3x1 array of line coefficients in the form
%                 c(1)*X + c(2)*Y + c(3) = 0
%          dist - Array of distances from the fitted line to the supplied
%                 data points.  Note that dist is only calculated if the
%                 function is called with two output arguments.
%
% The magnitude of C is scaled so that line equation corresponds to
%   sin(theta)*X + (-cos(theta))*Y + rho = 0
% where theta is the angle between the line and the x axis and rho is the
% perpendicular distance from the origin to the line.  Rescaling the
% coefficients in this manner allows the perpendicular distance from any
% point (x,y) to the line to be simply calculated as
%   r = abs(c(1)*X + c(2)*Y + c(3))
%
% If you want to convert this line representation to the classical form 
%     Y = a*X + b
% use
%  a = -c(1)/c(2)
%  b = -c(3)/c(2)
%
% Note, however, that this assumes c(2) is not zero

% Copyright (c) 2003-2008 Peter Kovesi
% www.peterkovesi.com
% 
% Permission is hereby granted, free of charge, to any person obtaining a copy
% of this software and associated documentation files (the "Software"), to deal
% in the Software without restriction, subject to the following conditions:
% 
% The above copyright notice and this permission notice shall be included in 
% all copies or substantial portions of the Software.
%
% The Software is provided "as is", without warranty of any kind.

% June      2003 - Original version
% September 2004 - Rescaling to allow simple distance calculation.
% November  2008 - Normalising of coordinates added to condition the
%                  solution.
% December  2016 - Simplified rescaling of coefficients to allow line
%                  distances to be calculated


function [C, dist] = fitline(XY)
  
  [rows,npts] = size(XY);    

  if npts < 2
      error('Too few points to fit line');
  end  
  
  if rows ==2    % Add homogeneous scale coordinate of 1 
      XY = [XY; ones(1,npts)];
  end

  if npts == 2    % Pad XY with a third column of zeros
    XY = [XY zeros(3,1)]; 
  end
  
  % Normalise points so that centroid is at origin and mean distance from
  % origin is sqrt(2).  This conditions the equations. 
  [XYn, T] = normalise2dpts(XY);
  
  % Set up constraint equations of the form  XYn'*C = 0,
  % where C is a column vector of the line coefficients
  % in the form   c(1)*X + c(2)*Y + c(3) = 0.

  [u d v] = svd(XYn',0);   % Singular value decomposition.
  C = v(:,3);              % Solution is last column of v.

  % Denormalise the solution
  C = T'*C;
  
  % Rescale coefficients so that line equation corresponds to
  %   sin(theta)*X + (-cos(theta))*Y + rho = 0
  % so that the perpendicular distance from any point (x,y) to the line
  % to be simply calculated as 
  %   r = abs(c(1)*X + c(2)*Y + c(3))
  C = C / sqrt(C(1)^2 + C(2)^2);
  
  % If requested, calculate the distances from the fitted line to
  % the supplied data points 
  if nargout==2   
      dist = abs(C(1)*XY(1,:) + C(2)*XY(2,:) + C(3));
  end




  
       
       
==== END Teodora Tanurkova | Teodora Tanurkova_18912_assignsubmission_file | fitline.m ====

==== BEGIN Teodora Tanurkova | Teodora Tanurkova_18912_assignsubmission_file | normalise2dpts.m ====
% NORMALISE2DPTS - normalises 2D homogeneous points
%
% Function translates and normalises a set of 2D homogeneous points 
% so that their centroid is at the origin and their mean distance from 
% the origin is sqrt(2).  This process typically improves the
% conditioning of any equations used to solve homographies, fundamental
% matrices etc.
%
% Usage:   [newpts, T] = normalise2dpts(pts)
%
% Argument:
%   pts -  3xN array of 2D homogeneous coordinates
%
% Returns:
%   newpts -  3xN array of transformed 2D homogeneous coordinates.  The
%             scaling parameter is normalised to 1 unless the point is at
%             infinity. 
%   T      -  The 3x3 transformation matrix, newpts = T*pts
%           
% If there are some points at infinity the normalisation transform
% is calculated using just the finite points.  Being a scaling and
% translating transform this will not affect the points at infinity.

% Peter Kovesi
% www.peterkovesi.com
%
% May 2003      - Original version
% February 2004 - Modified to deal with points at infinity.
% December 2008 - meandist calculation modified to work with Octave 3.0.1
%                 (thanks to Ron Parr)
% December 2016 - Disabled warning messgae for points at infinity.


function [newpts, T] = normalise2dpts(pts)

    if size(pts,1) ~= 3
        error('pts must be 3xN');
    end
    
    % Find the indices of the points that are not at infinity
    finiteind = find(abs(pts(3,:)) > eps);
    
%    if length(finiteind) ~= size(pts,2)
%        warning('Some points are at infinity');
%    end
    
    % For the finite points ensure homogeneous coords have scale of 1
    pts(1,finiteind) = pts(1,finiteind)./pts(3,finiteind);
    pts(2,finiteind) = pts(2,finiteind)./pts(3,finiteind);
    pts(3,finiteind) = 1;
    
    c = mean(pts(1:2,finiteind)')';            % Centroid of finite points
    newp(1,finiteind) = pts(1,finiteind)-c(1); % Shift origin to centroid.
    newp(2,finiteind) = pts(2,finiteind)-c(2);
    
    dist = sqrt(newp(1,finiteind).^2 + newp(2,finiteind).^2);
    meandist = mean(dist(:));  % Ensure dist is a column vector for Octave 3.0.1
    
    scale = sqrt(2)/meandist;
    
    T = [scale   0   -scale*c(1)
         0     scale -scale*c(2)
         0       0      1      ];
    
    newpts = T*pts;
    
    
    
==== END Teodora Tanurkova | Teodora Tanurkova_18912_assignsubmission_file | normalise2dpts.m ====

==== BEGIN Teodora Tanurkova | Teodora Tanurkova_18912_assignsubmission_file | Vaja2koda.m ====

%% 2 naloga
figure(1);
shahovnica = imread('Image_rect13.tif');
imshow(shahovnica);

[x0,y0] = ginput(4);
points = [x0, y0];
squares_alongX = 12;
squares_alongY = 11;
dxy = 30;

dolzinoX = squares_alongX*dxy;
dolzinoY = squares_alongY*dxy;

OutputP = [0 0;
    dolzinoX 0; 
    dolzinoX dolzinoY; 
    0 dolzinoY];

T = maketform('projective', points, OutputP);
I_corrected = imtransform (shahovnica,T,'XData', [0 dolzinoX],'YData', [0 dolzinoY]);

[xg, yg] = meshgrid(0:dxy:dolzinoX, 0:dxy:dolzinoY); 

figure;
imshow(I_corrected);
hold on;
plot(xg, yg, 'rx', 'MarkerSize', 5);

T_inv = maketform('projective', OutputP, points); 

[x_back, y_back] = tformfwd(T_inv, xg(:), yg(:));

figure; 
imshow(shahovnica);
hold on;
plot(x_back, y_back, 'rx', 'MarkerSize', 6, 'LineWidth',1);
title('Vsa oglišča šahovnice preslikana nazaj na original (inverzna transformacija)');

%% 3 naloga

figure
ImDistortion = imread('Image15.tif');
imshow(ImDistortion);
title('oznaci 6 tocke ki so kotov kvadratkov na eno vertikalo, da bi se izrisali 3 premici')
hold on

cx = 305.69055;
cy = 243.09499;

%X = [43, 41, 40, 41, 44, 47;
 %    311, 304, 298, 292, 284, 279];
%Y = [69, 146, 221, 294, 394, 454;
 %    88, 162, 233, 301, 396, 454];
for i=1:2
    [x2,y2] = ginput(6);
    %x2 = X(i, :);
    %y2 = Y(i, :);
    XY = [x2'; y2']; %jo transporiramo(') ce pisemo tocke z ginuput, da dobimo obliko 2xN, avtomatski pa prevzame Nx2 oblika
    C = fitline(XY);

    yPoints = linspace(min(y2), max(y2), 500);
    xPoints = (-(yPoints*C(2)) - C(3))/ C(1);

    if i == 1
        plot(xPoints, yPoints, 'g')
        alpha1 = atan2(XY(2,:) - cy, XY(1,:) - cx);
        rad1_popacen = sqrt((cx - XY(1, :)).^2 + (cy - XY(2,:)).^2);
    elseif i ==2
        plot(xPoints, yPoints, 'b')
        alpha2 = atan2(XY(2,:) - cy, XY(1,:) - cx);
        rad2_popacen = sqrt((cx - XY(1, :)).^2 + (cy - XY(2,:)).^2);
    end
end

f_values = 400:20:800;
SumSq1 = zeros(size(f_values));
SumSq2 = zeros(size(f_values));
SumAll = zeros(size(f_values));


for k = 1:numel(f_values)
    f = f_values(k);

    r_popr1 = (-f/2) .* ((exp(-(2*rad1_popacen)./f) - 1) ./ (exp(-(rad1_popacen)./f))); %pika zarad mnozenje posebnega elementa iz vektor z vec elementov
    r_popr2 = (-f/2) .* ((exp(-(2*rad2_popacen)./f) - 1) ./ (exp(-(rad2_popacen)./f)));
    
    X1_popr = cx + r_popr1 .* cos(alpha1);
    Y1_popr = cy + r_popr1 .* sin(alpha1);
    XY1 = [X1_popr;Y1_popr]; %tukaj ne transporniramo, ker r_popr1,2,3 so ze vrsticni vektorji 1xN
    [~, dist1] = fitline(XY1);
    SumSq1(k) = sum(dist1.^2);
    
    X2_popr = cx + r_popr2 .* cos(alpha2);
    Y2_popr = cy + r_popr2 .* sin(alpha2);
    XY2 = [X2_popr;Y2_popr]; 
    [~, dist2] = fitline(XY2);
    SumSq2(k) = sum(dist2.^2);
    
    SumAll(k) = SumSq1(k) + SumSq2(k);

end

figure
title("Napaka distorzije")
plot(f_values,SumSq1,'b')
hold on
plot(f_values,SumSq2,'g');
hold on
plot(f_values,SumAll,'y')
legend('Napaka prve črte','Napaka druge črte', 'Skupna napaka')
xlabel("f")
ylabel("Napaka")

% 4 Naloga

r_popacen = {rad1_popacen, rad2_popacen};
alphas = {alpha1, alpha2};
f0 = 600;

[f_optimalen, minNapaka] = fminsearch(@(f) distorzijapopr(f, cx, cy, r_popacen, alphas), f0);
 %@(f) pomeni da vhodni parameter v funkciji je f, ker fminsearch prevzame
 %funkcijo samo z enim parametrom tukaj povedamo s tem da spreminjamo samo f
 %ostale par. v fji ostanejo nespremenjeni, zacne pa iskati pri f0
 fprintf('Optimalni parameter f = %.2f\n', f_optimalen);
 fprintf('Minimalna skupna napaka = %.6f\n', minNapaka);
 
 function skupnaNapaka = distorzijapopr(f, cx, cy, r_popacen, alphas)
        
         skupnaNapaka = 0;
         for i = 1:numel(r_popacen)

             r_popravljen = (-f/2) .* ((exp(-(2*r_popacen{i})./f) - 1) ./ (exp(-(r_popacen{i})./f)));
           
             X_popravljen = cx + r_popravljen .* cos(alphas{i});
             Y_popravljen = cy + r_popravljen .* sin(alphas{i});

             XYo = [X_popravljen; Y_popravljen];
             [~, dist] = fitline(XYo);

             skupnaNapaka = skupnaNapaka + sum(dist.^2);
         end
end

%% Naloga 2.1: Kalibracija
calib_gui

%% Naloga 2.2: Projektivna transformacija

% 1. Preberi sliko in pokaži
Slika1 = imread("Image_rect2.tif");  
figure; imshow(Slika1); axis image;
hold on

disp('Klikni 4 kote šahovnice v urinem vrstnem redu, začni zgoraj levo.');
[x, y] = ginput(4);   % input
src = [x y];          % izvorne koordinate
plot(x, y, 'ro', 'MarkerSize', 8, 'LineWidth', 2);

hold off

W = 500; H = 500;              % velikost slike (px)
dst = [0 0; W 0; W H; 0 H];    % zgoraj-levo, zgoraj-desno, spodaj-desno, spodaj-levo

tform = maketform('projective', src, dst);
% tform = fitgeotrans(src, dst, 'projective');

T = imtransform(Slika1, tform, 'XData', [0 W], 'YData', [0 H], 'Size', [H W]);
figure; imshow(T); axis image;
title('Projektivno poravnana slika');

num_squares_x = 12; % število kvadratov v x smeri
num_squares_y = 11; % število kvadratov v y smeri
[xg, yg] = meshgrid(linspace(0, W, num_squares_x+1), linspace(0, H, num_squares_y+1));
grid_pts = [xg(:), yg(:)];

inv_tform = maketform('projective', dst, src); % inverzna transformacija
[x_back, y_back] = tformfwd(inv_tform, grid_pts(:,1), grid_pts(:,2));

figure; imshow(Slika1); axis image; hold on;
plot(x_back, y_back, 'rs', 'MarkerSize', 6, 'LineWidth', 1.2);
title('Originalna slika z mrežo napovedanih oglišč');
hold off;

%% Naloga 2.3: Radialna distorzija

Slika3 = imread("Image15.tif");
figure; imshow(Slika3); axis image; hold on;

num_points = 13;

% Prva vrstica (ob robu slike)
disp('Klikni 13 vogalov v vertikalni vrstici ob robu slike (od zgoraj navzdol)');
x1 = zeros(num_points,1);
y1 = zeros(num_points,1);

for i = 1:num_points
    [x1(i), y1(i)] = ginput(1);
    plot(x1(i), y1(i), 'rs', 'MarkerSize', 5, 'LineWidth', 2);
end

% Druga vrstica (na sredini)
disp('Klikni 13 vogalov v sredinski vertikalni vrstici (od zgoraj navzdol)');
x2 = zeros(num_points,1);
y2 = zeros(num_points,1);

for i = 1:num_points
    [x2(i), y2(i)] = ginput(1);
    plot(x2(i), y2(i), 'bs', 'MarkerSize', 5, 'LineWidth', 2);
end
hold off;

% Središče slike
cx = size(Slika3,2)/2;
cy = size(Slika3,1)/2;

r_edge = sqrt((x1 - cx).^2 + (y1 - cy).^2);    % radiji za robno vrstico
r_center = sqrt((x2 - cx).^2 + (y2 - cy).^2);  % radiji za sredinsko vrstico

f_values = 400:20:800;
errors = zeros(size(f_values));

for i = 1:length(f_values)
    f = f_values(i);
 
    r_lin_edge = -(f/2) * ((exp(-2*r_edge/f) - 1) ./ exp(-r_edge/f));
    r_lin_center = -(f/2) * ((exp(-2*r_center/f) - 1) ./ exp(-r_center/f));
    
    theta_edge = atan2(y1 - cy, x1 - cx);
    theta_center = atan2(y2 - cy, x2 - cx);

    x1_corr = cx + r_lin_edge .* cos(theta_edge);
    y1_corr = cy + r_lin_edge .* sin(theta_edge);
    x2_corr = cx + r_lin_center .* cos(theta_center);
    y2_corr = cy + r_lin_center .* sin(theta_center);

    XY1_corr = [x1_corr'; y1_corr'];
    XY2_corr = [x2_corr'; y2_corr'];

    [~, dist_edge] = fitline(XY1_corr);
    [~, dist_center] = fitline(XY2_corr);

    sse_edge = sum(dist_edge.^2);
    sse_center = sum(dist_center.^2);

    errors(i) = sse_edge + sse_center;

end

% Rezultat
figure;
plot(f_values, errors, '-o', 'LineWidth', 1.5);
xlabel('f [pix]');
ylabel('Vsota kvadratov razdalj (napaka)');
title('Iskanje optimalne goriščne razdalje f');
grid on;

[min_error, idx] = min(errors);
fprintf('Najmanjša napaka pri f = %.2f px (SSE = %.3f)\n', f_values(idx), min_error);

%% Naloga 2.4: Nelinearna optimizacija parametra f z uporabo fminsearch

% Slika in točke iz naloge 3
I = imread("Image15.tif");
cx = size(I,2)/2;
cy = size(I,1)/2;

num_points = 13;
figure; imshow(I); axis image; title('Klikni 13 vogalov ob robu slike (od zgoraj navzdol)');
hold on;

x1 = zeros(num_points,1);
y1 = zeros(num_points,1);

% Prva vrstica
for i = 1:num_points
    [x1(i), y1(i)] = ginput(1);
    plot(x1(i), y1(i), 'rs', 'MarkerSize', 6, 'LineWidth', 1.5);
    if i > 1
        plot(x1(i-1:i), y1(i-1:i), 'r-', 'LineWidth', 1.2);
    end
end

% Druga vrstica
title('Klikni 13 vogalov v sredini slike (od zgoraj navzdol)');
x2 = zeros(num_points,1);
y2 = zeros(num_points,1);

for i = 1:num_points
    [x2(i), y2(i)] = ginput(1);
    plot(x2(i), y2(i), 'bs', 'MarkerSize', 6, 'LineWidth', 1.5);
    if i > 1
        plot(x2(i-1:i), y2(i-1:i), 'b-', 'LineWidth', 1.2);
    end
end

hold off

% Napaka kot funkcija f
error_fun = @(f) compute_error(f, x1, y1, x2, y2, cx, cy);

% Začetna vrednost f
f0 = 600;  % začetni približek

% Nelinearna optimizacija
f_opt = fminsearch(error_fun, f0);

fid = fopen('fminsearch.txt', 'w');
fprintf(fid, 'Optimalna vrednost f = %.4f\n', f_opt);
fclose(fid);

fprintf('Optimalna vrednost f = %.4f\n', f_opt);

function E = compute_error(f, x1, y1, x2, y2, cx, cy)
    
r1 = sqrt((x1 - cx).^2 + (y1 - cy).^2);
    r2 = sqrt((x2 - cx).^2 + (y2 - cy).^2);

    r_lin1 = -(f/2) * ((exp(-2*r1/f) - 1) ./ exp(-r1/f));
    r_lin2 = -(f/2) * ((exp(-2*r2/f) - 1) ./ exp(-r2/f));

    theta1 = atan2(y1 - cy, x1 - cx);
    theta2 = atan2(y2 - cy, x2 - cx);

    x1_corr = cx + r_lin1 .* cos(theta1);
    y1_corr = cy + r_lin1 .* sin(theta1);
    x2_corr = cx + r_lin2 .* cos(theta2);
    y2_corr = cy + r_lin2 .* sin(theta2);

    XY1_corr = [x1_corr'; y1_corr'];
    XY2_corr = [x2_corr'; y2_corr'];

    [~, dist1] = fitline(XY1_corr);
    [~, dist2] = fitline(XY2_corr);

    E = sum(dist1.^2) + sum(dist2.^2);
end

plot(tocke([1:4 1],1), tocke([1:4 1],2)); %narišemo te točke(1 znotraj [] je, da je zaprt pravokotnik, na koncu spet prva točka)

W = 12*30; %širina šahovnice je 12 kvadratkov po 30mm

G = fitgeotrans(tocke, pravokotnik, 'projective');   %ocenimo projektivno preslikavo iz tocke v pravokotnik
tform = maketform('projective', G.T); %ustvari tform, ki ga razume imtransform  %T je 3x3 homogena matrika projektivne trans.
%Irect = imtransform(I, tform); %podamo še velikosti x in y, da dobimo sliko zgolj šahovnice, ne pa tudi ozadja
Irect = imtransform(I, tform, 'XData', [0 W], 'YData', [0 H]); %preslika sliko v ciljni pravokotnik

[X,Y] = meshgrid(0:30:W, 0:30:H);

plot(x0(:), y0(:), 'mx');

% fitline pričakuje 2*N matriko, prva vrstica x, druga y
% vrne koeficiente premice ax+by+c=0 z normo sqrt(a^2+b^2)=1, torej C = [a;b;c] 
% vrne tudi d - pravokotna razdalja vsake točke do premice 

% stranska premica
[x1,y1] = ginput(12); %poklikati je potrebno vsa presečišča stranske premice

if abs(b1) > eps %primer, ko premica ni vertikalna 
    x = [0 W];
    y = -(a1*x + c1)/b1;        
    plot(x, y, 'r');
else
    x0 = -c1/a1;  %vertikalna premica x=x0, nagib je neskončen, b=0 torej ax+c=0
    plot(x0, [0 H], 'r');
end


% sredinska premica
[x2,y2] = ginput(12); %poklikati je potrebno vsa presečišča sredinske premice 
plot(x2,y2,'gx');
[C2,d2] = fitline([x2'; y2']);
a2=C2(1); b2=C2(2); c2=C2(3);

if abs(b2) > eps %primer, ko premica ni vertikalna 
    x = [0 W];
    y = -(a2*x + c2)/b2;        
    plot(x, y, 'g');
else
    x0 = -c2/a2;  % vertikalna premica x=x0, nagib je neskončen, b=0 torej ax+c=0
    plot(x0, [0 H], 'g');
end


% minimizacija vsote kvadratov razdalj
load Calib_Results.mat;
cx = cc(1);            %središče v pikslih cx= 303, cy=243
cy = cc(2);              

% popačeni radiji
r0_rob    = hypot(x1 - cx, y1 - cy);   %hypot - hipotenuza 
r0_center = hypot(x2 - cx, y2 - cy);

% nepopačeni radiji
f = 400:20:800;
vsota_kvadratov = zeros(size(f)); 

for i = 1:size(f,2)  %f je vrstični vektor (21)
    ft = f(i);

    rl_rob = -(ft/2) .* (exp(-2 .* r0_rob ./ ft) - 1) ./ exp(-r0_rob ./ ft);  % ., ker množimo in delimo po elementih (r0_rob je lahko element in vektor)
    rl_center = -(ft/2) .* (exp(-2 .* r0_center ./ ft) - 1) ./ exp(-r0_center ./ ft);

    s1 = rl_rob ./ r0_rob; %radialni faktor
    s2 = rl_center ./ r0_center;

    x1u = cx + (x1 - cx).*s1;   y1u = cy + (y1 - cy).*s1; %x in y koor. undistorted robnih točk
    x2u = cx + (x2 - cx).*s2;   y2u = cy + (y2 - cy).*s2; %x in y koor. undistorted centerskih točk
    
    [~,d1] = fitline([x1u'; y1u']); %fitline undistorted točk
    [~,d2] = fitline([x2u'; y2u']);

    vsota_kvadratov(i) = sum(d1.^2) + sum(d2.^2);
    %vsota_kvadratov(i)= sqrt((sum(d1.^2) + sum(d2.^2)) / (numel(d1)+numel(d2)) ); če bi želeli rms 
end

figure; plot(f, vsota_kvadratov);
xlabel('f'); ylabel('vsota kvadratov razdalj');

% Nelinearna optimizacija vrednosti parametra distorzije
f0 = 500;
obj = @(ft) optimizacija(ft, x1, y1, x2, y2, r0_rob, r0_center, cx, cy); %anonimna fun., da dostopamo do vseh elementov
f_opt = fminsearch(obj, f0)

==== BEGIN Tina Gutnik | Tina Gutnik_18914_assignsubmission_file | optimizacija.m ====
function  vsota_kvadratov = optimizacija (ft, x1, y1, x2, y2, r0_rob, r0_center, cx, cy) 
    rl_rob = -(ft/2) .* (exp(-2 .* r0_rob ./ ft) - 1) ./ exp(-r0_rob ./ ft);  % ., ker množimo in delimo po elementih (r0_rob je lahko element in vektor)
    rl_center = -(ft/2) .* (exp(-2 .* r0_center ./ ft) - 1) ./ exp(-r0_center ./ ft);

    s1 = rl_rob ./ r0_rob; %radialni faktor
    s2 = rl_center ./ r0_center;

    x1u = cx + (x1 - cx).*s1;   y1u = cy + (y1 - cy).*s1; %x in y koor. undistorted robnih točk
    x2u = cx + (x2 - cx).*s2;   y2u = cy + (y2 - cy).*s2; %x in y koor. undistorted centerskih točk
    
    [~,d1] = fitline([x1u'; y1u']); %fitline undistorted točk
    [~,d2] = fitline([x2u'; y2u']);

    vsota_kvadratov = sum(d1.^2) + sum(d2.^2);
end
==== END Tina Gutnik | Tina Gutnik_18914_assignsubmission_file | optimizacija.m ====

%% RV2_VSE_NALOGE
close all; clear; clc;

%% ---- nastavitve slik ----
projImgName = 'Image_rect6.tif';   % 2. naloga -> prilagodi po potrebi
radImgName  = 'Image15.tif';   % 3. in 4. naloga

%% ---- središče distorzije iz Calib_Results.mat ----
R = load('Calib_Results.mat');

cx = R.cc(1); cy = R.cc(2);

%% ===================== 2. NALOGA: PROJEKTIVNA =====================
Ip = imread(projImgName);

figure; imshow(Ip,[]); axis image;
title({'2. naloga: klikni 4 vogale šahovnice v zaporedju:', ...
       '1: levo zgoraj, 2: desno zgoraj, 3: desno spodaj, 4: levo spodaj'});
[xv, yv] = ginput(4);
moving = [xv yv];

% prilagodi glede na svojo šahovnico:
numSquaresX = 12;
numSquaresY = 11;
squareSize  = 30;
W = numSquaresX * squareSize;
H = numSquaresY * squareSize;

fixed = [0 0; W 0; W H; 0 H];

T = maketform('projective', moving, fixed);
[J, ~, ~] = imtransform(Ip, T, 'XData',[0 W], 'YData',[0 H]);

figure; imshow(J,[]); axis image;
title('2. naloga: korigirana (projektivna) slika');

% oglišča v idealni mreži + preslikava nazaj na original
[Xg, Yg] = meshgrid(0:squareSize:W, 0:squareSize:H);
ideal_pts = [Xg(:) Yg(:)];
invT = maketform('projective', fixed, moving);
[xg, yg] = tformfwd(invT, ideal_pts(:,1), ideal_pts(:,2));

figure; imshow(Ip,[]); axis image; hold on;
plot(xg, yg, 'r.', 'MarkerSize', 6);
title('2. naloga: oglišča šahovnice na originalni sliki');


%% ========== 3. & 4. NALOGA: RADIALNA DISTORZIJA (Image15) ==========

Ir = imread(radImgName);

figure; imshow(Ir,[]); axis image;
title({'3./4. naloga:', ...
       'Najprej klikni ROBNO vertikalno vrsto oglišč (Enter za konec),', ...
       'nato SREDINSKO vertikalno vrsto (Enter za konec).'});

disp('3./4. naloga: robna vrsta, Enter za konec.');
[x1, y1] = ginput;
disp('3./4. naloga: sredinska vrsta, Enter za konec.');
[x2, y2] = ginput;
close;

%% ---- 3. naloga: diskretno iskanje f v [400:20:800] ----

f_vals = 400:20:800;
E_vals = zeros(size(f_vals));

for k = 1:length(f_vals)
    E_vals(k) = radial_error(f_vals(k), x1,y1,x2,y2, cx,cy);
end

[Emin, idxMin] = min(E_vals);
f_best_grid = f_vals(idxMin);

fprintf('3. naloga: najboljši f (diskretno) = %.4f, napaka = %.4f\n', ...
        f_best_grid, Emin);

figure;
plot(f_vals, E_vals, '-o'); grid on;
xlabel('f'); ylabel('Vsota kvadratov razdalj');
title('3. naloga: napaka glede na f (diskretno)');
    


%% ---- 4. naloga: nelinearno iskanje f z fminsearch ----

f0 = f_best_grid;                 % začnemo pri diskretnem minimumu
opts = optimset('Display','off'); % minimalno, brez spama v konzoli

[f_best_cont, E_best_cont] = fminsearch( ...
    @(f) radial_error(f, x1,y1,x2,y2, cx,cy), f0, opts);

fprintf('4. naloga: optimalni f (fminsearch) = %.6f, napaka = %.6f\n', ...
        f_best_cont, E_best_cont);

% (neobvezno, a pregledno) lokalni prikaz okoli optimuma
f_loc = linspace(max(100, f_best_cont-200), min(1000, f_best_cont+200), 30);
E_loc = arrayfun(@(f) radial_error(f, x1,y1,x2,y2, cx,cy), f_loc);

figure;
plot(f_loc, E_loc, '-o'); grid on;
xlabel('f'); ylabel('Vsota kvadratov razdalj');
title('4. naloga: napaka okoli optimalnega f');


%% ===== LOKALNE FUNKCIJE (OSTANEJO V ISTEM .m, TO JE ŠE VEDNO SKRIPTA) =====

function E = radial_error(f, x1,y1,x2,y2, cx,cy)
    % Napaka za dano f: vsota kvadratov razdalj obeh stolpcev do svojih premic
    if f <= 0
        E = 1e12;
        return;
    end

    [xu1, yu1] = undistort_points(x1, y1, cx, cy, f);
    [xu2, yu2] = undistort_points(x2, y2, cx, cy, f);

    [~, d1] = fitline([xu1.'; yu1.']);   % 2xN
    [~, d2] = fitline([xu2.'; yu2.']);

    E = sum(d1.^2) + sum(d2.^2);
end


function [xu, yu] = undistort_points(x, y, cx, cy, f)
    % Inverz radialne distorzije:
    % r' -> r_l = f * sinh(r'/f)

    dx = x - cx;
    dy = y - cy;

    rp = sqrt(dx.^2 + dy.^2);       % popačen radij
    rl = f .* sinh(rp ./ f);        % nepopačen radij

    scale = ones(size(rp));
    mask = (rp > 0);
    scale(mask) = rl(mask) ./ rp(mask);

    xu = cx + dx .* scale;
    yu = cy + dy .* scale;
end
